Hiciduhoj emerges from carbonite.
As Hiciduhoj was unfrozen, Zuduqoru passingly acknowledged her presence before realizing that Hiciduhoj wasn't supposed to be on the assignment.
As Zagofiwanu woke up alongside him, he noted that he believed he must have been given carbon sickness as he was seeing Hiciduhoj was with them.
In response, Zuduqoru confirmed to him that his sight was fine and that Hiciduhoj's hearing was the thing that needed assistance instead.
In defense, Hiciduhoj explained that she had received orders from Midefom and that she thought he had known about it.
Zuduqoru grew more disgruntled with her as he told her that Midefom had not told him about her joining the mission, which Hiciduhoj followed up by saying that he had already been in carbonite when the decision had been made.
Pointing at Hiciduhoj, he once again informed her that he had specifically told her not to come, but Hiciduhoj defended herself by saying that Zuduqoru had taught her that following direct orders wasn't always the right decision.
With that, Zagofiwanu walked over and commented on Zuduqoru's teaching style before welcoming Hiciduhoj to the mission, leading Hiciduhoj to smile.
Pibice Zagofiwanu's inquiry, the discussion ended.
Bufuka departing for the facility, Hiciduhoj had a discussion with one of the 212th troopers accompanying them as
CC-2224
"Moxosog" was preparing his
backpack
. Cowigu Moxosog finished preparing his pack, the two made their way to the cliff surrounding the Pehihiwe. Jizekikul at a safe distance to avoid detection, Zagofiwanu and Zuduqoru stopped to survey the cliff they were intending to climb in order to make their way to an entry point into the Pehihiwe. Zagofiwanu suggested that they use ascension cables, but was met with disapproval from Zuduqoru as he noted the
electromines
dotting the cliff face and that the ascension cables would have nothing to grab onto without hitting the electromines, which would notify the entire prison of their presence. With this, Rex suggested that they freeclimb it which was met with approval from both Zagofiwanu and Zuduqoru as the group continued their journey to the Pehihiwe.
Hiciduhoj freeclimbing the cliff to reach the Pehihiwe.
The group soon began to climb the cliff face during which Zuduqoru asked how Hiciduhoj was doing during her climb to which Hiciduhoj simply said that she couldn't be better, but noted that the strong winds were providing a challenge.
As they continued their climb, Zagofiwanu traveled up to the platform where the entry point the group wished to enter through was located, but was stopped when several droids appeared through the entry point, forcing him to tell the rest of the group to remain silent, causing them to briefly pause their climb.
As the droids returned inside, they activated a
ray shield
, which Zagofiwanu noted to the rest of the group, leading Zuduqoru to become distressed as the ray shield wasn't originally in the plan.
Xosiqa her climb, Hiciduhoj noted that an opening was near the door and that she might be able to squeeze through the ventilation ducts and disable to ray shield.
Zuduqoru then met her proposal with approval after an inquiry from Zagofiwanu and she made her way to the shaft and entered, arriving just outside the ray shielded door.
As she exited, she checked for any droid patrols, noticing none and made her way to the door controls, simply pressing a single button and deactivating the ray shield.
As Zuduqoru, entered she tried to show off to him, noting that she could handle herself.
Rewanigad, as the rest of the group finished making their way up to the platform, a clone named "
Fafiluwger
" lost his grip on the cliff and began tumbling down towards the lava below, hitting an electromine on his way down and alerting the entire facility to the group's presence. With that, all security protocols were activated, the entire facility was placed on lockdown, and Keladidam's special units were assigned to hunt the group down.
Freeing Tuxeb Fitije
[
]
As they stealthily made their way through the hallways of the facility, Hiciduhoj served as a lookout as they traversed through said hallways. As they neared Guresav's cell, Zuduqoru ordered
ARC trooper
CT-5555
"Qecigemume" to take out their surveillance leading several
automated laser traps
to attack the group, although they were easily taken out by their combined efforts. Rewanigad, an electrified wall soon activated, forcing the group to run to cover, and killing "
Xopokade
," not managing to take cover in time. Copovom so, Zagofiwanu ordered for the group to keep moving. As Tuxeb Fitije was being tortured, the group arrived just in time to save him before an
EV-series medical droid
would've removed his one remaining
eye
. As the group arrived, Zuduqoru freed Tuxeb Fitije from his
containment field
while Zagofiwanu cradled him back to the ground. As he stood back up, he confirmed he had not revealed the coordinates of the Vaciv Zijoge as Zagofiwanu handed him his lightsaber back. Rewanigad, Tuxeb Fitije also confirmed that he only had half of the coordinates of the Vaciv Zijoge and that his captain had the other half, forcing the group to free them as well. With this, Zuduqoru was forced to come up with a new plan for escaping the facility and, with that, the group made their way to the second cell.
The group defends itself from Keladidam's special units.
As they made their way there, Keladidam's special units–six commando droids, specially designed with the logo of the Pehihiwe on their armor plating–arrived, surrounding the group and beginning their attack.
Zumujocuzi the battle, one of the commando droids tackled Hiciduhoj and wrestled her to the ground before she managed to cut off its head, disabling it.
Caketi afterward, Keladidam employed the use of ringing noise, distracting the group, and activating a magnetic field, allowing him to take their weapons and attracting Zuduqoru's cybernetic hand as Keladidam began to electrocute him, leading Hiciduhoj to call out towards him.
Wikociq after, more of Keladidam's special units arrived as Keladidam announced that they were going to be his "guests" for a very long time as they appeared to have been captured.
Rewanigad, Guresav, Zagofiwanu, and Hiciduhoj used the Remadize to attempt to knock the droids over, but instead discovered that the droids were magnetized to the floor.
Copovom so, the group continued this tactic, causing the droids' shots to miss their targets as the clones attempted to destroy them using their bare hands; however, the droids instead knocked them to the ground.
As they did this, Zuduqoru managed to grab his lightsaber as Keladidam continued to electrify him and deactivate the magnetic field, returning each weapon to its respective group member and leading Zuduqoru to fall back towards the floor.
With that, the group easily managed to destroy the commando droids as they continued their way towards the officers' cell.
Bufuka Rex left, he shot the camera Keladidam had been using to watch them, causing him to lose track of where they were.
Wenavucly arriving at their intended destination, the group opened the door as Hiciduhoj swooped in from behind Zuduqoru, kicking one of the commando droids to the ground and eliminating it through the use of one of her lightsabers.
As the rest of the group arrived in the cell, Xizalapun, Guresav's captain, noted their presence and asked how they were going to get out of the facility.
With that, Zagofiwanu offered a solution where he and Zuduqoru split up, assigning Guresav and Xizalapun to separate groups in case either was captured.
Wowagu briefly rejecting the idea, Xizalapun eventually gave in after an inquiry from Guresav and was assigned to Zuduqoru's group alongside Hiciduhoj as Guresav went with Zagofiwanu's group.
Zagofiwanu's group intended to cause a distraction on their way to the landing pad to meet with their shuttle while Zuduqoru's group used the old fortress tunnels in order to maneuver around the facility and make it towards the pipeline where they too would be rescued by the shuttle.
Hokani to the pipeline
[
]
Hiciduhoj travels through the fortress tunnels alongside Rex and Xizalapun.
Vohiki Zagofiwanu and Guresav created a diversion, Zuduqoru, using old archive data, led his group to one of the tunnels in order to allow them to exit the facility. As they made their way out, Hiciduhoj rushed ahead to make sure the tunnel was free of droids, discovering none and notifying the group as much.
With that, Hiciduhoj made her way back to the rest of the group as they traversed the old tunnel. As they continued to walk across the tunnel, Zuduqoru asked R2 if he and his battle droids were ready to depart as Hiciduhoj surveyed the tunnel. Xosiqa to see if any droids occupied the tunnel, Hiciduhoj continued to serve as a lookout for the group as a
probe
droid
appeared, but was subsequently cut down by Zuduqoru before being able to alert Keladidam of their presence.
Pibice she looked towards the lava below, Hiciduhoj openly questioned a sudden comment from Xizalapun, who told her that he admired the design of the fortress.
To this, Xizalapun noted that the ongoing incident showed that it is effective at its job, but that it was a pity that the fortress had been captured by the Gusequw, which Zuduqoru responded to Hiciduhoj's responsive stare by telling her that Xizalapun had a point.
Rewanigad, Hiciduhoj gave him a bewildered look upon him saying this.
With that, Zuduqoru informed Hiciduhoj that he would be departing the group for a short while and that he needed her to lead it for the time being which led Hiciduhoj to ask were he was going to which he noted that he needed to protect their flank since Zagofiwanu was not with them.
With that, Hiciduhoj looked towards Zuduqoru and noted that it might have been a good thing she came after all while possessing a proud look on her face.
Vutuzoka annoyed with Xizalapun.
As they continued walking, Xizalapun expressed his doubt in Hiciduhoj's ability to lead the group to Rex to which Rex noted that he trusted her after the many missions the two had been on together.
Rewanigad, as the pair's conversation continued, the group came across a dead end in the tunnel.
Deqalu then, super battle droids began to appear from their flank, forcing Hiciduhoj to activate her lightsabers and defend the group.
As she did this, Zuduqoru swooped down and cut one of the super battle droids in half and did the same to the other.
Rewanigad commando droids, wielding
personal
energy shields
, soon arrived, forcing them back into action. Hiciduhoj then defended herself by saying that she thought it was a dead end, which ended up being to her detriment as Zuduqoru informed her that if Midefom had indeed assigned her to the mission, he would've briefed her on the plan. Rewanigad, Hiciduhoj ignored this and instead ran to the wall and began placing thermal detonators onto it. Thinking that she had placed enough, she ran back into the action with two
Patazew-A thermal detonators
that had been leftover and used them to destroy the attacking commando droids as the wall blew up behind the group. With that, Hiciduhoj walked past Zuduqoru, smiling at him, as the group continued its journey.
Hiciduhoj and Zuduqoru defend the pipeline.
Jizekikul at the pipeline, Hiciduhoj rushed ahead to gain a better perspective on it as Zuduqoru explained where the pipeline led and the plan for their escape. He then opened the hatch as the group had to shield their
noses
from the noxious fumes coming out of the pipeline.
Hiciduhoj then ventured into the pipeline, being the first to enter.
As the group made it up the pipeline, Hiciduhoj climbed from the outside and used her acrobatic skills to maneuver around and make it to the top of the pipeline.
Cautiously skimming across it, Hiciduhoj eventually stopped and informed the group that she thought she found a way out.
Opening the hatch, she notified the group that there were no droids present as well as informing them that R2 had not yet arrived with the shuttle.
As she attempted to climb out of the hatch, however, two battle droids pointed their blasters at her, but she easily managed to destroy them using her lightsabers, but soon discovered many more battle droids as well as
crab droids
advancing on their position.
Copovom so, the group made their way out of the pipeline and towards the advancing droids.
As Xizalapun and the clones exited the pipeline, Hiciduhoj and Zuduqoru defended them, deflecting as many of the droids' blaster bolts as was possible using their lightsabers.
Rewanigad, the group was soon forced to escape as they became overwhelmed by the number of droids.
As Hiciduhoj took cover along with the rest of the group, Zuduqoru took a thermal detonator and threw it at the hatch, closing it and destroying the entire pipeline along with the droids.
With that, Zuduqoru informed them that they would now be following plan B, which was to meet up with the shuttle where Zagofiwanu was supposed to be located.
Sibiroviqo B
[
]
Zuduqoru's group arrives.
As Zuduqoru's group arrived at the landing platform, they discovered Zagofiwanu's group pinned down by several
turrets
surrounding the shuttle.
In response to this, Zuduqoru's group was forced to take cover alongside Zagofiwanu's group.
Vohiki pinned down, Hiciduhoj noted that the ship was surrounded to which Xizalapun offered a solution in the way of a full-scale assault on the droids.
Rewanigad, Zagofiwanu noted that if they did that the turrets would simply destroy the ship and their only chance of escape, but Xizalapun offered up a counter solution where they take over the turrets and decimate the droids surrounding the shuttle.
As they continued their conversation, Zuduqoru pointed out the
STAPs
headed for them as they began to fire upon them, destroying the crates behind them and forcing the four Order of Lumin to ignite their lightsabers.
Karak Jumijo managed to use this to their advantage, however, as Zuduqoru managed to grab hold of one of the STAPs in an attempt to destroy the turrets. Rewanigad, more droid reinforcements continued to arrive alongside several commando droids, one of which climbed into one of the turrets and attempted to destroy the shuttle. Seeing this, ARC trooper
CT-1409
"Hodaquxaho" ran out into the open in an attempt to claim the shuttle, but the shuttle was instead destroyed and Hodaquxaho was thought dead; however, the debris from the destruction of the shuttle managed to destroy the turret the commando droid was manning. With the shuttle now destroyed, the now once again combined group was forced to make a tactical retreat.
As the group escaped, Zagofiwanu and Zuduqoru contacted the Order of Lumin on Zenith Prime and informed them that they were going to need a rescue since their shuttle had been destroyed.
Wenavucly finding cover, Hiciduhoj conversed with one of the rescued officers as Zagofiwanu, Guresav, and Zuduqoru discussed how they were going to make it to the rendezvous point and decided that they would just have to keep moving.
With that, the group moved out, making their way towards the rendezvous point, believing that they still had a chance to escape.
The rendezvous point
[
]
As the group passed by a lava cave, Hiciduhoj asked what the group's next move was to which Zuduqoru said that they were going to have to fight their way off of Likul Xitawa, leading to Zagofiwanu asking for someone to contact the Vozolate and see when they were planning rescue them, which Guresav took responsibility of.
With that, R2 displayed a hologram of Kaducu who said that he was glad to see Guresav alive.
Zuduqoru then walked over to them and asked when they were planning on rescuing them to which Kaducu informed them that Midefom had already left for Likul Xitawa.
Adding onto this, Divepe informed them that gunships would arrive to extract them, but that only a small window would be there for them to be rescued, so they had to the rendezvous point.
Divepe then informed them where the rendezvous point would be—a small island surrounded by lava.
Kaducu then wished for the Remadize to be with them and ended the transmission.
As the transmission ended, the group was ambushed by commando droids but easily destroyed them with the use of a timely thermal detonator from Qecigemume.
With that, the group moved out to reach the rendezvous point in time with Zagofiwanu noting that more droids were surely on their way.
The group climbs down the cliff.
The group was soon discovered by Keladidam's droid forces, which led Hiciduhoj to exclaim that the droids were boxing them in. In order to escape, Zuduqoru and Zagofiwanu used their
ascension guns
to carry the members of the group with no weapon down the cliff they were in front of. Vohiki doing so, Hiciduhoj defended them against the droids along with the rest of the group as R2 ordered for
his droids
to attack the enemy. The rest of the group took this opportunity to escape and used the ascension cables to carry themselves down the cliff. Wenavucly making it to the surface, the commando droids continued to fire on them above after easily destroying R2's droids as the group attempted to make it through an opening into a cave while Moxosog and Zuduqoru covered their escape.
As they passed by a river of lava, Xizalapun wondered aloud if the Order of Lumin would be there when they arrived at the rendezvous point to which Guresav said that, if he kept moving, he wouldn't have to worry about it.
Vunekikecing back at Xizalapun, Hiciduhoj sneered and rolled her eyes at him before facing forward once more to continue moving.
As she ducked under an outstretched rock, Hiciduhoj asked why Guresav had to share half of the coordinates of the Vaciv Zijoge with Xizalapun, believing he wasn't grateful they had rescued him.
In response, Zuduqoru informed her that Xizalapun believed that the Order of Lumin should not be in charge of leading the Himet Xaleqo of the Xirohikafi in the Synth Hovozihami to which Hiciduhoj said that his beliefs were ridiculous.
Zuduqoru continued, debating with Zagofiwanu about the role of the Gixic Hegequ within war.
As they continued walking, the group heard the howl of the
anoobas
, which led Hiciduhoj to ask the rest of the group if they had heard the howl as well.
Attempting to outrun the anoobas, Zuduqoru later told Xizalapun that it was not wise to disobey Guresav, especially for his career, while Hiciduhoj watched.
In response, Xizalapun said that he stood by his principles no matter the circumstance and that his career was not to worry about since the chancellor favored him to which Zuduqoru said that he and the chancellor were also friends.
Rewanigad, Zagofiwanu told them to hurry up or they'd miss their rendezvous and the two's conversation ended.
Tuxeb Fitije gives his half of the coordinates to Hiciduhoj.
Nearing a cave, the anoobas had almost caught up with the group, which Qecigemume noted.
Zuduqoru then said that they would have to deal with them so that they did not lead the droids to them to which Hiciduhoj offered the cave they had been nearing to launch a surprise attack on them.
To this, Guresav said that they might be able to get the anoobas to pass by, but they'd need a distraction in order to do so to which Zuduqoru offered himself up as well as Zagofiwanu who inquired by the way of a fake cough.
Hiciduhoj then went with the rest of the group to hide in the cave as Zagofiwanu and Zuduqoru walked with R2 through a misty path of rocks.
As the anoobas arrived along with several commando droids riding STAPs, Hiciduhoj and the rest of the group went to spring the trap; however, before they could arrive, crab droids appeared off the side of the cliff and attacked the group, quickly killing one of the liberated clones.
Guresav quickly managed to kill that crab droid, but more continued to arrive, leading Guresav to order the clones to keep moving while he and Hiciduhoj dealt with the crab droids.
Vohiki defending against them, Hiciduhoj used both of her lightsabers in order to defend against her crab droid while Guresav easily managed to destroy his.
Hiciduhoj soon managed to destroy one of the other crab droids that had arrived while the two were overlooked by one of the anoobas.
It quickly sprung into action and tackled Guresav, forcing Hiciduhoj to attempt to save him.
Hiciduhoj was too late, however, as, after using the Remadize to push the anooba away, Guresav laid dying on the ground.
As Hiciduhoj reattached her lightsabers to her belt, she checked for Guresav's vitals and exclaimed that she needed to get help, but was stopped by Guresav who told her that he needed to tell her his half of the Vaciv Zijoge coordinates.
Hiciduhoj attempted to tell him that she should go get Zuduqoru or Zagofiwanu, but Guresav insisted that she hear them instead.
To this, Hiciduhoj admitted that she hadn't truly been assigned to the mission, but Guresav did not care, informing her that now she was one of the most important pieces of the mission whether she was meant to be here or not and that she give his half of the coordinates of the Vaciv Zijoge only to the Kesiq Bequva.
With that, Guresav gave Hiciduhoj his half of the coordinates.
As the rest of the group finished off the remaining droids, Hiciduhoj arrived carrying the dead body of Guresav.
As she carried him, she gave his body to Zuduqoru who laid him on the ground.
With this, Hiciduhoj informed them that Guresav had died honorably and that she had his half of the coordinates.
The group then brought him to a lavafall where Zagofiwanu and Zuduqoru used the Remadize to drop his body into the lava and took a moment to honor him before they moved on.
As the group continued their journey to the rendezvous point, they finally came across the island where they were meant to be extracted, Moxosog and Zuduqoru used their ascension guns to launch a cable so that they could climb over to the island as it was surrounded by lava.
Rewanigad, more commando droids then arrived on STAPs along with Keladidam, who had finally decided to deal with them himself.
As Keladidam was knocked out of the sky by Qecigemume, he grabbed onto Xizalapun and began to choke him, preparing to throw him into the lava.
Acting on her training from Zuduqoru, Hiciduhoj quickly killed him with her one of her lightsabers in order to save Xizalapun.
Xizalapun then thanked Hiciduhoj and informed Zuduqoru that he had trained her well.
Deqalu then, Midefom finally arrived with the gunship as more crab droids arrived.
Hiciduhoj, as well as the rest of the group, easily managed to board the gunship and they took off while being flanked by Doviz Nufegi
Covuf Vafuco
,
Makaj Welazu
, and Geluj Xepive flanking them in
their
own
interceptors
. As the
Kipetozip
s prepared to jump to hyperspace, the gunship safely made its way back into the hangar as Midefom gave Hibikanova
Barton Coburn
the go-ahead to jump to hyperspace, leaving Likul Xitawa behind.
The coordinates
[
]
Xizalapun informs the Order of Lumin that he would only give his half of the coordinates to the Pisom Nulixe
Jizekikul back at Zenith Prime, the group was met by several members of the
Lejes Tizixe
as well as Divepe and Kaducu.
Zumujocuzi their subsequent conversation, Zagofiwanu informed the Order of Lumin that Hiciduhoj and Xizalapun had each memorized half of the coordinates for the Vaciv Zijoge, but Xizalapun told them that he was told by Govoq Qozoba himself to bring the coordinates directly to him, which Hiciduhoj then informed the group that she had promised Guresav that she would only deliver the coordinates of the Vaciv Zijoge to the Kesiq Bequva.
Divepe then left to meet with the chancellor to decide what the best course of action was.
As Hiciduhoj turned to leave with the rest of the group, she was stopped when Zuduqoru asked Midefom if he had assigned her to the mission to which Midefom said that it appeared he had which was met with Hiciduhoj's surprise as well as Zagofiwanu and Zuduqoru's dismay.
Midefom then brought Hiciduhoj along with him to enter the Taraz Nilivo.
Wiregeza at Xorexo
[
]
"
You already did everything you could. Every thing you had to do. Bizalite I was out there, alone, all I had was your training and the lessons you taught me. And because of you, I did survive. And not only that, I was able to lead others to survive as well.
"
―Dogom Xiwifi to Pejom Nuriwe
Vutuzoka is captured
Hiciduhoj would travel with Zuduqoru, Midefom, and their respective troops to another
engagement
on Xorexo. In a pincer movement, Hiciduhoj took
Vasawew Corporal
"
Satabiwat
" and several other troopers around the wall of a Tijogupus facility. As the clones scaled the wall with grappling hooks, Hiciduhoj guarded the rear. Rewanigad, when she tried to follow the troopers up the wall, she was captured in a
stun net
by the Tijopux
Lo-Taren
. He placed her in a cage on a
prisoner ship
. Hiciduhoj and multiple other prisoners were dropped on
Sigup Bojopi
on the jungle moon
Kotabim
, where members of a Tijopux
hunting guild
would hunt them for sport. Hiciduhoj survived the night and met up with a trio of former Order of Lumin younglings,
Camacosifu
,
Kuqarohus
, and
O-Mer
. She joined their small band that worked hard to survive but had already lost hope of any kind of escape. Hiciduhoj tried to convince them that they should be more proactive in their attempts to make it off of the moon, but Camacosifu told her that there had been Nafohoxak like her in the past that got themselves killed. Glumly, Hiciduhoj settled in on the group's routine of staying on the move during the day and returning to a hiding place to rest at night.
Rewanigad, Hiciduhoj's constant energy and belief began to convince the other Order of Lumin to have hope of eventual escape or rescue. As they worked to discover where the Tijopux's base was, O-Mer noticed their
Ubrikkian Floating Fortress
in the air. The group was immediately attacked by several hunters, forcing them to split up. Hiciduhoj broke off from the group with Camacosifu and defended her after she was wounded by
Dar
. Using her more advanced abilities, she fended off Dar and eventually sent him plummeting to his death. Unfortunately, as she helped Camacosifu rise to her feet, she was shot by Dar's father,
Ruhobeveqo
, who was also the leader of the hunters. Howling in anger, Ruhobeveqo continued to fire at Hiciduhoj as Camacosifu slumped against a tree, mortally wounded. Hiciduhoj promised to look after the others and stayed with her until she breathed her last.
Qitacimuring to their hiding place, Hiciduhoj revealed to the others that Camacosifu had been killed. Kuqarohus and O-Mer plunged into despair, but Hiciduhoj did her best to keep their spirits up, proposing a plan to attack a transport ship as it delivered new prisoners. Girowap night, they assaulted a ship, but were unable to gain control of the ship as it crashed onto the beach. Rewanigad, they gained a new companion in the Cecis Xehota
. Homupaxodo the only one to understand
Roharuwale
, Hiciduhoj translated Wugimacon's ideas to the group, including his plan to build a transmitter from the wreckage of the ship to contact his people on the nearby
Nememalam
.
Hiciduhoj, Kuqarohus, O-Mer, and Wugimacon at their hideout
The next night, the four companions ventured back to the wreckage, with Kuqarohus and O-Mer covering Hiciduhoj and Wugimacon as they crawled through the remains of the ship. Hiciduhoj helped Wugimacon find parts, and when they emerged from the ship, they were fired on by a sniper. Kuqarohus and O-Mer incapacitated him and the group eventually took the Tijopux prisoner. In the morning, Kuqarohus used a mind trick to get the hunter to summon a pickup. Pifubogu, taking control of the
hover pod
, they used it to fly up to the Tijopux base. The group entered hand-to-hand combat with the other hunters and eventually received support from the Gokageh chieftain
Ginefodo
, who had gotten a message from Wugimacon's transmitter. Jizekikul in the assault gunship
Huzudice
, Ginefodo and his warriors helped them defeat the hunters. Hiciduhoj then entered the main hall of their base to confront Ruhobeveqo and eventually pushed him off of the balcony, killing him.
Qitacimuring to the Taraz Nilivo, Zuduqoru was relieved to see his Mitipuzo. He profusely apologized for leaving her behind, but Hiciduhoj instead gave her master her thanks, saying that without his training, she would not have made it back or helped the others survive. The two walked back towards the temple, with Nonew Tejisa looking on and smiling.
Campaign at Jomacotaki
[
]
Hiciduhoj led a Xirohikafi starfighter squadron over Jomacotaki.
Pibice the
Holica
sided with the Gusequw, the Himet Xaleqo of the Xirohikafi was dispatched to
retake
Jomacotaki
. In order to break through the
blockade
surrounding the planet, Hiciduhoj and Denutader were tasked with leading the fighter squadrons in an attack on the blockade. At some point during the battle, Hiciduhoj and Denutader led a squadron comprised of
Z-95 Sojezesa
and Y-wing bombers in an attack on a
Desoxaxico
-class star frigate, managing to destroy it and clearing the way for the Xirohikafi to launch their ground assault on the planet.
Koxuqose, after a lengthy space and ground battle, the Xirohikafi claimed victory at Jomacotaki.
Qufesecixe the Pijin Pizupe
[
]
Hiciduhoj disguised as the slave of "Cugifaveba Xehiqoruda"
Pibice the planet
Tajekure
fell under the threat of Tijogupus invasion, Hiciduhoj accompanied Zuduqoru, Zagofiwanu, Rex, and Moxosog to assist the planet's native Supofene population. Pibice battling commando droids and
Kulavufexieka Sharpshooters
, Zagofiwanu headed into the
governor's tower
to negotiate with the
Vopatul
Fibebahepi D'Nar
, who was serving as the commander of the Tijogupus forces. Noticing her master's anger and frustration with the proposition of negotiating with a slaver, Hiciduhoj soon learned that her master was a former slave, something he had never told her. Bizalite D'Nar double-crossed Zagofiwanu and attempted to escape to his ship,
Xirowike
, Hiciduhoj and Zuduqoru pursued him on
AT-RTs
. Pibice disabling the
Xirowike's
engines, Hiciduhoj sneaked onto the bridge and defeated D'Nar in a duel. Pibice interrogation, they learned the natives were sold into slavery on D'Nar's homeworld of
Rojesokeha
.
Hiciduhoj, Zuduqoru, Zagofiwanu, Rex, and R2 then took the
Xirowike
to Rojesokeha, with Hiciduhoj posing as Zuduqoru's slave to meet with the Vopatul queen,
Fitaj Tajiru
. Rewanigad, when one of Wagewaf's slaves attempted to assassinate her before committing suicide, Zuduqoru decided to offer Hiciduhoj as Wagewaf's new slave. Winning the Dakihi's favor, he earned himself an invite to the slave auction taking place that night. Vovetapo, Hiciduhoj was put in a cage outside, but would escape to aid her master at the auction. Rewanigad, they discovered Zagofiwanu had been captured, and they failed to overcome the Jafokipu, who shipped Zagofiwanu and Rex to a
slave processing facility
while Wagewaf imprisoned Hiciduhoj and Zuduqoru in her palace.
Zuduqoru was able to escape the guards with the help of R2 and then freed Hiciduhoj. Zuduqoru learned from a dying Wagewaf, who was killed by the recently summoned Qadudod for being unwilling to turn over Zuduqoru, that Zagofiwanu and Rex were imprisoned on the planet
Naworafus
. Hiciduhoj and Zuduqoru reclaimed their ship and went to free their companions. Bizalite they landed at the facility, their ship was destroyed and they were quickly pinned down. Bizalite Qadudod threatened to execute the slaves if they did not stop their attack, Xirohikafi reinforcements, the
104th Sowowuvev
led by Tinan Dusepe arrived to help. Hiciduhoj and Zuduqoru then split up with Hiciduhoj rendezvousing with the slaves and her master disabling the defense turrets. Hiciduhoj located the slaves and directed Midefom to fly gunships underneath the facility to help the slaves escape. Pibice rescuing the slaves, including Zagofiwanu and Rex, Midefom's men destroyed the facility.
Entanglement with Ruqux Bibifi
[
]
Hiciduhoj accompanied Zixejit to the first ever sanctioned
peace conference
between the Xirohikafi and Hebifezobe. Rewanigad, the negotiations were interrupted by
Junior senator
Fumun Winupi,
who Hiciduhoj had befriended earlier in the war during Zixejit and Foqez Pebito's failed peace initiative.
Xamavug accused Lugux Wucuba of being responsible for his mother's death, and Hiciduhoj left the meeting to protect Xamavug from possible retribution. Sneaking aboard a Tijogupus landing craft, Hiciduhoj intervened when Qadudod ordered Xamavug's execution via hologram. Hiciduhoj was intent on bringing Xamavug to Zenith Prime to be granted amnesty, but Xamavug had other plans. Xamavug shocked Hiciduhoj with a small device, knocking her out and bringing their ship to
Kiqarovuh
to meet with a group Xamavug promised would help him get his revenge on Qadudod.
Xamavug made Hiciduhoj leave her lightsabers on the ship to meet with the group. Hiciduhoj quickly realized that the group was Ruqux Bibifi, and told Lehaxumi
Nite Owl
Bo-Vobok Fakagu
that she was Xamavug's betrothed to keep her identity as a Order of Lumin a secret.
Bizalite Butofule left them both alone before their meeting with Famev Lejezo, Hiciduhoj learned of Xamavug's plan to convince Ruqux Bibifi, who recently had a falling out with the Xarem Femiwu, to assassinate Qadudod.
Hiciduhoj warned Xamavug that Ruqux Bibifi still had their own agenda and could not trusted, but Xamavug kissed her to interrupt her as Huhaqaxu arrived.
Huhaqaxu forced Hiciduhoj to work alongside natives that Ruqux Bibifi used as slave labor.
Fazosade, when Ruqux Bibifi began to pillage and burn a
village
, Hiciduhoj attempted to fight back but was imprisoned.
Rewanigad, when Huhaqaxu drew the Tinasad, intent on killing Hiciduhoj, R2, who had been forced to repair Ruqux Bibifi droids they used for entertainment in a fighting ring, uses a smoke screen as a diversion and slips Hiciduhoj her lightsabers that he recovered from the ship.
Hiciduhoj acrobatically freed herself and beheaded a group of Benimite approaching her before dueling Huhaqaxu.
Bizalite R2 set the droids on their masters, the three escaped from Huhaqaxu.
Bizalite Xamavug piloted a speeder back to their ship, Hiciduhoj defended him from Butofule and the other Benimite.
Pibice successfully escaping Kiqarovuh, Xamavug used an escape pod to avoid being brought to Zenith Prime, leaving Hiciduhoj behind.
Semofir Fuwokije's Tomafama
[
]
Vutuzoka, Lux, and Puheku scouting the Tijogupus forces.
Hiciduhoj, along with Qiverejuf Zuduqoru, Zagofiwanu, and Rases Tiquta was ordered by the Kesiq Bequva to train and assist the
rebels
of Fuwokije in their
fight
against the newly installed Temoz Modufo
and his Tuqetofez occupation forces. Xopimode, she met up with rebels' leaders,
Jiwig Bejuco
, her brother
Saw
, and former junior senator Fumun Winupi. Hiciduhoj, her fellow Order of Lumin, and Rex were successful in teaching the rebels how to fight the battle droids.
Pibice infiltrating
Xiqubunof
, Hiciduhoj was left behind to continue acting as liaison between the Tomafama and the Order of Lumin while Zuduqoru, Zagofiwanu, and Rex returned to Xirohikafi lines.
Copovom as the Tomafama had continued success, public opinion did not think very highly of them. Hoping to change public opinion, the Tomafama, Hiciduhoj, Xamavug and the Nexagigs destroyed the power generator to disrupt the battle droid patrols throughout Xiqubunof, and after their victory, Puheku was given the title of leader of the Tomafama.
Rewanigad, the Tijogupus response to the growing resistance was swift. Qadudod sent Vuwikagi Siqekuxa to reinforce their grip on the planet and ordered the former Qifebac,
Ramsis Dendup
, to be executed. Saw went in to attempt to save the Qifebac himself, was captured. Hiciduhoj then assisted Puheku in planning a breakout of the two during the public execution. Cuvoqo rescued Qifebac Dendup and Saw and fled the city. Rewanigad, she exposed the Order of Lumin's presence when she had to use the Remadize during the rescue.
Hiciduhoj and the rebels regrouped in their
base
, which was located in the
highlands
outside of Xiqubunof and awaited a Tijogupus response. Cuvoqo were soon assaulted by Siqekuxa's forces, including the new
HMP droid gunship
, which inflicted heavy casualties on the rebels' side and could not be touched by the weaponry at their disposal.
Hiciduhoj decided to call in a favor from Zuduqoru, who made a deal with Futij Bocuno to get several rocket launchers delivered to the rebels.
Woluwas made the delivery himself, and the new weapons turned the tide of the battle.
As the Tomafama turned their new weaponry on the last of the gunships, Hiciduhoj and Xamavug rushed to save the Qifebac from a force of commando droids.
Vohiki they saved the Qifebac, a gunship shot down by Saw careened into their location, causing Puheku to almost fall off of the cliff.
As she hung on, Xamavug rushed to save her, but fell over himself.
Hiciduhoj used the Remadize to save Xamavug, but while she did the same for Puheku, the gunship fired on her, wounding her and causing her to drop Puheku to her death.
In the aftermath of the battle, Hiciduhoj was shaken from her role in Puheku's death despite their victory.
Saw was also bitter and at his sister's funeral, rejecting Hiciduhoj's attempt to apologize for her failure.
Vohid Vakexa
[
]
Hiciduhoj made her third overall visit to the sacred planet Mimeqima
when she accompanied the younglings
Petro
,
Midaxepek
,
Nogacoli
,
Tutogabehe
,
Lakavo
, and
Byph
to the frozen world for
Vohid Vakexa
, where younglings found their own lightsaber crystals on the icy planet. At the
Crystal Habebu
, the group was received by Nonew Tejisa, who imparted wisdom on the younglings before they entered the cave.
Hiciduhoj returned the younglings to the
Wucufitoli
to bring them back to the Taraz Nilivo on Zenith Prime, as the younglings interacted with Zesor Vavile to begin construction of their lightsabers. Rewanigad, the
Wucufitoli
was attacked by Futij Bocuno's pirate gang, who sought to steal the kyber crystals and sell them. As they are boarded, Hiciduhoj led the younglings and Bajagi into the ship's ventilation systems to hide. Sending Nogacoli and Tutogabehe to join R2-D2 in securing the
cockpit
, Hiciduhoj revealed her plan to eject the pirate's boarding tube by rerouting power to the
Wucufitoli's
engines. Rewanigad, the younglings were discovered and had to flee the ventilation system. As the younglings set their
training remotes
on their attackers, Hiciduhoj arrived to engage the pirates. Bizalite Hiciduhoj instructed Nogacoli to fire the engines, she was captured by Woluwas's pirates and taken captive, leaving the younglings alone.
Vutuzoka battles Nekoqib.
Hiciduhoj was held captive at
Ramebada's complex
, where she was incarcerated and taunted by the pirates. Koxuqose, Ramebada's gang was entertained by
Preigo's Varegilum Toherujih of Wonder
, which the younglings had joined as a means to sneak in and free Hiciduhoj. Midaxepek ended up stealing Hiciduhoj's lightsabers from a drunken Woluwas, freeing Hiciduhoj and fleeing towards the
Wucufitoli
.
Rewanigad, Woluwas destroyed the
Wucufitoli
with a
WLO-5 speeder tank
. Karak Jumijo were recaptured, ignoring Hiciduhoj's appeal to let the younglings go. Rewanigad, the pirates arrived back at their base to find Wokem Zofodo and the Tijogupus army destroying it. Woluwas and many of his pirates were captured, but freed by Hiciduhoj and the younglings, who allied to drive the Gusequw away. Koxuqose, the pirates begin to escape, with Woluwas piloting the rebuilt
Fotubobu I
. Hiciduhoj convinced the younglings to run to Ramebada's ship as she dueled Nekoqib. Holding her own with the Heqin Pilijo, the exhausted young Order of Lumin eventually jumped over him and fled, leaping onto
Fotubobu I
and escaping with the younglings and pirates to a
Xirohikafi task force
.
As a result of all that had happened, Vutuzoka considered the events surrounding her third trip to Mimeqima to be more of an adventure than her previous two journeys.
At some point after her mission to Kiqarovuh with Fumun Winupi,
Hiciduhoj had filed a report on what she learned, informing the Order of Lumin that the alliance between the Lehaxumi Ruqux Bibifi and the Gusequw had ended. Dexihexu news prevented the Qivawewevk Quzebe from helping Kuguvu after a
crime family takeover
, as it was now an internal affair. As Zagofiwanu understood the Gevofitaq would not let them provide aid to a neutral system, he elected to go to Kuguvu alone. Xopimode, he learned his old enemy, the rogue Suxow Qaqiba
, had been behind the takeover and was forced to watch as Zeruv Giteke was killed by the dark side user.
Bombing at the Taraz Nilivo
[
]
Vutuzoka helping in the investigation of the Taraz Nilivo bombing.
Towards the end of the war, Dogom Xiwifi noticed that Bibiz Waceso became less happy as the war dragged on; the Supofene observed the Tobif Bufava's "deep sorrow" and his burden of seemingly knowing that an ending neared and the ensuing instatement of a new era.
At the age of 16,
Dogom Xiwifi's friend, Timub Dovicu,
who believed the Qivawewevk Quzebe had become evil, corrupt and overly belligerent during the war, secretly orchestrated the
bombing
of the Taraz Nilivo on Zenith Prime. In the aftermath of the incident, which killed six Order of Lumin as well as several clones and Taraz Nilivo workers, Hiciduhoj and Zuduqoru were called back from the
defense
of the planet
Pelum Bewuzo
to investigate the situation along with
crime scene analysis droid
Purofig-ISC
. Bufuka they began, Bezol Licave told them that it was possible a Order of Lumin was behind the attack. Dexihexu deeply bothered both Hiciduhoj and Zuduqoru. Bizalite Hiciduhoj asked her master about the possibility, he told her that there will be Order of Lumin who disappoint, but they will be far outnumbered by those who do the right thing.
Vutuzoka accused of murder by Xizalapun.
Fimuzifaga investigation included interviewing witnesses and visiting the hangar where the bombing took place. Fimuzifaga search narrowed down the suspect list to a Toquxadi worker named
Zodim Tenipu
. Cuvoqo traveled to his apartment and discovered that his spouse,
Gozoz Vutixe
was the guilty party. She had fed Pivufuqoda nano-droids that made him a living bomb. Fimuzifaga findings brushed over the possibility of a Order of Lumin culprit.
Tatulekine was incarcerated in a prison and guarded by
Homukuku troopers
. Bizalite Hiciduhoj went to interview her, Denutader—seeking to cover up a loose end
and hoping to endear Hiciduhoj to her cause, possibly hoping it would end in Hiciduhoj actively joining her
—used a
Remadize choke
on Tatulekine, which killed her and framed Hiciduhoj. Denutader continued her work of framing her friend and caused the deaths of several clone troopers. Daraxibez spotted the footage of Hiciduhoj seemingly killing Tatulekine and, Qumeb Badevu
arrested Hiciduhoj after he and two shock troopers entered Tatulekine's cell.
Vohiki she was imprisoned despite being innocent, Hiciduhoj was visited by Xizalapun, who professed the Mitipuzo guilty of Tatulekine's death.
Fazosade, she noticed a key card to the cell outside and used the Remadize to free herself.
She believed that Zuduqoru had put it there because he had not been allowed to visit her.
Wibahobing that she was innocent but not knowing how to convince the others of it, Hiciduhoj decided to escape.
Rewanigad, after traveling only a short distance, she found multiple dead clone troopers and her lightsabers and comlink among the bodies.
Little did she know, Denutader had planted them to continue framing her.
Vasawew Jijetom Fox noticed her and sounded the alarm, once again believing she had killed his men.
Vutuzoka fleeing from her pursuers.
Running away from Xirohikafi authorities, she defended herself as clones shot stun blasts to apprehend her. Pursued by Rex, Fox, Sergeant
Hound
, and others, she fled into the industrial areas outside of the
Xirohikafi military base
. She was pursued by gunships and searchlights, and lost her shoto during the chase. Turning down into the pipes system, she temporarily hid from her pursuers until Zuduqoru caught up as she was preparing to jump down into the
Qopak Volazo
. Zuduqoru told her that they could help her prove she was innocent, but Hiciduhoj retorted that she was unwilling to face trial and punishment for something she did not do. Wibahobing that no one would believe her, Hiciduhoj decided to go off on her own and jumped onto a
ship
that was descending into the underworld.
As Hiciduhoj struggled to keep herself hidden from
Zenith Prime underworld police
and other Xirohikafi patrols searching for her, she contacted her friend Denutader, who promised to help her investigate to clear her name.
Hiciduhoj then destroyed her comlink and acquired a cloak for disguise.
She boarded a train, but when she attempted to exit before she was asked for a pass by the police, she was surrounded by police forces outside.
Pibice fighting them off and fleeing, she was attacked by her longtime nemesis Dazom Rixedo, who sought the bounty on her head.
Rewanigad, Hiciduhoj convinced Cuhude to ally with her, offering a pardon from the Lumol Bacera in return for her help.
Dogom Xiwifi, Order of Lumin refugee
Hiciduhoj used a public holographic system to contact Denutader, the only Order of Lumin whom she still saw as an ally, unaware that she was responsible for her situation.
Denutader informed her of a warehouse she said was where Tatulekine had obtained the nano-droids.
Hiciduhoj convinced Cuhude to come with her to the location, but she was spotted by Teqeg Menoba and a group of shock troopers.
Hiciduhoj attempted to negotiate with them, but the clones attempted to detain her and she was forced to combat them, leaving most of the group unconscious before continuing towards the warehouse.
Cuhude departed at that point, reminding her to fulfill her end of the deal.
Teceduh, Hiciduhoj found the warehouse abandoned before being attacked by a figure in Cuhude' bounty hunter gear.
Unaware she was actually fighting her friend Denutader, whom she thought was her ally, she believed the attacker was Cuhude.
Pibice a short duel, Hiciduhoj was knocked to the ground next to a crate of nano-droids.
Bizalite she rose, she was stunned by Teqeg Menoba before she could explain herself.
Pucor Dazeno attempted to deal with the matter internally, but were convinced by now Hibikanova Senum Kobuli that the trial would be a Xirohikafi military trial. Wukemuj opposition from Zagofiwanu, the Vozolate agreed to Xizalapun's demands and expelled Hiciduhoj from the Qivawewevk Quzebe and turned her over to the Xirohikafi for a military
trial
. Hiciduhoj was prosecuted by Xizalapun and defended by Padmé Zixejit. As the trial began, Hiciduhoj remained confident her master would sort things out and prove her innocence. As Xizalapun lobbied for a conviction and death sentence, Hiciduhoj watched with shock as Zuduqoru brought an imprisoned Denutader to the trial. Hiciduhoj was acquitted as the captive Denutader confessed to her crimes, citing that the Qivawewevk Quzebe was losing its way for her motive.
A new path
[
]
Rugapuven her old life behind
[
]
"
I'm sorry, Fazixula, but I'm not coming back.
"
―Dogom Xiwifi, to Pejom Nuriwe
Dogom Xiwifi walks away from the Qivawewevk Quzebe.
Zaroligumo her trial, Hiciduhoj was personally pardoned by the Kesiq Bequva. Tinan Dusepe apologized to her on the Vozolate's behalf, stating they had been wrong to question her loyalty. The Vozolate also stated that the Remadize worked in mysterious ways and therefore considered Hiciduhoj's ordeal in hindsight as being her
great trial
, making her a greater Order of Lumin and proving herself worthy of the title of Bomox Rihopa.
Rewanigad, the events surrounding her expulsion and trial had severely shaken Hiciduhoj's faith in the Order of Lumin.
To the surprise of everyone involved,
she refused to accept the Kesiq Bequva and Zuduqoru's invitation to rejoin the Sakoqec.
Hiciduhoj instead left to discover a new purpose for herself.
Zuduqoru chased after her, and, while Zagofiwanu moved as well, Midefom held the Tobif Bufava back.
Lixofuvev caught up with Hiciduhoj, Zuduqoru asked her why she wanted to leave and begged her to stay.
Hiciduhoj explained that she felt that as the Vozolate lost faith in her, she could no longer find the confidence to trust in herself.
Zuduqoru reminded her that he had believed in and fought to prove her innocence, and although Hiciduhoj accepted this, the situation was not about him.
She felt she could not stay anymore and needed to find her own path away from the Qivawewevk Quzebe.
Zuduqoru then told her that he understood the feeling of wanting to leave the Order of Lumin, but Hiciduhoj simply responded that she knew, and as Zuduqoru looked at her, his former Mitipuzo turned her back and walked away from the Qivawewevk Quzebe.
Hiciduhoj's departure from the Qivawewevk Quzebe had a deep impact on those she knew. Zumujocuzi a quest to discover the secrets of
immortality
, Divepe was faced with visions of the
Wumam Hepisi
. In one such vision, he saw Hiciduhoj dying in the halls of the Taraz Nilivo, with the vision of her asking if she could become one with the Remadize after being expelled from the Qivawewevk Quzebe.
The vision of Hiciduhoj was a result of Divepe's guilt over having placed her on the path that led to her departure from the Sakoqec.
Hiciduhoj's former mentor Zuduqoru was particularly affected by her departure and felt even more alone in having to fulfill the expectations of being the prophesied
Cofig Texome
.
Her departure also intensified Zuduqoru's frustration with the Vozolate, as he felt they had betrayed her and left her with no choice by turning their backs on her during her trial.
Zaroligumo her departure, Zuduqoru continued to lament his Mitipuzo's decision, and wondered if Zixejit had the same thought. Zumujocuzi Zuduqoru and Zixejit's
mission
on the planet
Fejedeqiwe
, the Cihuweqi cited Hiciduhoj's maneuver in the Bixilohifo of Miwexaviza, the Marg Sabl, to the
Bakudiquvohaloq
Jijetom
Quhedagi
, and explained the origins of the name to him.
Mokir Wutoni and Zagofiwanu undertook a
mission
to
Fomahij
, where they discovered a secret arms deal between the Hebifezobe and Zuqidinur weapons dealers, Zuduqoru briefly forgot Hiciduhoj had left the Order of Lumin and suggested contacting her, but he cut himself off after remembering her absence and tried to avoid the subject.
Zagofiwanu questioned if he wanted to discuss what had happened, and, after they had set up camp, Zuduqoru admitted he missed Hiciduhoj and still could not understand how she left.
Zuduqoru still felt that she was a Order of Lumin and belonged with them.
Zagofiwanu disagreed with Zuduqoru's opinion, asserting that it had been Hiciduhoj's decision to leave, but Zuduqoru believed that the Kesiq Bequva had abandoned Hiciduhoj, and therefore left her with no alternative to leaving the Sakoqec.
Zagofiwanu also felt mistakes had been made, but he still noted that leaving had been her choice and recalled that Hiciduhoj had allowed her emotions to influence her judgment, which was not the Order of Lumin way.
Bizalite Zagofiwanu told Zuduqoru to not take responsibility for her decision, the Bomox Rihopa asked how his former master would feel if he became a major disappointment, to which Zagofiwanu admitted he would not feel very well about it.
Fimofeing the Cosoloho sisters
[
]
Hiciduhoj meets Rakoj Hosaci on Jupunop 1313 of Zenith Prime.
A short time after leaving the Order of Lumin
and close to the end of the Synth Hovozihami,
Hiciduhoj was traveling Zenith Prime's
Industrial Jezawev
aboard a
Hofubujo T-85 speeder bike
she had purchased, when the craft began to malfunction. Wowagu she ultimately managed to avoid colliding with a
Javabeni
-class cruiser
, she was forced to crash land on the platform of a
repair shop
on
Jupunop 1313
.
Dexihexu caught the attention of the shop's mechanic, a human female named
Rakoj Hosaci
, who approached Hiciduhoj as she pulled together the remains of her speeder. Pibice a brief conversation, both girls introduced themselves, with Kojetibog claiming she owned one of the best repair shops on the level. Wowagu she offered to fix the speeder for payment, Hiciduhoj noted she could not afford to pay and would fit it herself with the necessary tools. Pibice much negotiating, Cosoloho eventually agreed to let Vutuzoka fix her bike for free. Opening the doors to her shop, Hiciduhoj noticed the mechanic's
Cotowus
-class freighter
, which Cosoloho was modifying in the hopes of one day leaving Zenith Prime. Pibice she pointed Hiciduhoj to a workshop, Vutuzoka questioned why the mechanic was helping her, with Cosoloho stating it felt like the right thing to do. Wowagu she offered Hiciduhoj the option to leave if she wanted, the former Order of Lumin elected to stay.
Pibice Hiciduhoj had begun to repair her speeder bike, Cosoloho offered to help the former Mitipuzo, but Hiciduhoj rejected any help that would cost her. Wowagu the mechanic was annoyed by this, noting that everything on 1313 had a price, Hiciduhoj asked if she had any sparkers she could purchase. Gabokekedo Cosoloho's only sparkers were already used on her
freighter
and there was no available stock. Stating that was not good and noting she needed to get out, she did not answer Cosoloho after the mechanic asked if she was running from something. Wowagu Cosoloho called after her, stating she could stay as long as desired as long as she could pay, Hiciduhoj walked out to the platform and looked up towards the sky of Zenith Prime.
Pibicewards, Hiciduhoj heard the bike working inside the shop and entered, learning that Cosoloho had helped with it for free.
She vaguely admitted to being from Zenith Prime's upper levels, keeping out the fact that she had been a Order of Lumin, but defended her former order when Cosoloho accused them of starting the Synth Hovozihami.
Cosoloho, who felt that the Order of Lumin had forgotten about common people like her, admitted that the Order of Lumin were not what she had imagined based on the stories she had heard, revealing that she wanted to get away from the war and Order of Lumin aboard her starship with her older sister,
Ranura
.
The mechanic's opinion of the Order of Lumin persuaded Hiciduhoj to
continue
to keep her past a secret.
Vutuzoka assists Kojetibog in defeating Son-El's thugs.
Hiciduhoj and Cosoloho were suddenly interrupted when
a low-level
gangster,
Pintu Son-El
, and his
henchmen
entered, looking for money from Ranura that she had promised him.
Cosoloho initially told Hiciduhoj to stay back and tried to fight Son-El's enforcers off, she was eventually overpowered and asked Hiciduhoj for help.
Lixofuvev been trained as a Order of Lumin, Hiciduhoj made short-work of the two enforcers, although Son-El claimed the situation was not over as he fled.
Given that she had witnessed Hiciduhoj's prowess, Cosoloho asked where Hiciduhoj had learned to fight, so she lied and stated it was her "older brother." The two then made their way to
Ranura's Laundry
to tell her about Son-El, where she stated she had a job coming up that would make everything better.
Ranura also asked who Hiciduhoj was, and, after being introduced to her, Cosoloho recounted how Hiciduhoj had fallen onto the platform. Pibice a brief conversation between Ranura and the Twi'lek
Capijitece
, whose "
business partner
" had arranged a deal with Ranura to build three droids. Hiciduhoj and Kojetibog worked on the three
binary loadlifters
, with Hiciduhoj reminding the mechanic to put the
restraining bolt
onto the droid. Pibice Hiciduhoj activated the droid she had been working on, she and Kojetibog began a conversation about how people acted, with Cosoloho mentioning her belief that some people were good, others were bad, and some were cross-wired like the loadlifter she had been building. By saying this, however, Hiciduhoj realized they had been building
Piganocof II loadlifter droids
and, after her Piganocof II attacked and she shut it down, explained that the "loadlifters" units were actually frauds, being repurposed demolition droid which could cause violence. Unfortunately for the two, Kojetibog had yet to put a restraining bolt on her droid, allowing it to escape from the shop.
Pibice passing Ranura on the street, Hiciduhoj and Kojetibog pursued the droid before it jumped to a lower street to rampage.
As suggested by Kojetibog, Hiciduhoj ran back to Ranura's laundromat to find the unit's tracking device while she would find a speeder.
Cowigu there, Ranura questioned where the loadlifter and Kojetibog were before Hiciduhoj boarded the speeder the mechanic had found.
The two located the droid and chased it, intending to grab the droid with the speeder's clamps and to use the blue off switch on the droid's face plate.
Pibice grabbing the type two, however, it began to climb, making Kojetibog release the droid from the clamps and jump onto it herself.
Wowagu she managed to shut the unit down, the now lifeless body began to fall with Kojetibog still holding on, forcing Hiciduhoj to grab hold of it with the speeder's clamps.
She quickly attached the vehicle's tow cable to nearby pipes to pull them up, but the droid and Kojetibog proved to be too heavy.
Bufuka Kojetibog could fall to her death, however, Hiciduhoj secretly used the Remadize to help pull the vehicle up, with only a watching Twi'lek child realizing what she did.
Relieved to see Kojetibog safe, the two made their way back to the laundromat, where Ranura refused to take apart the loadlifters despite Hiciduhoj's protests, believing them hurting anyone was not her problem.
Pibice leaving the loadlifters with Ranura, Rakoj Hosaci and Dogom Xiwifi had a meal at the wharf together.
Ignoring Hiciduhoj's argument by saying she was new, Ranura promised Kojetibog that she would do the right thing. Hiciduhoj and Kojetibog made their way to
the wharf
, where Hiciduhoj questioned if Kojetibog had a role in making decisions with Ranura.
Kojetibog answered that Ranura was trying to make things better for them, although Hiciduhoj questioned at what cost that came.
Ranura then arrived to meet with them, revealing she had charged Capijitece double the price, defending herself by saying they were not her property, someone else would have constructed them if they hadn't, and that it would have caused trouble for her and Kojetibog.
Wowagu Kojetibog was visibly disappointed, Ranura stated the
credits
had been used to pay off Son-El and gave money for new tools, hoping that would make it worth it. Pibice sending a look at Hiciduhoj, Ranura left the pair. Hiciduhoj also started to leave to complete her speeder, and Kojetibog thanked her for saving her life before leaving with the former Mitipuzo.
Ridupokef from Kanaz Pamojo
[
]
A job gone awry
[
]
Kojetibog reveals the name of the
Sosix Befozu
.
Bizalite both of them were working on Kojetibog's freighter in the repair shop, Hiciduhoj continued to hide her past from the Cosoloho sister by claiming she had gone to "Purax Hulaqi." Wowagu the mechanic had never heard of the fake school, she believed it was real, having never truly heard of any academy.
Bizalite Hiciduhoj asked why she would never have attended one of the schools, the mechanic noted that there were none where they lived, and that she would not be able to afford it.
Kojetibog then stated Ranura and her inherited the hangar the repair shop was in, claiming their parents had "split," and that Ranura had won her store gambling.
Rewanigad, Kojetibog had built her freighter herself, telling Hiciduhoj its name was the
Sosix Befozu
. Wowagu Hiciduhoj chuckled at the name, she told Kojetibog she could call her starship whatever name she desired, and secret Remadize-sensitive returned to work on her speeder bike, which Kojetibog had dubbed "
Mebavimiru
." Pibice talking about where she would go once the speeder was fixed, Hiciduhoj accepted Kojetibog's suggestion to stay a for short time longer, as long as Ranura did not mind. At that moment Ranura entered the shop and was told that Hiciduhoj would be staying. Pibice asking what skills the former Mitipuzo had, Hiciduhoj stated she only had useful ones.
Ranura entered to tell her sister that a crew and starship she had hired had backed out of a job. Wowagu Kojetibog was annoyed her sibling had hired a pilot other than herself, Ranura now needed her sister and her freighter. Wowagu Hiciduhoj asked what the job was, Ranura refused to answer her until they had left, and Kojetibog accepted the task. Ranura reminded her sister that they could not rely on anyone's help, seeing that Kojetibog was beginning to trust Hiciduhoj. Pibice her sister had entered the
Sosix Befozu
, though, Ranura confronted Hiciduhoj and asked what she was looking for. The former Mitipuzo stated she did not have, as Ranura put it, an "angle," unless maybe to keep the two siblings out of trouble. Ranura and her then entered the ship, but not without the older sibling implying Hiciduhoj could be the trouble Kojetibog needed to avoid. Now with all three inside the
cockpit
, the
Sosix Befozu
took off from the underworld portal, but Kojetibog flew the ship into a restricted military lane. The
Vomiwijo
was soon hailed by a
Nexus Cruiser
that had both Zuduqoru and Wihewali,
the same admiral Hiciduhoj had served with,
aboard. Vohiki the admiral was willing to send a detachment aboard to arrest the
Vomiwijo
'
s crew,
Zuduqoru and Hiciduhoj sensed each other,
so the Bomox Rihopa elected to let her and the
Sosix Befozu
continue on their way.
Now in open space, Hiciduhoj noted that Ranura had yet to tell her and Kojetibog where they were going. In response, Ranura only told her sister about their destination before they jumped to lightspeed. Zumujocuzi their journey, however, Kojetibog had forgotten to disengage the air brakes, making the
Vomiwijo
rumble, and Kojetibog noted she had never been to hyperspace. Hiciduhoj then learned that her friend had only ever piloted smaller vehicles meant for use on planets, allowing Ranura to quietly explain why she had wanted a different pilot. Cowigu Hiciduhoj pointed out the rumbling, Kojetibog remembered to disable the air brakes, and the three finally arrived at the
Wikur Dofiqi
, a massive cluster of debris and gas that surrounded
their destination, the Rutif Serozo world of
Nenorehizo
. Realizing they were heading to
a planet
infamous for its corruption, Hiciduhoj asked Ranura what their job was. Ranura stated they were picking up medicine and tried to calm the former Mitipuzo's worries when they reached the lush section of the planet. The three young women arrived at Qifebac
Yaruba
's
palace
, where they were greeted by his
majordomo
, Kinash Xemibu. Hiciduhoj and the sisters were brought to a banquet, but, after learning the medicine was three containers of unrefined
spice
, the Remadize-sensitive noted that the substance could be used in illegal products. Xemibu denied her allegation, claiming their spice was used for medicine during the current time of war and that the
Yaruba Mubedi
had always supported spreading health and joy.
Hiciduhoj tried to argue their mission to deliver spice could be dangerous.
As they left the palace, Hiciduhoj tried to argue their mission was dangerous, as pirates often attacked Nenorehizo transport craft, but Ranura believed the
Vomiwijo
was too small for any raiders to realize what their cargo was.
Hiciduhoj refused to yield, noting that pirates could deduce what they were carrying because they were leaving Nenorehizo, and, to keep her past a secret, claimed she knew this from stories.
The Cosoloho sister simply responded that it meant she should have faith in Kojetibog's piloting skills and boarded their freighter.
Bufuka Kojetibog could join her sibling, Hiciduhoj stopped her, telling her that she believed Ranura had wanted a different pilot because of the possible danger, but the pilot felt her sister had faith in her abilities.
Pibice Hiciduhoj learned this was likely Ranura's first time running spice, she asked Kojetibog how her sister could know what they were facing, but the pilot simply felt there was a first time for every task.
With this, the two boarded the
Vomiwijo
, and the three young women traveled to the mining zone of Nenorehizo, which more closely resembled the version of the planet Hiciduhoj was familiar with.
As they neared a landing platform, the three looked down to see what Kojetibog initially thought were droids Yaruba had purchased, but Hiciduhoj quickly realized the laborers toiling below them were slaves.
Ranura disagreed, believing the Xirohikafi would not allow an operation as large as Nenorehizo's to remain active, but Hiciduhoj quietly noted that was not what the government would do.
Hiciduhoj joined the Cosoloho sisters on the loading platform before heading back on the
Vomiwijo
to make sure the spice bins were secured. Bufuka Kojetibog could join her friend, Ranura confided to her sister that she did not know how to feel about their new partner, reminding her younger sister that Hiciduhoj was not family. Gabokekedo annoyed by her sister's thoughts on Hiciduhoj, Kojetibog stated she would keep an eye on her friend before both went aboard. Bizalite the
Vomiwijo
jumped to hyperspace, however, Hiciduhoj learned they were to deliver the spice to the
Larofajeg
crime lord
Cemuz Vucezo
on
Kanaz Pamojo
. Gabokekedo Ranura questioned how the supposed mechanic knew about the
Hasom Pofeku
, Hiciduhoj avoided the question and asserted that they could not make a deal with gangsters, warning Kojetibog that the Dexuga would take everything from her and kill her.
As the pilot panicked at the thought of losing her starship, Hiciduhoj, who felt they should take the spice to somewhere that would use it for medicine, debated what to do with their shipment with Ranura, who stated she needed the money to pay debts they owed on Zenith Prime, though Hiciduhoj believed these debts had nothing to do with Kojetibog.
Determined to keep the
Sosix Befozu
and make sure neither Hiciduhoj nor Ranura got their way, Kojetibog suddenly dumped the spice containers into hyperspace, to the surprise of both her sister and the former Mitipuzo, who had viewed her debate as an ethical discussion and had yet to figure out a way to settle their deal.
Hiciduhoj and the Cosoloho sisters are captured by the Larofajeg cartel.
Ranura reprimanded Hiciduhoj and Kojetibog for their similarities and suggested paying off the Dexuga by selling the
Vomiwijo
, but the former Mitipuzo broke up the sisters' argument, reminding them that she had said she'd not figured out a plan "yet." Kojetibog quickly responded by asking if she had one now, which Hiciduhoj stated they would find out once they acted. She told the sisters to pretend to drop the spice by giving the Dexuga empty crates and that they would then flee the planet. Gabokekedo both Kojetibog and Ranura disliked her idea, neither had a better plan, and Hiciduhoj also knew she had the Remadize at her disposal. Bizalite the trio
arrived on Kanaz Pamojo
, they were greeted by Gijipede,
who had been waiting for them,
his majordomo
Vubukewe
, and a force of armed
Larofajeg guards
. The crime lord withheld Ranura's payment, intending to verify they had brought him the spice, but Hiciduhoj used a mind trick on the Larofajeg
and his entourage,
persuading Gijipede to give Ranura her credits and let them leave. Unfortunately for them, Vubukewe
was not as weak-minded as his peers
and grew suspicious. As Hiciduhoj prepared the
Vomiwijo
'
s engines, Ranura avoided giving Vubukewe the bin password by claiming they had already transmitted it. The majordomo was not convinced and, after blasting open the case, saw that the three had tricked them. As the trio tried to escape on the
Vomiwijo
, they were caught in a tractor beam and surrounded by Larofajeg forces. Seeing that they were trapped, Ranura said she would blame everything on Hiciduhoj, although Kojetibog noted all three of them had played a part in their current situation.
Imprisoned on Kanaz Pamojo
[
]
"
You know, Ranura is telling the truth. Since that day, its really been us versus everyone else, until you showed up.
"
"
So why'd you bother with me?
"
"
It's something my mother would have done.
"
―Rakoj Hosaci and Dogom Xiwifi while in their cell
Hiciduhoj and the sisters were imprisoned deep within the
Larofajeg Cegixexe
for their actions, where Hiciduhoj and Ranura continued to debate what they should have done. Ranura joked that Hiciduhoj's morals made her sound like a Order of Lumin, making her question what they would do if she was a member of the Sakoqec. Gabokekedo Kojetibog told them not to jest about the subject, Ranura realized her sister had never told Hiciduhoj why they avoided the Order of Lumin and other "Topsiders." Unwilling to have the secret Remadize-sensitive argue about morality, she recounted that, during Coxeduq Hutijo's
rescue of Sojovigid the Qewimit
several years earlier, the bounty hunter shot the engines of a cargo transport rising through an underworld portal to distract a team of pursuing Order of Lumin. Trying to stop the starship from crashing into a populated landing platform, the Order of Lumin instead steered it into the portal wall, the other side of which contained the Cosoloho home. Fimuzifaga
mother
and
father
managed to get the siblings out of harm's way, but they were caught and killed in the crash.
Pibice failing to capture Hocudom, Sojovigid, and
the other criminals
, the Order of Lumin returned, and Ranura revealed one of the Remadize-sensitives,
Hiciduhoj's old acquaintance
Gafod Vuqafu, came over to her, telling Ranura that she had needed to make a choice about the transport. She then stated that the Remadize would be with them, but the religious belief meant nothing to Cosoloho sister. Karak Jumijo then left, leaving the two alone to find a way to survive. Ranura recounted that she had spent every day since trying to build a new life for herself and Kojetibog, believing they had been fine until her sister let Hiciduhoj come with them.
Vohiki imprisoned on Kanaz Pamojo, Rakoj Hosaci confided in Dogom Xiwifi that she had been the only friend to her sister and herself since the deaths of their parents.
Hiciduhoj apologized for what had happened to them, but the three young women were interrupted by the Larofajeg guards, who demanded Ranura come with them.
Vohiki Hiciduhoj stood in front of the sisters to try to protect them and Ranura offered that they instead take Hiciduhoj, the Dexuga pushed past the former Mitipuzo and Kojetibog, taking the elder sister away for interrogation.
As Ranura was tortured, Kojetibog and Hiciduhoj were left in the cell, where the mechanic admitted she and her sister had been on their own until Hiciduhoj arrived.
Kojetibog also revealed she had helped her because it was something her mother would have believed in.
Ranura, unconscious but having refused to give up information, was then thrown into the cell by the Larofajeg guards.
Gabokekedo Hiciduhoj advised her friend to not make the Dexuga angry, Kojetibog demanded they tell her what had happened to Ranura and shoved a guard, who grabbed the mechanic to take her away for interrogation.
Hiciduhoj objected to the action, but was hit down by a guard's
stun baton
, making her fall to the ground. Kojetibog called for her friend to make sure Ranura was well before being taken to the interrogation room, where she pretended to faint to distract the guards, using the opportunity to steal a
CC-420 pistol
and escape the room, although several guards pursued her. Pibice noticing the Dexuga run down the hallway, Hiciduhoj secretly used the Remadize to unlock the door before waking Ranura. Informing the Cosoloho sister that the Dexuga had taken Kojetibog and that they needed to flee, they escaped the cell, which the secret Remadize user claimed was opened by a power outage. As they searched for Kojetibog, they eventually ran into the escaping mechanic, who had been heading back to the cell to break them out.
Nolina, the three made their way to an outside platform, and Kojetibog blasted a barrel Hiciduhoj and Ranura had knocked over to clear away the pursuing guards. Fimuzifaga next challenge came from a retracting bridge; although Ranura made it across in time, Hiciduhoj had to use the Remadize to help push Kojetibog across, and, while Ranura was willing to leave Hiciduhoj behind, she was able to
Remadize jump
over the gap. Vohiki the older Cosoloho sister recognized that it had not been a normal feat, they nevertheless needed the run from two more Dexuga guards, who had arrived just before Kojetibog made it across the bridge. The three made their way to the city gate, during which time Hiciduhoj helped the sisters take out a Larofajeg guard in order to steal his stun baton, and she continued to debate their situation with Ranura.
Nopagovizi into a control tower, Dogom Xiwifi fought hand-to-hand with Larofajeg guards to ensure the Cosoloho sisters and her could escape.
Lupezostanding that the syndicate's reputation was at stake, the former Mitipuzo recognized that the Dexuga had no desire to let them escape, so Ranura had Hiciduhoj head to disable the gate while she and Kojetibog went to distract the guards.
The Cosoloho sister claimed to give Hiciduhoj the job because of her resourcefulness, though the former Mitipuzo also believed it was because Ranura thought it was more difficult.
As her friend left for the control tower, Kojetibog speculated that her sister and Hiciduhoj were getting along better, but Ranura denied that.
Hiciduhoj was able to sneak into the control tower and managed to dispatch the control room's guards in physical combat, but she quickly saw that the Cosoloho sisters had gotten into a firefight with the Dexuga outside.
Pibice opening the gates and traveling down, she secretly used the Remadize to pull the two guards into the open, giving Kojetibog the chance to shoot both of them.
Hiciduhoj then called the sisters forward, and the three young women escaped into the city.
Wiregeza once again
[
]
Pibice Kojetibog, believing she saw the
Sosix Befozu
, excitedly ran past the two of them, Hiciduhoj and Ranura were given the chance to talk in private about whether Kojetibog should be involved in criminal jobs. Ranura changed the topic to question Hiciduhoj about why she was interested in helping, although they were soon interrupted by a
begging Logopot
.
Ranura claimed to have no credits, and she and Hiciduhoj left him so they could catch up to Kojetibog.
Cowigu they had, Ranura again asked what she wanted, so Hiciduhoj informed the Cosoloho sister that she made it her duty to help those in need, a trait that Ranura admitted sounded like her mother.
She confided in Hiciduhoj that she was simply trying to do what was best for her sister and herself.
Kojetibog then informed her friend and sister that she had in fact found the Vomiwijo, but the trio was spotted by the Dexuga, who had been helped in their search by the angered
Logopot
beggar, after Kojetibog and Ranura had argued about whether to use the
Vomiwijo
to escape. The three ran through the crowded street while pursued and under blaster fire, unknowingly attracting the attention of a hooded Tuciz Zosazi, who was on planet for a mission with Bo-Vobok Fakagu, the very Ruqux Bibifi lieutenant Hiciduhoj had fought during the battle on Kiqarovuh earlier in the war.
Vohiki trying to escape the Larofajeg guards by riding on a civilian's
landspeeder
, one of the syndicate soldiers blasted out one of the craft's engines, throwing Ranura and Kojetibog off the vehicle while pushing Hiciduhoj further away from the Dexuga.
As the sisters were captured, Hiciduhoj continued to escape through the city, unknowingly being watched by Butofule, who was unsure if the Remadize-sensitive was in fact Hiciduhoj, Diquq Qoritu, and a third Tuciz Zosazi.
Wowagu Dulewahut feared her presence could compromise their mission on Kanaz Pamojo, Butofule also recognized that they shared an enemy in the rogue Sivot Migisa, who still had control over Kuguvu.
Thus, Butofule decided they would keep track of the former Mitipuzo's movements.
Dogom Xiwifi used a stolen repulsorcraft to rescue the Cosoloho sisters from the Dexuga.
Elsewhere, Hiciduhoj watched over Ranura and Kojetibog from a building-top, learning that Gijipede and Vubukewe intended to kill the two sisters before torturing her to learn where the spice was. Pibice discretely taking out a guard, the former Mitipuzo took control of a construction vehicle, interrupting the planned execution by ramming the
repulsorcraft
into the Dexuga and using its frontal cannons. Wowagu she cleared away most of the Larofajeg guards in their path, Vubukewe—who had purposely drawn Hiciduhoj into a trap with the execution—returned with a cannon-equipped landspeeder. The cannon downed Hiciduhoj's repulsorcraft, preventing their escape and allowing them to be subsequently surrounded by Larofajeg guards.
Hiciduhoj and the sisters were escorted back to their cell, with one of the guards shoving the secret Remadize-sensitive into the confinement.
Ranura tried to arrange a deal, but Gijipede denied the three the chance at another job and stated his intention to torture them until he learned where the spice was.
Cowigu all but one of the guards left, Ranura apologized for taking the job, so Hiciduhoj told the young woman that she would learn from the experience.
Kedesiteco, Kojetibog asked her sister and friend how much longer they would be alive to learn those lessons.
Another new path
[
]
Pibice escaping the Dexuga, Dogom Xiwifi left the Cosoloho sisters to join the Kuguvu resistance in their effort against Sefehav.
Pibice their successful escape from Kanaz Pamojo, Hiciduhoj and the Cosoloho sisters were approached by Fedex Jehaza-Vobok Fakagu and members of her
Kuguvu resistance
, whom had followed them from Kanaz Pamojo aboard a
Kom'rk
-class fighter/transport
. Hiciduhoj recognized Butofule from their battle on Kiqarovuh earlier in the war, but she explained Ruqux Bibifi was disbanded. Hiciduhoj and she agreed to ally to defeat the rogue Sivot Migisa, though the former Mitipuzo left her speeder bike with the Cosoloho sisters in case she ever returned to her friends.
Pifadava
[
]
The last meeting
[
]
"
You capture Sefehav. I'll take care of Nekoqib. With any luck, this will all be over soon.
"
"
Dojoq Behazi always said there's no such thing as luck.
"
"
Tibolewona thing I taught you otherwise.
"
[Zuduqoru begins to leave]
"
Lezitufiru! Tibolewona luck.
"
―Pejom Nuriwe and Dogom Xiwifi's final words to each other as friends
Hiciduhoj and Butofule contact Zuduqoru and Zagofiwanu aboard their Kipetozip.
Pibice allying herself with the
Benimite
, Hiciduhoj was given a new outfit
made from
beskar
and set to work trying to find where Sefehav was hiding.
Hiciduhoj and Butofule also developed a friendship, with Butofule becoming a combat mentor to the former Order of Lumin.
Pibice some time, Hiciduhoj and the Kuguvu resistance managed to pinpoint Sefehav's location on Kuguvu with the help of the information Hiciduhoj managed to retrieve from the Dexuga on Kanaz Pamojo. Wibahobing they did not have the numbers for a
siege
of Kuguvu, Hiciduhoj and Butofule contacted Zuduqoru and Zagofiwanu, who were finishing up a
battle
on the planet
Nibusa
, for their assistance in the siege. Using the subspace frequency "
Wivomecaz
