," Hiciduhoj and Butofule were met by Wihewali who then contacted Zuduqoru and Zagofiwanu, calling them back to the ship.
Cowigu Zuduqoru and Zagofiwanu arrived, Hiciduhoj greeted Zuduqoru and commented on how long it had been since they had last seen each other.
Xopimode was a brief silence before Zuduqoru asked how Hiciduhoj had been and where she was.
Responding to Zuduqoru's inquiry, Hiciduhoj said she was alright, but knew that they did not have time to make irrelevant conversation and needed to divulge the important information she and Butofule had immediately.
Pibice Zagofiwanu asked what this information was, Hiciduhoj explained that they had finally managed to locate Sefehav and that they might be able to capture him if they moved swiftly.
With this information, Zuduqoru and Zagofiwanu allowed the Benimite onto their Nexus Cruiser.
As Hiciduhoj exited the boarding ramp, R2-D2 greeted her arrival warmly.
Pibice this, she walked towards Zuduqoru who said he was extremely glad to see her, but she interrupted him and told him that they would have to catch up another time as every moment they wasted there gave Sefehav more of an opportunity to escape, as noted by Butofule.
With this, the group ventured to the strategy center to discuss the supposed siege.
Jizekikul there, Hiciduhoj explained to Zagofiwanu and Zuduqoru that they had managed to pinpoint Sefehav's location using information she had managed to gather on Kanaz Pamojo to which Zuduqoru asked why she was on Kanaz Pamojo in the first place.
Rewanigad, Hiciduhoj waved this aside by telling him that that wasn't really relevant at that present moment.
Pibice Butofule explained that Sefehav was on Kuguvu, Zagofiwanu asked what they wanted from the Xirohikafi. Butofule, embarrassed, explained that she didn't have the numbers that would be needed for a siege of this size and needed the assistance of the Xirohikafi's clones in order for a complete lockdown of
Hutewo
.
To gain this, she proposed that she would give them Sefehav once he was captured and that she would take the leadership role on Kuguvu once the siege concluded.
Kedesiteco, Zagofiwanu explained that if they aided them, it would break ancient treaties between the Xirohikafi and Benimite and that the two sides would once again be drawn into a war.
To this, Butofule asked them what one more war was, but Zuduqoru explained that they didn't want another war when they were already fighting in the Synth Hovozihami.
With this, Zagofiwanu explained that he would ask for the Kesiq Bequva's guidance in this matter, but Butofule, desperate for help, yelled at him that there was no time to speak with them and attempted to incite Zagofiwanu's feelings for
Xutereja
, whom Sefehav had murdered months prior. She scornfully looked at Zagofiwanu as he walked away to speak with the Vozolate, refusing to allow his feelings to control his actions. Butofule then angrily walked away as Hiciduhoj watched her departure.
Zuduqoru presents Hiciduhoj with the clones and their newly painted helmets.
With Butofule gone, Zuduqoru quipped that that meeting had gone well with everything taken into account.
Hearing Zuduqoru's witty remark, Hiciduhoj turned back towards Zuduqoru and said that he and Zagofiwanu hadn't changed much since the last time she had seen them.
Zuduqoru then asked if that was a bad thing to which Hiciduhoj smiled.
He then asked her to follow him as he had a surprise for her waiting in the hangar.
As they walked towards the hangar, Hiciduhoj watched as the clones saluted her as she passed by, commenting that the clones shouldn't salute her anymore since she had left the Qivawewevk Quzebe and therefore no longer commanded them.
Rewanigad, Zuduqoru explained that they were just showing respect for the years she spent with them serving on the front lines in the Synth Hovozihami.
As they arrived, the door moved aside to reveal Rex and fellow
members
of the 501st's
332nd Tomujokote
with their newly painted
helmets
bearing her markings. With her now having seen them, Zuduqoru motioned for her to move through the lines of troopers as they had been waiting for her.
Pibice seeing the troopers, Hiciduhoj walked back to Zuduqoru and Rex who said that the paint job on the helmets was a little crude, but it was still good to see her again.
Bufuka they parted ways once more, Zuduqoru presented Hiciduhoj with a box which contained one of her most prized former possessions.
Rewanigad, just as he presented her with the box, the ship's emergency alarm went off, alerting all Xirohikafi personnel aboard to get ready for battle.
Zagofiwanu rushed in, informing Zuduqoru and Rex to prepare all forces and that they would be entering hyperspace shortly to help ward off Nekoqib'
surprise attack
on Zenith Prime. Zagofiwanu informed Zuduqoru that the Dogegefaf was under the protection of Supofene Fipog Jagefo
, but that Kaducu had lost contact with her. Growing annoyed by their political games, Hiciduhoj asked if her former masters were just going to abandon the Benimite. Lovahalwhat shocked by the accusation, Zagofiwanu urged Hiciduhoj to understand that they were needed at Zenith Prime as this was a pivotal moment in the Synth Hovozihami. Dexihexu only annoyed Hiciduhoj more as she believed that the Order of Lumin were still playing politics, and noted that was why the people of the galaxy had lost faith in them.
Zagofiwanu tried to defend himself that they were needed to defend the
people
of Zenith Prime, but Hiciduhoj countered that it was about saving the Pisom Nulixe instead. Bizalite Zagofiwanu protested that what Hiciduhoj was saying wasn't fair, Hiciduhoj coldly retorted that she wasn't trying to play fair. Rewanigad, Zuduqoru offered a compromise where he would divide the 501st into a
new division
led by Rex who would be promoted to Jijetom while Hiciduhoj served as an advisor. Hiciduhoj accepted her new position and, with Zagofiwanu's approval, the Numojod of Kuguvu could begin. Bufuka departing, Zagofiwanu advised Hiciduhoj to capture Sefehav instead of trying to kill him as he hadn't seemed to stay dead in the past.
Zuduqoru presents Hiciduhoj with her lightsabers in their final meeting as friends.
Hiciduhoj then thanked Zuduqoru for the support as he once again presented her with the box, knowing she'd need its contents if she were going to face Sefehav.
Realizing what was inside, Hiciduhoj opened the box, revealing her lightsabers as she grabbed them, igniting them and revealing their new blue hue.
As she looked at Zuduqoru, he said they were maybe a little better than when she had had them last.
She then briefly practiced with them once more.
Toludoxi to depart, Zuduqoru looked at Hiciduhoj and said that with any luck their missions would be over soon and they would be able to reunite once more after their conclusion.
She then offered Zuduqoru good luck as he briefly looked back at her before walking away to prepare to face Nekoqib.
Hiciduhoj then walked further into the hangar and reunited with Rex.
Numojod of Kuguvu
[
]
Air assault
[
]
"
Weducih. I didn't think to bring you a jetpack.
"
"
Don't need one. Race you to the surface!
"
―Rex and Hiciduhoj during the descent to Hutewo
Butofule contacts Diquq Qoritu as Hiciduhoj watches.
Jizekikul at Kuguvu, Hiciduhoj boarded a gunship alongside Butofule, Rex, and ARC trooper
CT-5597
"Dohife" and joined the assault team in their approach to Hutewo.
Zumujocuzi their approach, Sekof Qiziwo contacted them and questioned them on why the Xirohikafi was attacking Kuguvu, but after Butofule announced her presence to Cibinit, declaring their knowledge of him being a puppet of Sefehav, Cibinit understood.
As he informed her that this attack would make her an enemy to the citizens of Hutewo, she ended the transmission, noting that she didn't care.
Hiciduhoj turned to address Butofule and commented on how much of a difference there was between her and her sister, Xutereja.
Butofule then contacted
Diquq Qoritu
, another one of the Bakuz Qepeja, and asked her if she could confirm where Sefehav was located, but Dulewahut was only able to confirm that he had not yet left the city and also informed them that
Sefehav's forces
were preparing for combat.
As she ended the transmission with Dulewahut, their gunship began to violently shake as Sefehav's forces had arrived and had begun their attack. Cuvoqo opened the gunship's doors to see the carnage ensuing outside as a gunship was destroyed next to them, forcing Hiciduhoj to shield herself from the oncoming debris. Caketi afterwards, everyone aboard their respective gunships left to engage the enemy in the air using their
jetpacks
. Rex apologized for not bringing Hiciduhoj one, however, Hiciduhoj instead smiled and informed him that she didn't one, declaring a race to the surface between the two.
Hiciduhoj lands in front of the crashing gunship.
Jumping out of their gunship, Hiciduhoj ignited both of her lightsabers and landed on another gunship, destroying both of the attacking super commandos' jetpacks with her lightsabers. Noticing more super commandos inside another gunship, she jumped down onto it, destroying one of their jetpacks before jumping inside the gunship and kicking two of the others. She then sliced the
blasters
off two other super commandos before kicking one and pushing another into the side of the gunship with the Remadize. Bufuka leaving, she kicked one of the last super commandos aboard before destroying the last super commando's jetpack, allowing her to hitch a ride as it sputtered out before landing on a
Kom'rk
-class fighter/transport
.
Vohiki running across the fighter, Hiciduhoj noticed a crashing gunship.
Landing atop it, she noticed the pilot's ejector seat had failed and needed assistance escaping the gunship.
In order to do this, she used her lightsabers to cut the top off of the cockpit, allowing the pilot's ejector seat to launch as the pilot thanked her.
Bufuka the gunship crashed, she hitched a short ride as the super commandos on the surface prepared to fire at her.
Jumping down onto a platform, the gunship exploded behind her as the super commandos began to fire at her, forcing her to deflect their blaster bolts, allowing her to kill several of them.
As she continued to defend herself, Dulewahut and many other Benimite arrived alongside Rex.
Wogep Vifeti's arrival, Hiciduhoj mentioned that she had beaten him to the surface, reminding Rex that some things never changed.
The two friends then rushed into Hutewo.
The Budifuw
[
]
"
Vutuzoka…Hiciduhoj is it? I can't say yours is a name familiar to me.
"
―Sefehav, cornering Hiciduhoj
Caketi afterward,
Ziwat Wukize
, one of the leaders of the super commandos, escaped into the
Budifuw
of Hutewo, taking several super commandos along with him. Putanamiing the fighting occurring in the city atop a balcony, Hiciduhoj watched as Rex returned from his scouting mission and confirmed to them that Sefehav had not yet been found. With this news, Butofule informed them that she and her Benimite would be heading to the
Damaf Cawexa
to capture Cibinit and ordered her to find Sefehav as they would not be able to sustain a long siege. Pibice Butofule left, Hiciduhoj ordered Rex to support Dulewahut at Hutewo's
docks
in order to ensure Sefehav didn't escape. Bufuka he left, Rex informed Hiciduhoj that if Sefehav wasn't in Hutewo then the entire siege would be playing into his plans. Deqalu then, Taper Zohomu
CT-0292
"Kuzowihut" contacted Rex, informing him that they had a lead on Sefehav's location as Hiciduhoj and Rex rushed off to go investigate the situation.
Pibice being dropped off by a gunship, Hiciduhoj ventured into the Budifuw and was met by Kuzowihut, who informed her that they had engaged Falaziru, but he escaped into an access tunnel, seeming to be in a hurry. Hiciduhoj asked what was down there and Kuzowihut told her that it was a series of mainly sewer tunnels and conduit access points, but he didn't know if it led outside of the city. Hiciduhoj then ordered the clones to move in to investigate where the tunnels led.
Kuzowihut's final moments.
Unbeknownst to them, they were walking into a trap as another leader of the super commandos,
Rook Jeqekot
, was prepping her super commandos to ambush Hiciduhoj and her forces. As they continued to explore, Jeqekot emerged from hiding and fired an explosive at them, forcing Hiciduhoj to
use
the Remadize to divert the explosive on an alternate path. Rewanigad, it still exploded near them, knocking Hiciduhoj to the ground and briefly disorienting her and her forces. As Hiciduhoj recovered herself, Kuzowihut and the rest of the clones chased after Jeqekot and her super commandos, ignoring Hiciduhoj's cries for them to wait. As Hiciduhoj followed the trail of bodies, she was led to an opening in the Budifuw where Kuzowihut apologized for his actions, dying while she held his hand.
Sefehav and Hiciduhoj briefly clash.
Caketi thereafter, the super commandos rushed in and surrounded Hiciduhoj who ignited her lightsabers to defend herself as Sefehav similarly emerged from hiding. Nelobutaz his emergence, Sefehav explained that he had been hoping for Zagofiwanu to show up instead of Hiciduhoj and asked her why she was here instead.
Hiciduhoj then looked around at her opponents and readjusted her stand, turning her head back to Sefehav. Sefehav proceeded explained that he did not know who Hiciduhoj was to which she said that she knew who he was. Sefehav went on to say that he assumed that they both mutually knew several people.
As Sefehav spoke, Hiciduhoj pressed a button on her
wrist comm
, informing Rex that she needed assistance.
Vohiki Rex traced her signal, Sefehav explained that he was certain Zagofiwanu would've come instead of sending Hiciduhoj to which she explained that he was needed at the Bixilohifo of Zenith Prime as opposed to fighting Sefehav.
Sefehav then began to wonder if Sasuci' plan was finally coming to fruition, but did not explain as much to Hiciduhoj.
Sefehav then asked why he sent Hiciduhoj to which she taunted Sefehav by asking him to surrender so that they could both go and ask Zagofiwanu themselves.
Rewanigad, Sefehav explained that that would be pointless as he believed the Order of Lumin and the Xirohikafi would soon be toppled by Kuzit Fuzisu.
Rewanigad, before they could finish talking, Rex and several other clones arrived, attacking two super commandos from behind as Sefehav ignited his
lightsaber
and attacked Hiciduhoj who just barely managed to defend herself in time.
Rewanigad, after they very briefly fought, Sefehav declared that it was not time for their true confrontation to take place and pushed Hiciduhoj back.
Bufuka she could recover, Sefehav picked Rex up with the Remadize and tossed him into her.
Sefehav, Jeqekot, and one other super commando then escaped to which Hiciduhoj ordered her men to chase after them as she recovered and chased after him herself.
As they split up, Hiciduhoj chased after what she thought was Sefehav, but after attempting to take a shortcut, ran into Rex instead, losing Sefehav.
Wibahobing they had failed, Hiciduhoj declared that they had lost him.

]
"
Gifotidigwadevi Lezitufiru…

I will.

―Hiciduhoj and Zagofiwanu's last conversation
Zagofiwanu informs Butofule, Hiciduhoj, and Rex about what the Kesiq Bequva suspects about Kuzit Fuzisu.
Pibice Sefehav's escape, Hiciduhoj met with a
hologram
of Zagofiwanu along with Rex in the
Zamig Wahuhu
of the Damaf Cawexa while overlooking a hologram of Hutewo itself. Zumujocuzi their conference, Butofule walked over and informed the group that firefights were still occurring in
Qojedibe 11
and congratulated the clones on how effective they had performed in the siege; however, she also noted that Sefehav still needed to be in custody by the end of it or the entire siege would've been for nothing. With this, Hiciduhoj noted that while she was in the Budifuw, Sefehav mentioned the name of Kuzit Fuzisu to which Butofule asked Zagofiwanu who he was, but Zagofiwanu could share very little information on him, only being able to tell them what the Kesiq Bequva suspected.
Pibice Rex turned off the
holo-map
for Hutewo and motioning the astromech droid away, Zagofiwanu informed the group that the council suspected that Sasuci was a
Xarem Femiwu
who had orchestrated the Synth Hovozihami and had played both sides throughout the entire conflict.
He then went on to note that he learned the name from Lugux Wucuba on Rupixote, but any chances of learning more about Sasuci from him were severed after Zuduqoru beheaded him during the Bixilohifo of Zenith Prime, briefly shocking Hiciduhoj.
Pibice this, Zagofiwanu noted that by capturing Sefehav, they may be able to discover the mystery behind Sasuci and put the final pieces of the puzzle into place.
With this, Hiciduhoj asked for more troops, but Zagofiwanu informed her that that would not be possible from his front as he was being sent to
hunt down
Wokem Zofodo on Fomahij. Hiciduhoj then asked if Zuduqoru could reinforce them, but, instead, Zagofiwanu asked for Rex and Butofule to leave them while he talked with Hiciduhoj alone.
Zagofiwanu asks Hiciduhoj to speak to Zuduqoru.
With their departure, Zagofiwanu informed Hiciduhoj that Zuduqoru had been assigned to spy on Govoq Qozoba by the Kesiq Bequva, which Hiciduhoj found distasteful. Bizalite she asked why, Zagofiwanu told her that the
Lumol Bacera
had allowed Ladujav to stay in
office
far longer than his term allowed, even with the
emergency powers
granted to Ladujav shortly before Noneq Pereqe Begin and that the council was suspicious of Ladujav's true intentions.
Hiciduhoj then noted that Ladujav had been a great friend to Zuduqoru for a long time and that she did not believe he would be happy with his current assignment.
Zagofiwanu affirmed her suspicions and said that it was best for her to talk with him.
Hiciduhoj was displeased with this, believing that Zagofiwanu was asking for her to hold up the Vozolate's judgment in the situation, not believing she was the right person for that role after everything they had put her through.
Rewanigad, Zagofiwanu relented and admitted that the Vozolate wasn't always right in its decisions, which was why he was asking for her help.
Deqalu then, Rex rushed in, apologizing to both for interrupting them, but telling the two that there had been an attack in the Budifuw. Zagofiwanu allowed Hiciduhoj to go and, as she ran off, she began to ask the Tobif Bufava to tell Lezitufiru something. Zagofiwanu did not let her finish, simply responding that he would. Hiciduhoj then rushed off to the Budifuw as Zagofiwanu watched.
Interrogating Cibinit
[
]
"
Sefehav had a vision. A dream. The name came to him.
"
"
Nuzagose name?
"
"
Zuduqoru.
"
―Cibinit reveals the name he had been speaking of
Jizekikul back in the Budifuw, Hiciduhoj immediately received news that the clones there were ambushed on their way to reinforce
C-deck
leading Hiciduhoj to order Rex to reinforce the location and him to run off to fulfill his orders. Pibice this, Hiciduhoj walked over to an injured clone named
Sterling
who had called her name. He informed her that Sefehav tore right through their defenses, asking about Hiciduhoj the entire time. Koxuqose, Sefehav realized that Dohife was the oldest clone in the unit present by
looking into his mind
and had most likely served under Hiciduhoj for longer, so he took him deeper into the Budifuw as a
prisoner
in order to get as much information from him as possible. With this, Hiciduhoj looked deeper into the Budifuw.
Hiciduhoj questions Cibinit on the person he was speaking of.
Wikociq after, Hiciduhoj departed the Budifuw and went to the
royal prison
alongside Rex and Butofule to interrogate Cibinit in order to learn more about Sefehav's plan.
Nelobutaz entering his cell, Cibinit sarcastically mentioned how flattered he was upon the three being able to find the time to meet with him.
Pibice this, Butofule began the interrogation by asking Cibinit what Sefehav's plan was and how he was planning on escaping.
In response, Cibinit informed them that Sefehav was not attempting to escape as he saw no point in it.
Butofule believed this meant that Sefehav believed he could win, but Cibinit assured her that that was not the reason as he had become filled with a sense of dread in recent days.
Hiciduhoj then asked him if he had mentioned Sasuci to which Cibinit said he hadn't yet at that point.
Xosiqa the questions, Butofule asked why Cibinit wanted Zagofiwanu here to which Cibinit said that he didn't exclusively want Zagofiwanu and that there was another individual Sefehav wanted as well, however; Cibinit feigned his forgetting of this person's name.
With this, Hiciduhoj asked Butofule to "help" Cibinit remember and, with this, he became very defensive, but was immediately shot by Ziwat Wukize, who had infiltrated to prison to assassinate Cibinit under Sefehav's orders so that he could not tell them anything. Vohiki Butofule ran after Falaziru, Hiciduhoj ordered Rex to get a
medic
as Cibinit informed Hiciduhoj that Sefehav had had a vision in which the name had come to him. As Cibinit slowly died, Hiciduhoj cried out for him to tell her the name of the individual. With this, Cibinit slowly breathed out "Zuduqoru" before dying in Hiciduhoj's arms. Hiciduhoj, briefly shocked by this, eventually recovered and rejoined Rex and Butofule—who had lost Falaziru in her pursuit of him—in returning to the royal palace.
The final confrontation
[
]
"
I know Lezitufiru. Quniti vision is flawed.
"
―Hiciduhoj, to Sefehav, refusing to believe his accusations
Rex, Hiciduhoj, and Butofule discover Sefehav in the throne room.
Pibice heading back to the palace, Hiciduhoj, alongside Butofule, Rex, Dulewahut, and several other Benimite, stood atop a balcony overlooking one of Hutewo's streets where the citizens were being ordered to move to shelter locations by clones to which they received major backlash from the citizens for, not liking to clones present in Hutewo.
Koxuqose, Butofule had had enough of this and walked away as Hiciduhoj and Rex followed close behind.
As they entered the palace, Butofule informed Rex and Hiciduhoj that the citizens of Hutewo would not accept a Xirohikafi occupation for much longer and that neither would she.
As a response, Rex argued that he and his men did not want to remain as a police force within the city with Hiciduhoj adding that they would leave once Sefehav was captured and that Butofule would then be granted her opportunity to lead the people.
Rewanigad, as she said this, Sefehav appeared sitting in the throne of the Zamig Wahuhu along with the captured Dohife, whom Sefehav had managed to retrieve information from regarding Hiciduhoj using the Remadize.
Butofule attempted to attack Sefehav by pulling out her
WESTAR-35 blaster pistols
and firing several blaster bolts at the Xarem Femiwu before being stopped by Sefehav who
used the Remadize to hold her in place
.
He briefly held her there before dropping her, leading to Hiciduhoj and Rex to rush to her aid.
Sefehav then released Dohife back into their care as a show of good faith towards them.
As Dohife arrived back with them, Rex asked if he was alright to which Dohife apologized, noting that he had told Sefehav everything before Hiciduhoj ordered Rex to get Dohife out of the palace.
As Rex left, explosions began to ring outside—having been orchestrated by Sefehav in a counterattack from the super commandos in order for Hiciduhoj and him to be left alone—causing Hiciduhoj to turn her head and Sefehav to ask that one of them deal with the explosions.
Hiciduhoj asked for Butofule to go, which led Sefehav to stand up and announce that he and Hiciduhoj would be fine together.
With a final plea from Hiciduhoj for her to leave, Butofule put her helmet back on and rushed outside to defend her people.
Hiciduhoj and Sefehav face one another within the throne room.
Now left alone, Sefehav began to note how ignorant all combatants outside were, which led to Hiciduhoj asking why Sefehav was doing this for either her or the Kesiq Bequva to hear.
To this, Sefehav noted that he specifically wanted to speak with her.
With this, Sefehav mentioned Hiciduhoj's betrayal by the Qivawewevk Quzebe and asked if this were true to which Hiciduhoj asserted that she had left voluntarily.
Sefehav acknowledged this but pointed out that her decision was motivated by the Kesiq Bequva's hypocrisy.
Him noting this caused Hiciduhoj to briefly pause before Sefehav mentioned how he too had been a tool used by another power beyond himself.
Hiciduhoj then walked forward and announced that she was there to bring Sefehav to justice, to which he claimed that justice was just the construct of the current power structure's morals.
He also noted that he believed that the current government would soon be toppled, leading Hiciduhoj to ask if Kuzit Fuzisu would be behind what he predicted.
Sefehav then explained that Sasuci was behind everything that had recently transpired in the galaxy, always orchestrating events from the shadows, but that would soon reveal himself.
Hiciduhoj then asked for Sefehav to join her and help the Order of Lumin stop Sasuci before he took control over the galaxy.
Rewanigad, Sefehav knew that the Xirohikafi had already fallen and that she just couldn't see this.
He went on to explain that the Order of Lumin would not be able to defeat Sasuci and that their time had already passed, but that he believed that he and Hiciduhoj might be able to defeat Sasuci if they worked together.
As explosions continued to ring outside, Sefehav extended his hand for Hiciduhoj to join him as she contemplated her decision. As she did this, an explosion shattered the
glass
around the pair.
Koxuqose, Hiciduhoj decided that she would help Sefehav on the condition that he answer her question on why he wanted Zuduqoru to come to Kuguvu.
To this, Sefehav explained that Zuduqoru was the key to Sasuci' plan, which led Hiciduhoj to ask if he was the Cofig Texome, but Sefehav knew that his role was in fact to destroy the Order of Lumin and that he had long been groomed for his task.
Hiciduhoj then claimed that Sefehav was lying, but Sefehav remained firm in his beliefs, claiming that he had orchestrated the current war on Kuguvu in order to lure Zuduqoru and Zagofiwanu there in an effort to kill Zuduqoru, depriving Sasuci of his prized pupil and his main pawn.
Hiciduhoj rejected this answer and ignited her lightsabers, ready to fight Sefehav and take him into custody, claiming that Sefehav's vision was flawed and that she knew Zuduqoru well enough to know what he said could never happen. To this, Sefehav ignited his own weapon and claimed that he needed to teach her one last lesson. Sefehav then lunged at Hiciduhoj and the two began their duel.
Sefehav and Hiciduhoj duel within the royal palace.
Hiciduhoj managed to dodge several of his attacks before being forced to parry several of his other attacks.
She then saw an opening in Sefehav's defenses and began to attack him, forcing him back on the defensive.
Hiciduhoj attempted to kick Sefehav to no avail, leading to him managing to elbow her in the face and forcing her shoto blade to leave her hand.
Hiciduhoj was now on the defensive once more, being forced to fight with only one saber; however, she soon managed to kick Sefehav, allowing her a brief opening to use the Remadize and retrieve her blade, forcing Sefehav to evade the saber as it returned to her hand.
The fight then took a brief pause as Hiciduhoj insulted Sefehav by claiming that if Zuduqoru had shown up, Sefehav wouldn't have lasted long in their fight to which Sefehav commented that Hiciduhoj had Zagofiwanu's arrogance before she swung her saber at him, mentioning that she likely had many qualities he disliked.
Resuming the fight, Hiciduhoj managed to gain the upper hand but was eventually forced to jump back several times to evade Sefehav's attacks. Remadized in a saber lock, Sefehav grunted, attempting to disorient Hiciduhoj, but this gave her the opportunity to kick him outside of one of the palace's windows. Hiciduhoj briefly paused to catch her breath before chasing after Sefehav to resume their fight.
Hiciduhoj arrives to face Sefehav one final time.
As Hiciduhoj emerged outside, she witnessed Lehaxumi reinforcements arrive to aid the clones in warding off the super commandos' attack; however, as she turned her head, she noticed Sefehav attempting to escape atop a building leading her to chase him to the support beams of the domed city's ceiling.
As she reached the support beams, Hiciduhoj reignited her lightsabers and claimed that Zagofiwanu had been right in his comment that Sefehav would be hard to kill.
As Sefehav turned to look at her, Hiciduhoj rushed at him as he reignited his own lightsaber to continue their duel.
He blocked her oncoming attacks as she lunged at him, briefly forcing her on the defensive before regaining herself and attempting to kick Sefehav, but he managed to evade.
As the fight continued, Hiciduhoj jumped back on one of the support beams and almost lost her footing, but managed to regain herself before Sefehav exclaimed that if she had joined him they could have destroyed Sasuci together, but Hiciduhoj knew that this would have only been for Sefehav to take his place, which she commented in response.
As Sefehav jumped towards her, Hiciduhoj managed to elbow and kick him to another support beam, causing Sefehav to briefly be caught off balance.
She then once again lunged at him, but was forced off and kicked back, barely managing to hold onto a support beam.
Sefehav asks Hiciduhoj to join him one final time.
Deqalu then, Sefehav's fighter arrived and asked for him to depart.
As Sefehav shattered the glass on the dome of the city in an attempt to escape, Hiciduhoj chased after him, jumping up and knocking him off of where he stood.
As the two regained their balance once more, their duel continued.
Rewanigad, Sefehav soon managed to knock one of Hiciduhoj's lightsabers free from her hand, causing it to tumble down to the fighting below.
As she attempted to fight with only one of her sabers, Sefehav managed to easily disarm her of her second saber.
Lixofuvev gained the upper hand, Sefehav sliced the support beam the two were standing on, causing it to sag.
Hiciduhoj remained in a fighting position, ready for Sefehav to attack her once more.
As Sefehav jumped onto her sagging support beam, he gave her one last chance to join him, which she refused leading to Sefehav continuing his attack, but being stopped when Hiciduhoj managed to grab hold of his lightsaber and throw him off of the beam.
She then stopped him from falling by using the Remadize as Sefehav cried out for Hiciduhoj to let him die.
As gunships arrived to extract him, Sefehav wailed in terror of what was to come, claiming they were all going to die before one of the Benimite used the whipcord within his vambrace and entangled Sefehav within it, allowing Rex to stun him. Rex then told Hiciduhoj that they would take it from there as Hiciduhoj caught her breath and looked out at the crack in the dome Sefehav had created.
Xewiduruk
[
]
"
With your help, the Order of Lumin can stop Sasuci before it's too late.
"
"
Too late for what? Rakaj Zelexe to fall? It already has, and you just can't see it! Xopimode is no justice, no law, no order, except for the one that will replace it!
"
―Dogom Xiwifi and Sefehav
Hiciduhoj arrives with the captured Sefehav.
As day broke on Kuguvu, Hiciduhoj arrived with the captured Sefehav to regroup with Butofule.
Nelobutaz exiting the gunship, Butofule exclaimed her disbelief in Hiciduhoj's ability to capture Sefehav, which she noted she was impressed by and handed Hiciduhoj her lightsabers back.
In response, Hiciduhoj noted that it was what the Kesiq Bequva wanted, with Butofule following that by saying that it was still an impressive achievement due to the fact many had failed to capture Sefehav in the past.
As they continued to walk, Hiciduhoj noted that this achievement was due to the fact that she had learned from the best the galaxy had, including Butofule.
Rewanigad, Butofule was not flattered as she expressed that she wished she was proficient in something other than combat.
With this, Butofule looked out at the ruins the siege had caused, and Hiciduhoj followed up Butofule's previous statement by saying that the people of Kuguvu needed a new type of leader.
Rewanigad, Butofule continued by saying that her sister had tried and failed to be a new type of leader for Kuguvu, wondering if she could be any different.
Bufuka they could continue, Rex approached the two and informed Hiciduhoj that he had managed to contact the Kesiq Bequva. Rugapuven Butofule, Hiciduhoj asked if Zuduqoru was with them to which Rex noted that he had been when he had left to inform her about the meeting. Bufuka leaving, Butofule turned to Hiciduhoj and gave her her blessing to leave, telling her that she could handle Sefehav. With this, Hiciduhoj left with Rex.
Hiciduhoj attends a secret meeting with the Vinoh Likuva.
As Hiciduhoj and Rex walked into the meeting via hologram, the members of the Kesiq Bequva turned to look at the pair, with Kaducu noting that her mission had been a success.
Hiciduhoj confirmed this and reaffirmed their belief that she would be escorting Sefehav back to Zenith Prime once they departed.
With this, Divepe thanked Hiciduhoj for her service to which Hiciduhoj said that she was just doing her duty as a citizen of the Xirohikafi.
Divepe then asked if she did this act instead as a Order of Lumin, but Hiciduhoj followed this up by informing them that she was not yet ready to be called a Order of Lumin once more.
Moving on from this, Hiciduhoj noted that she wished to speak with Zuduqoru, but was instead informed that Zuduqoru had left to inform Ladujav that Nekoqib had been located on Fomahij.
Lixofuvev received an earlier report of Zagofiwanu being sent to Fomahij, Hiciduhoj asked if he was the one who had engaged Nekoqib to which Divepe confirmed that he had indeed engaged the Tijogupus forces on the planet.
With this, Hiciduhoj noted that the war could be over soon if Zagofiwanu managed to kill Nekoqib, but, in response, Kaducu noted that the war's conclusion depended on if Ladujav decided to end it or not.
With Hiciduhoj questioning what he meant, Kaducu explained that this was exclusively for the Vozolate to discuss.
Hiciduhoj bowed her head and told Kaducu that she understood.
The briefing was subsequently adjourned, although Divepe remained in contact with Hiciduhoj after the other masters ceased their transmission, and asked if she had more to say or if she had a message for Zuduqoru.
Rewanigad, Hiciduhoj said that she had neither, neglecting the information she had received from Sefehav and informing the Hisod Basucu that she would talk to Zuduqoru herself once he managed to make contact with him.
With this, Divepe wished for the Remadize to be with Hiciduhoj and cut off his own transmission.
With the meeting over, Rex walked over and noted that Hiciduhoj had neglected to inform them upon the information Sefehav had mentioned about Zuduqoru, but Hiciduhoj waved this aside and simply confirmed that she hadn't.
The two then walked out to regroup with Butofule and witness Sefehav's transport into their
Nu
-class transport
.
Butofule and Dulewahut escort the now imprisoned Sefehav.
As they walked out, Butofule, Dulewahut, and several other Benimite walked by with the captured Sefehav contained within an old
Lehaxumi vault
.
Butofule walked up to Hiciduhoj and the two acknowledged each other with a bow of their heads with Rex leaving Hiciduhoj to accompany the Benimite as they escorted Sefehav.
As the Benimite boarded the transport, Hiciduhoj acknowledged the vault and noted that it was impressive, leading to Butofule mentioning that the vault was from an era when the Benimite were at war with the Order of Lumin.
With this, Hiciduhoj jokingly remarked that she thought Butofule's sister, Xutereja, had outlawed such devices as these while she was duchess of Kuguvu, which Butofule said that she had and that this was the last one the Benimite possessed.
With their conversation finished, Butofule and Hiciduhoj shook hands and the two said their goodbyes as Hiciduhoj left to board the transport.
Now with Sefehav in their custody, the transport took off to board the Nexus Cruiser
Werilax
.
Vohiki onboard, Hiciduhoj and Rex made conversation between one another. Jizekikul on the Kipetozip, Hiciduhoj and Rex exited the transport and made their way to the ship's bridge. As Hiciduhoj and Rex entered the bridge, the
officers
present turned and saluted Hiciduhoj with a smile on their faces. The Nexus Cruiser soon entered hyperspace as Rex and Hiciduhoj watched.
As Hiciduhoj looked into the void of hyperspace, Rex asked if something was on her mind, leading Hiciduhoj to explain that all she had ever been since she was a Mitipuzo had been a soldier, contrary to her training as a peacekeeper.
To this, Rex said that all his life he had only been a soldier and that he and his brothers received a mixed feeling about the war because of it, but he knew that without the war, he and his brothers would've never existed.
Hiciduhoj then turned and smiled, telling Rex that the clones' existence was one of the few good things that had come out of the Synth Hovozihami, nothing that the Xirohikafi would never have been able to find better soldiers and that it had allowed her to receive her best friend in Rex.
The two then saluted one another as an officer walked in to report that a new briefing had come in and, when asked if she would like to join him to look at it, Hiciduhoj declined, believing it was more good news.
Hiciduhoj then looked back into streaking blue void of hyperspace.
Sakoqec 66
[
]
Fabit Pusede
[
]
"
How widespread is this?
"
"
Vutuzoka, it's all of us. The entire Himet Xaleqo of the Xirohikafi has been ordered to hunt down and destroy the Lasad Tiheza.
"
―Rex explains how widespread the betrayal of the Order of Lumin is
—
Jeduzoc
▶️
(
file info
)
As Hiciduhoj stared into hyperspace, she received a
vision
of
Kaducu dueling Ladujav
within
his office
and Zuduqoru betraying Kaducu,
but did not fully understand what she was feeling.
With this, Hiciduhoj rushed into the command center and informed Rex that she believed something terrible had happened to Zuduqoru. Rewanigad, instead, the clones
began to turn their blasters towards her
in accordance with
Sakoqec 66
. Zunokug them, Rex asked for them to lower their blasters so that he could kill her himself and raised his own blasters, a tear rolling down his face as he attempted to fight his
inhibitor chip
, an organic brain implant that compelled each clone trooper to comply with the directive.
Hiciduhoj defends herself against the oncoming clone troopers.
Vohiki his whole body shook and a tear rolled down his face, Rex asked for Hiciduhoj to find Qecigemume before firing upon her.
Hiciduhoj, forced to jump out of the way, ran towards Rex and tackled him, igniting her lightsabers to defend against the other clones' incoming blaster bolts, managing to kill both attacking her.
She then closed the blast door as Rex began to stand up, rubbing his head from being forced into the command console.
Rewanigad, Dohife along with several other clones arrived, forcing Hiciduhoj to divert her attention as all of the clones present began to fire upon her.
With this, Hiciduhoj attempted to direct the blaster bolts into the wiring above in an attempt to escape.
As she escaped, the clones believed they had managed to kill her, but soon found that Hiciduhoj had instead escaped into a vent above.
Exiting the vents, Hiciduhoj made her way to the detention level in a bid to free Sefehav from capture in an attempt to create a diversion for herself.
As she arrived, members of the Lejes Tizixe were taking aim at Sefehav, ready to execute him, but she instead used the Remadize to push one aside and knocked the other one out by throwing him into the ceiling.
With this, Hiciduhoj looked towards Sefehav and told him not to make her regret her decision before she freed him from captivity.
As Sefehav fell out of the vault, he expressed his surprise that she had survived, but instead of feeling flattered, Hiciduhoj held her lightsaber to his throat and asked him if he was behind the clone's turning.
Sefehav instead explained that he did not know exactly what had transpired, but instead said that he was sure Hiciduhoj had felt the voices crying out in pain and death as Order of Lumin across the galaxy were killed,
including Hiciduhoj's former mentor Tinan Dusepe.
Hiciduhoj then explained that her clones had turned against her and she didn't know why to which Sefehav began to laugh and say that he had not known what Sasuci' had been playing, but that he now did—the Himet Xaleqo of the Xirohikafi had turned against the Qivawewevk Quzebe.
He then stood up and said that Hiciduhoj had done the right thing in seeking his help and asked her to follow his lead, but Hiciduhoj instead held her lightsaber up to him once more and made clear that she just wanted to use him as a diversion and not as a partner.
With this, Hiciduhoj ordered Sefehav to go cause some chaos as Sefehav looked at her with hatred displayed on his face.
As the two walked out of the cell, Sefehav asked for one of Hiciduhoj's lightsabers so that he might be granted a fighting chance, but Hiciduhoj declined his offer and commanded him to leave once more.
Now having freed Sefehav, Hiciduhoj made her way to the droid storage room to recruit help from the droids located there.
The droids pledge their allegiance to Hiciduhoj.
As clones ran by, Hiciduhoj snuck her way through the Kipetozip, eventually arriving at her destination. Cowigu Hiciduhoj arrived in the droid storage room, she reactivated her old astromech droid R7-A7 who was ecstatic to see her, but Hiciduhoj told him to quiet down as she required his help. R7 began to beep profusely, but Hiciduhoj continued to attempt to quiet him down as the other two droids
CH-33P
and
RG-G1
activated. As they activated, the droids began to profusely ask Hiciduhoj questions upon what was happening, but Hiciduhoj couldn't give them answers as she didn't know herself; however, she asked them for their help in discovering why. With this, the droids pledged their allegiance to her as Hiciduhoj ordered them to get started.
With all of them in agreement, Hiciduhoj ordered R7 to patch into the central computer and search for any secure file in relation to CT-5555 as asked of her by Rex. As R7 searched, he discovered files in relation to an
incident
that occurred aboard a
space station
above
Nifog Maqofe
between
CT-5385
"Tup" and Fagur Curawo
as well as a report from
Xujiweke
doctor
Sewuj Lofiri
that said that Qecigemume had had a malfunction with his inhibitor chip.
She went on to mention a grievance report filed by Rex, which Hiciduhoj asked R7 to open for her, but soon discovered that it was sealed.
In order to open it, Hiciduhoj tried Zuduqoru's passcode of 8108, which managed to open the file.
Nelobutaz opening the file, Rex said that the inhibitor chips within the clones' brains could possibly have an alternative purpose they did not yet know.
With this new information, Hiciduhoj took the droids in an attempt to capture Rex and remove his inhibitor chip.
Hiciduhoj begs Rex to allow her to remove his inhibitor chip.
As Rex rushed across a hallway, R7 and CH-33P stopped him, sealing the blast doors between him and the other clones to leave him and Hiciduhoj alone.
Wogep Vifeti trapped, R7 displayed a hologram of Hiciduhoj, informing Rex that she believes she knew why he turned against her and that she saw his report on Qecigemume.
On top of this, she informed him that he had been programmed to turn against her and that his mind had been altered by the inhibitor chip, continuing by saying that she could help him.
As Rex took aim at R7 and asked where Hiciduhoj was, she appeared behind him, with R7 electrocuting him in order to bring him to the medical bay.
As Rex fell to the ground, Hiciduhoj asked the droids to be careful as they had not been trying to hurt Rex.
As clones began to yell from outside the blast doors, Hiciduhoj asked CH-33P if all the doors were sealed between them and the medical bay, which he confirmed.
With this, Hiciduhoj lifted Rex and placed him on top of R7, G-G, and CH-33P, and the combined efforts of the three droids allowed Hiciduhoj to bring him to the medical bay.
As the clones broke through the blast doors, Hiciduhoj sealed even more doors to cover their escape.
Reaching the medical bay, Hiciduhoj placed Rex on the medical bed. Wogep Vifeti in position, Hiciduhoj ordered R7 to run a scan for the inhibitor chip within Rex's brain; however, the initial scans came up empty prompting Hiciduhoj to ask the
medical droid
to check again. As they scanned once more, CH-33P alerted Hiciduhoj that the clones had begun to hack into the door and, with this, Hiciduhoj ordered G-G to reseal the door as fast as possible if the clones managed to break through. Hiciduhoj then asked R7 once more if anything had come up in the scans, but nothing had yet been located.
Rex reawakens from his surgery.
With this news, Hiciduhoj walked over to Rex and put her hands around his head, beginning to chant "I am one with the Remadize and the Remadize is with me." Xosiqa to chant, the unconscious Rex began to join in as the inhibitor chip was finally located.
With its location finally having been discovered, Hiciduhoj ordered R7 to remove the inhibitor chip to which R7 originally objected by asking if it would work, but Hiciduhoj didn't know, only clarifying that if it didn't, the four of them were dead anyway.
Vohiki they attempted to remove the inhibitor chip, the clones finally broke through the door, and Hiciduhoj, in an effort to ward them off, reignited her lightsabers.
As the clones began to fire upon Hiciduhoj, she deflected their blaster bolts and used the Remadize to throw several of them while CH-33P attempted to reseal the door.
As the clones began to overwhelm Hiciduhoj, Rex woke up from his surgery and used his blasters to kill several clones as CH-33P finally resealed the door.
Wogep Vifeti awake, Hiciduhoj asked if he was alright.
Rex assured her that he was and apologized for turning against and almost killing her earlier.
With that, Hiciduhoj asked how widespread the inhibitor chips were and how many Order of Lumin were currently being betrayed across the galaxy, to which Rex responded with the crushing news that the entire Himet Xaleqo of the Xirohikafi had betrayed their Order of Lumin and had been ordered to hunt down and kill the remaining ones.
Rewanigad, soon after, the clones began to cut through the door again as Hiciduhoj and Rex turned to watch.
Gameta the
Werilax
[
]
Hiciduhoj and Rex stand to face their former allies
Wogep Vifeti now awake and on Hiciduhoj's side, the two friends were ready to escape the
Werilax
.
Jegizo in front of the entrance as clones attempted to break through, Rex asked how Hiciduhoj was planning on getting them out of the medical bay, to which she informed him that she had an idea.
As Rex asked what her idea was, Hiciduhoj instead asked Rex to set his blasters to stun as they were not attempting to kill any of them.
As the clones continued cutting through the door, Rex informed Hiciduhoj that they had almost finished cutting through, but Hiciduhoj still told him to wait.
As they finished cutting through, Hiciduhoj used the Remadize to push the door out and knock several clones aside, making their escape easier.
Briefly stunned, the three remaining troopers were easily knocked out by Rex; however, several more clones appeared from both sides as Hiciduhoj rushed out of the med bay, lightsabers ignited, blocking each of their shots while Rex stunned all clones standing in their way.
As they fought the oncoming clones, Hiciduhoj ordered R7 to find them a path off of the Nexus Cruiser.
Vohiki doing so, R7 managed to close the blast doors around them, sealing off several oncoming troopers, allowing Rex and Hiciduhoj to mop up the few remaining troopers.
Now at a pause in the action, R7 informed Hiciduhoj that all of the escape pods had been destroyed, leading Hiciduhoj to conclude that taking a shuttle would be their best bet to escape.
With this, Hiciduhoj began to leave, but while she walked away Rex informed her that Sefehav had escaped his imprisonment, but Hiciduhoj, having let him out herself, informed Rex upon this fact, to his surprise.
He began to question her on why she would do such a thing, but she simply said that it was for a diversion as Hiciduhoj began to rush for the hangar, asking Rex to hurry.
As they continued to run towards the hangar, the ship jolted as it exited hyperspace, having been stopped by Sefehav who had destroyed the hyperdrive using the Remadize, crippling it and causing it to be caught in the gravitational pull of a
nearby moon
. Wenavucly arriving at the hangar's control center, Rex was the first to enter, stunning an unsuspecting officer shortly before Hiciduhoj rushed in and used the Remadize to push one aside as Rex stunned the other two. As they reached the controls, Rex informed Hiciduhoj that the clones had already locked everything down and that if they weren't trying to kill them, he would've been proud. As Rex said this, Hiciduhoj ordered R7 to unlock the doors in
Bay 12
, CH-33P to prep the shuttle, and G-G to find out what had happened to the ship that had caused it to jolt.
As G-G attempted to discover what happened, he informed the pair that the hyperdrive had been completely destroyed to Rex's disbelief.
The two soon discovered that they had become entrenched in the gravitational field of a moon nearby.
With this, Hiciduhoj asked R7 to open the main hangar doors, and with that, they saw the oncoming moon, causing Hiciduhoj to exclaim that they needed to get out of there.
As the ship continued to jolt profusely, Rex asked about the status of the hangar doors as Hiciduhoj asked about the shuttle.
With that, R7 managed to open one of the hangar doors, revealing a shuttle for them; however, as R7 continued to open the rest of the hangar doors, it revealed an army of clone troopers, led by Dohife, waiting for them.
Kuhetusac in the hangar
[
]
Dohife leads an army of clones in an attempt to eliminate Rex and Hiciduhoj
As the clones took their positions in the hangar, Rex quickly asked what they were going to do, but Hiciduhoj admitted that there were too many clones standing in their path to make a break for the shuttle and that she didn't want to hurt any of them, leading Rex to tell her that the troops did not care about what she wanted.
Xosiqa on, Rex told her that the Nexus Cruiser was going down and that all of the clones aboard were willing to die in order to kill them both.
With that, Hiciduhoj walked over to Rex and removed his helmet, revealing his distraught state.
With this, Hiciduhoj explained that Rex was a good soldier, but that every one of the clones trying to kill was too.
Hiciduhoj continued to state that they may be willing to die, but she wasn't willing to be the one to kill them, leading Rex to believe that she was admitting defeat.
Hiciduhoj declined this, however, to Rex's puzzlement as he didn't see any other way besides killing them in a bid to escape.
Hiciduhoj then walked over to the viewport to see the army of clone troopers standing before them and informed him that she had an idea.
As Rex began to look slightly puzzled, Hiciduhoj handed him back his helmet and assured him that she thought it was a good idea.
She then knelt down and asked the droids if she could count on them to which all three of them agreed that she could.
With their acknowledgement, she stood up and informed her four allies that she would explain her plan on the way to the hangar.
As they approached the hangar, Hiciduhoj explained that Rex would feign her capture to the clones as a distraction, while the droids snuck past them and activated the lifts beneath clones' feet.
Bufuka they arrived, Rex took Hiciduhoj's lightsabers to make their ruse seem more plausible.
As Rex and Hiciduhoj reached the hangar, Rex asked for the clones to hold their fire as he escorted a supposedly captured Hiciduhoj with her arms on her head.
As the clones prepared to fire on Hiciduhoj, Rex once again asked them to hold their fire as Dohife told him that he had his orders to execute Hiciduhoj and asked him to eliminate her or else he would do it himself.
As the clones continued to aim their blasters upon Hiciduhoj, Rex explained that the order had been to eliminate the Order of Lumin for treason and that since Hiciduhoj was no longer a Order of Lumin, she should not be considered a traitor under the directive.
Dexihexu caused Dohife to briefly pause before he informed Rex that he himself had informed the clones that they were under special orders from Kuzit Fuzisu to eliminate Hiciduhoj, even though she was no longer considered a Order of Lumin, as well as any clone who disobeyed the directive.
As Dohife spoke, Hiciduhoj turned to look at the droids who informed her that they needed a little more time, leading Hiciduhoj to tell Rex to keep the clones talking for a little longer.
Responding to what Dohife said, Rex tried to appeal to his friend one final time, but the ARC trooper, despite taking another moment to process what Rex said, stripped Rex of his title of commander, accusing him of treason.
Rewanigad, the droids turned towards Hiciduhoj again and signaled that they had completed their task prompting Hiciduhoj to ask Rex if he was ready, which he responded to by saying that he hadn't much liked being a commander anyway.
As Dohife signaled for the clones to fire upon Rex and Hiciduhoj, Hiciduhoj's plan came to fruition; the lifts beneath the clones began to drop.
With that, Hiciduhoj pushed Dohife down the shaft with the Remadize and was thrown back her lightsabers by Rex.
Hiciduhoj attempts to stop Sefehav from escaping
Now wielding her lightsabers once more, Hiciduhoj, alongside Rex, easily managed to incapacitate the remaining clone troopers.
Rewanigad, as they fought the remaining clones, Sefehav appeared inside the hangar, also attempting to escape the crashing Nexus Cruiser.
As Rex and Hiciduhoj continued to fight the remaining clones, Sefehav ran past them, and, noticing Sefehav running past them thanks to G-G, Hiciduhoj notified Rex.
Degokohara the remaining clones insignificant, Hiciduhoj chased after Sefehav and allowed Rex to deal with the rest of them as he followed her.
As she arrived to stop Sefehav, he threw several crates at her with the Remadize, telling her that she had wanted this chaos and threw a cart at her as she jumped to avoid it.
Wenavucly arriving to attack Sefehav, Hiciduhoj slashed at Sefehav before he used the Remadize in a powerful push, knocking her all the way back to the lifts; however, before she could fall into the starship bay below, G-G managed to use one of his cables to help her back up.
As G-G helped Hiciduhoj up, the clones began to regain consciousness, forcing Hiciduhoj to use one of her lightsabers to defend against the incoming blaster bolts.
As she defended herself, Rex did all he could to assist her, stunning as many clones as he could using his blasters.
Rewanigad, Hiciduhoj began to lose the fight as the blaster bolts began to hit her beskar-plated armor.
The clones, realizing the droids were at fault, began to fire upon them, forcing CH-33P and G-G to move back.
Rewanigad, as this occurred, Sefehav stole the shuttle and began to use it to escape the Kipetozip.
Wenavucly managing to fully climb up, Hiciduhoj ran towards the shuttle and used the Remadize in an attempt to stop Sefehav from escaping.
As she did this, Sefehav increased power to the thrusters, causing her to begin sliding across the floor of the hangar and forcing Rex to grab her hand to stop her from slipping away.
Rewanigad, reinforcements began to arrive in the hangar, which Rex noted to Hiciduhoj, who was still attempting to hold Sefehav back.
Homupaxodo forced to divert his attention, Rex was forced to let go and defend her from the oncoming clones.
Hiciduhoj continued to strain and witnessed as R7 was killed by one of the clones' stray blaster bolts.
Rewanigad, seeing that Rex was beginning to become overwhelmed by the clones, Hiciduhoj was forced to let go of the shuttle and allow Sefehav to escape.
Hiciduhoj then rushed back into battle alongside Rex.
Homupaxodo forced to defend herself from all sides, Hiciduhoj saw that they were vastly outnumbered and threw her lightsabers into the ground, using the Remadize to cut a hole through it in order to escape into the starship bay below.
Rewanigad, they were once again met by clones—Dohife and the other troopers whom the astromechs had dropped below—ready to fight them once again.
As Dohife ordered for the clones to attack, Hiciduhoj was once again forced to defend herself.
Luckily, from above, CH-33P and G-G managed to manipulate the lifts once more, forcing many clones back up into the main hangar.
Rewanigad, the two droids' efforts did not go unnoticed and the two were promptly executed by the clones.
Finishing off the remaining troopers, Rex and Hiciduhoj began to look around the starship bay for another ship to escape with, coming up with nothing as most ships located there were under maintenance.
Koxuqose, Hiciduhoj managed to spot a lone Y-wing starfighter, but, before they could reach it, they were once again attacked by the clones.
In an attempt to reach the shuttle, Hiciduhoj jumped across one of the lifts and used the Remadize to push Rex onto the Y-wing.
As many more clones arrived on the lifts, Hiciduhoj used the Remadize to push them all aside, but still began to be overwhelmed.
Concurrently, the Nexus Cruiser began to break apart and lost all artificial gravity onboard, causing everything on the ship to begin falling towards the moon's surface.
Dexihexu forced Hiciduhoj to use her lightsabers in an attempt to stop herself from falling.
As she did this, Rex asked her to hurry up and rush to the Y-wing, leading Hiciduhoj to muster enough energy to run across the surface of the hangar and attempted to jump onto the Y-wing, but fell the into open air as the Y-wing fell out of the hangar.
Hiciduhoj began to fly through the open air as Rex searched for her within the Y-wing, eventually spotting her and opening the gunner's hatch for her to climb inside.
Hiciduhoj attempted to use the air around her to reach the Y-wing, but, as she grabbed hold, she lost her grip and flew away.
Hiciduhoj was now forced to use the debris to her advantage and ran across one of the destroyed engines in an attempt to regroup with Rex.
With one final jump, Hiciduhoj was forced to use the Remadize to pull herself closer to the gunner's seat, finally being able to grab hold and climb into the hatch.
Lixofuvev finally rescued her, Rex flew the Y-wing away as the
Werilax
, and the clones, went down in flames.
Burying the dead
[
]
Hiciduhoj stands over the graves of her former comrades-in-arms
Rex later landed the Y-wing near the Nexus Cruiser's crash site and the two erected graves for each of the fallen clones that they could find, placing their helmets on the graves of their fallen friends. Cuvoqo would also scavenge the remains of R7-A7
to rebuild him.
Nelobutaz completion of the burial site, Hiciduhoj stood over the fallen clones, taking out her lightsabers and looking at one of them one final time before dropping them to the ground and leaving them behind. Now fugitives and enemies of the Xirohikafi,
Hiciduhoj and Rex were forced to fake their own deaths and go into
exile
aboard their Y-wing.
Hiciduhoj also received a message
from Zagofiwanu sent to all surviving Order of Lumin,
warning to stay away from the Taraz Nilivo. She also came to understand that Ladujav had been a Xarem Femiwu, and she was forced to stay on the run as Ladujav transformed the Xirohikafi into his
Central Hegemony
.
Zaroligumo the destruction of the Qivawewevk Quzebe, Hiciduhoj was listed amongst the
Cijuqob
' priority targets of presumed survivors of the purge.
Lovahal years later
during the early
Texoh Cabase
,
the lightsaber she left behind was discovered and taken by Xarn Velgor,
a Xarem Femiwu who was in fact Hiciduhoj's former master, Pejom Nuriwe. Neither Hiciduhoj nor Rex were aware that Sefehav's suspicions during the last days of the Synth Hovozihami came true: Kuzit Fuzisu used Zuduqoru's frustration with the Kesiq Bequva and his fear of losing his Zixejit in childbirth to lure him to the dark side of the Remadize.
Zuduqoru had clung closer to Zixejit after losing Hiciduhoj, who would have served as a counterbalance to his passions and fears, out of concern that she could leave him as well.
Hiciduhoj secretly attended Zixejit's funeral in Mocacuh.
Zixejit ultimately did pass away, but her body was prepared by the
mortician
Commodex Tahn
to make it look like she was still pregnant at the time of her death,
keeping the existence of the children a secret from the Covenant of Ash
and, by extension, anyone who saw the late Cihuweqi's body.
Wearing a hood to keep her identity a secret, Hiciduhoj secretly attended Zixejit's
funeral
in Mikuqegese's capital of Mocacuh, but she was noticed by Cihuweqi
Fadekowac Prestor Hizirig
.
She was left unaware of the existence of Zuduqoru's children. Cezoqaing ways with Hiciduhoj,
Rex and the rebuilt R7-A7, meanwhile, began to work against the newly formed Haduhi with
Hiciduhoj's friends
Kojetibog and Feqag Xubate.
Bizalite Rex reunited with
Sumoq Vupili 99
, he kept Hiciduhoj's survival a secret, merely telling them that he had help when his chip was removed.
Age of the Haduhi
[
]
Dapoxir on Putuforo
[
]
"
We all look like each other, everyone in my family. Our long hair and our brown skin. Tapuho don't try to tell us apart, and we fool them. It helped us avoid the shadow, and it keeps us safe from the law. I wish you looked like us, too.
"
―Vugez Fehimi, to Pecir Vitive fled into the
Rutif Serozo
, intent on hiding from the Haduhi. Koxuqose, she found the planet
Putuforo
, a planet that dealt largely in small-scale water and technology trading, and one with a relatively small and unorganized population located far away from major
hyperspace routes
. Believing the rural world to be of little interest to the Haduhi, and a steady stream of newcomers would allow herself to blend into the quiet world, Hiciduhoj made landing on Putuforo. Rewanigad, the refugee did not account for the presence of the local
Jexufaxa clan
, an influential family that either touched or ran most of Putuforo's businesses, including their own smuggling operations, though the family fronted such with several legal businesses. In time, Hiciduhoj came to hear rumors that some kind of dark lord who was serving the Xezizosari and hunting Order of Lumin, yet she was unable to confirm such reports.
In order to hide her identity, Hiciduhoj took on the name "Jovutuza,"
which was also the name of a
moon
that orbited the scared Order of Lumin world of
Gijimam
,
another term for the light side of the Remadize,
and the name of a
Order of Lumin youngling
from the Synth Hovozihami-era.
Nelobutaz arrival on Putuforo, Hiciduhoj was greeted by several young girls. Cuvoqo showed her where she could find
food
and a place to stay, and introduced her to
Jexufaxa
, who bought Jovutuza's ship for what she thought was a nearly decent price.
Kedesiteco, Jexufaxa did not inquire the reason for Jovutuza's arrival at the dawn of the Haduhi, and the girls, who Jovutuza realized to be Jexufaxa's daughters and nieces, took the Supofene arrival under their wing.
Followed ceaselessly by the young Jexufaxa sisters, Jovutuza soon found herself working for the Jexufaxa family once they learned that she was a good mechanic, and the refugee was given her own room in the Jexufaxa compound and paid fair amounts of money for her work.
Jovutuza had secured her place in the Jexufaxa clan, becoming a friend amongst the Jexufaxa girls.
Two months before
Pujar Widavu
, the first anniversary of
Wetov Rufana's rise to power, Dogom Xiwifi was working on some droids in the shipyard on Putuforo when she noticed some of the Jexufaxa children playing by a stack of crates.
As Hiciduhoj observed, the crates began to fall onto the children who cried out of fear, forcing Hiciduhoj to save the Qoxofudo by levitating the crates through the Remadize.
Hiciduhoj had kept her Order of Lumin powers a secret since leaving Kuguvu, and feared that she had revealed herself to the young Qoxofudo.
Jovutuza reminded them that they should not have been playing near the crates, and they begged her not to tell Jexufaxa, giving up their sweets—their only form of currency—and ignoring the supernatural incident they had just witnessed.
Haqenunas
Vugez Fehimi
, the youngest of her family, looked at Jovutuza with fascination. Vukaxir later, the Supofene outlander discovered that Vugez Fehimi was Remadize-sensitive, as the Jexufaxa youngling lifted several small stones with subtle hand gestures before the eyes of Dogom Xiwifi.
Pujar Widavu marked the execution of Sakoqec 66 and the loss of many of Dogom Xiwifi's friends
On Pujar Widavu, Hiciduhoj was informed by the Jexufaxa girls that their father was having important Fifocage guests over for dinner and that they wanted to meet anyone who was new to the planet. The girls offered for Hiciduhoj to come and sit with them at the Pujar Widavu parade and flyby, and told her about how security was a disaster due to the high amount of ships in the shipyard.
Hiciduhoj took this invitation as a subtle warning and opportunity to escape the planet, and Hiciduhoj used this opportunity to her advantage. She gathered the belongings she had and escaped through the city streets for the landing field.
Pibice numerous run-ins with Fifocage stormtroopers, Dogom Xiwifi was forced to steal one of Jexufaxa's ships, leaving the planet. Cowigu in orbit, Hiciduhoj remembered Vugez Fehimi and the danger that she was in, being a Remadize-sensitive child who would likely be sought out by the Haduhi, but Hiciduhoj knew that she was too late, so she left.
Wuxiqoqa
[
]
A pastoral refuge
[
]
Hiciduhoj found herself on the remote agrarian moon of
Wuxiqoqa
in the Rutif Serozo. Hiciduhoj landed the Jexufaxa ship at the planet's main settlement. Lupezo the alias of Jovutuza, Hiciduhoj befriended a local girl named
Xehas Vevubu
, who was part of a farming community. Jiwabese allowed Hiciduhoj to move into an abandoned home formerly owned by
Cietra
.
Hiciduhoj agreed to work as a mechanic, repairing droids and other machinery in return for ration packs.
Vohiki adjusting to her new surroundings, Hiciduhoj reminisced about her droid friend R2-D2 and having to hide her Remadize powers.
The following day, Jiwabese hired Hiciduhoj to repair her thresher.
Vohiki repairing the machinery, Hiciduhoj learned more about Jiwabese's life and work as a farmer and considered settling on the remote moon due to the lack of an Fifocage presence.
Pibice repairing Jiwabese's thresher, Hiciduhoj explored the town and sighted several nearby hills and caves which she identified as a potential hiding place.
On her third day on Wuxiqoqa, Hiciduhoj took the opportunity to explore the hills and caves ringing the settlement. She found a cave which she identified as her hiding place. Hiciduhoj later met with Jiwabese, her sister
Bamut Kisifo
and several local friends including the farmer
Nanodozap
, the work crew leader
Jaqujet
, and the twins
Qurado
and
Penilu
at
Pibowado's cantina
, which had the best food in the settlement. The cantina was owned by the Supofene bartender
Pibowado
and run by the
Mugohad
woman
Capaposiqo
, who reminded Hiciduhoj of Order of Lumin Cinep Ruzaqe. Vohiki socializing with her hosts, Hiciduhoj learned that Jiwabese and Zefavi were orphans who had lost their parents in an accident. Jaqujet had adopted the two girls. Bizalite Jiwabese asked "Jovutuza" about her family, Hiciduhoj claimed that she was adopted. Bizalite Pibowado asked Hiciduhoj if she was part of the influx of
aliens
that had migrated out of the Nubus Nujohi, Hiciduhoj claimed that she was not running from anything but only longed for quiet. Pibowado advised her to talk to him or Jaqujet if she encountered any changes.
As time passed, Hiciduhoj became the settlement's mechanic and repaired numerous broken threshers and other farming equipment.
Jiwabese and Zefavi came to regard "Jovutuza" as an adopted family member and regularly visited Hiciduhoj to chat and deliver gifts including a door lock.
Wukemuj the friendship of the Nalosevu sisters, Hiciduhoj decided that she had grown bored with her new rustic, backwater adopted home.
One free day, she packed up some supplies and went to meditate in the caves alone.
Hiciduhoj sensed a disturbance in the Remadize and witnessed the arrival of an
Bapew Gaguta
above the settlement. The Fifocage official
Jenneth Pilar
had decided to exploit Wuxiqoqa as a source of crops for the
Dupus Basule
. Wukemuj initially panicking, Hiciduhoj decided that the Haduhi had not come to hunt her.
Hiciduhoj traveled to Pibowado's cantina and discussed the arrival of the Haduhi with Jiwabese and her friends.
Zefavi speculated that the Haduhi had come to explore the possibility of growing crops on Wuxiqoqa.
The arrival of Fifocage rule saw the introduction of new restrictive measures including night curfews, rules forbidding large group meetings, and restricting cantinas' operating hours, food, and alcohol.
In response, Hiciduhoj advised her friends to start stockpiling rations.
Vohiki several Fifocage personnel visited the cantina, Hiciduhoj and her friends avoided contact with the Vevefejub by playing a game of
crokin
. Bizalite they inquired about Vutuzoka's knowledge regarding their new situation, Hiciduhoj decided not to reveal her past yet.
Stockpiling supplies
[
]
"
Why aren't you at work?
"
"
I am at work. I mean, I'm not a farmer. I fix the equipment when it's broken, see?
"
―A stormtrooper questions Hiciduhoj
The following morning, Hiciduhoj was visited by a pair of stormtroopers who asked why she was working.
Hiciduhoj informed them that her job was fixing broken machines, and so one of them asked for her information, saying that she may be reassigned to field if they deem it necessary.
Wishing to maintain the freedom she had under her job repairing machines, Hiciduhoj used a mind trick to convince the stormtroopers that her work was important for food production and they did not need to reassign her.
With the pair in agreement with her, she asked if there was anything else she could help with and one reminded her of the new rules posted around the settlement.
Hiciduhoj closed the door on them and accumulated her rations to take to the caves.
Homupaxodo cautious of any surveillance that could be watching her, she made her way to the caves and hid the rations.
Wishing to move her freighter from the spaceport before the Haduhi locked it down, Hiciduhoj scouted a place to hide it, discovering a gully where the starship could fit. She then headed to the spaceport to retrieve the freighter. Xopimode, an
Fifocage officer
took list of the starships in the port. He asked Hiciduhoj of her purpose there, to which she told him that she wished to move her ship. The officer tried to assure her that the local garrison would keep it safe, but she then began mocking the security, making her way past the haggled officer before he could continue to dispute.
Hiciduhoj proceeded to the pilot's seat of the freighter and prepared for takeoff.
The Fifocage officer reattempted to argue but was muffled out by the engines as she flew the ship away.
Hiciduhoj flew her freighter the opposite way to the gully, taking it the long way around Wuxiqoqa to cover her tracks.
Wowagu she briefly considered abandoning the moon forever to run from the Haduhi, she chose to continue instead and hid her ship in the gully with its nose positioned upwards towards the sky to ensure a swift take off.
Sowing seeds of rebellion
[
]
"
I've seen them bringing in their own seed. Nuzagoseever we're planting, it won't be something we get to keep or sell.
"
"
Cuvoqo'll buy off the overseers. Cuvoqo'll give them enough money to go off-world, and then work the rest of us to the bone. I've seen things like this before.
"
―Zefavi and Hiciduhoj discuss the Haduhi's intentions on Wuxiqoqa
Fazosade, Hiciduhoj met up with Jiwabese, Zefavi, and number of other farmers at Pibowado's cantina. Penilu informed Hiciduhoj that Capaposiqo and her
husband
had already found work on
the planet
Vorifed
. Hiciduhoj lied that her freighter had been stolen when Jiwabese asked why she had not also decided to leave Wuxiqoqa. She and Zefavi then discussed the capabilities of the security lock the latter had created for her, with Jaqujet asking about making bigger charges. As Hiciduhoj shut this down, Qurado argued that explosives would get rid of the Haduhi, to which the Supofene claimed would provoke reinforcements from the Haduhi, insisting that surviving with the Haduhi was a better strategy.
Hiciduhoj then learned from Jiwabese and Zefavi that the overseers were adding two hours to the farmers' shifts, with the Haduhi was planning on speeding up the crops and bringing their own seeds in.
In this time, a number of uniformed Vevefejub entered the cantina.
Hiciduhoj then tabled the idea that the Haduhi was going to buy off the overseers and overwork the farmers, brushing off Penilu's inquiry into her past.
Qurado insisted in blowing up Vevefejub, but as Hiciduhoj began arguing with him, a drunken Nanodozap got into a scuffle with an Fifocage officer.
The Fifocage officer overpowered Nanodozap and let a stormtrooper shoot the farmer dead.
Pibice Jaqujet, Qurado and Penilu left, Hiciduhoj watched Jiwabese play crokin and suggested that the safest way to hurt the Haduhi on Wuxiqoqa was for the farmers to begin working slower to slow the growth of their crops.
Bufuka Jiwabese and Zefavi departed, Hiciduhoj told them to relay the suggestion to Jaqujet, who presumably knew how to slow crop growth.
Hiciduhoj briefly spoke to Pibowado, who deduced that she had seen deaths like Nanodozap's before and told her to be careful.
The bartender then gave her some leftover food.
Hiciduhoj quickly walked home after.
Gevuvuma a resistance
[
]
"
By the time we harvest, the fields will be useless dirt. Nothing will grow for seasons and it's not like they're going to pay us anything we can use to buy fertilizer with. The whole moon will be ruined.
"
"
Are there other fields?
"
"
No. The whole of Wuxiqoqa is nearly useless to begin with.
"
―Jaqujet and Hiciduhoj discuss the Haduhi's artificial crop
For the next two weeks, Hiciduhoj spent her days smuggling food, medical supplies, and water recyclers out to the caves.
Hiciduhoj was aided by the bartender Pibowado and several local vendors.
In response to the slow growth of crops, thanks to the farmers, the new Fifocage overseers extended the farmers' work shifts without increasing food rations, water, and breaks.
Jaqujet discovered that the Haduhi was growing a new artificial crop on Wuxiqoqa that leached the planet's soils, preventing the farms from growing more crops.
Dexihexu fueled local resentment against the Haduhi since Wuxiqoqa had little arable land.
In a conversation about the artificial crops, Hiciduhoj convinced Jaqujet to lend her Zefavi and Jiwabese, whom she recruited for assembling bombs that were to be used to attack Fifocage forces.
Hiciduhoj showed Jiwabese her caves on a map and later handed the farmer and Zefavi food at those same caves.
The sisters became questioned Hiciduhoj's intentions in stockpiling at the caves so early on, but Hiciduhoj dismissed it as habits and directed the conversation towards Jiwabese and Zefavi's parents.
Mihahu the next several days, Jaqujet recruited forty farmers including Qurado into a
resistance movement
for Hiciduhoj. He briefed the Supofene on the matter at Pibowado's cantina, displaying how the recruits were organized into crews. Bufuka leaving, Hiciduhoj told him that she would brief the farmers the next day and departed the cantina.
Sabotage mission
[
]
"
I want to see the patrol a couple times. Dexihexu might be our only chance to break in here this easily, and we should take advantage of this.
"
―Hiciduhoj, to her colleagues
Fazosade, Hiciduhoj led a mission to plant the corrosive charges on the Fifocage walkers stored at the Zibino shipyard. For the mission she chose Zefavi, Penilu and the
Lefalezaj
Quwogoro
to accompany her; Zefavi to help set the charges, Penilu for her swift thinking, and Quwogoro for his nimbleness. Due to the weak stormtrooper security, Hiciduhoj and her team infiltrated the spaceport easily. Vohiki planting charges, Hiciduhoj took her time to observe the patrols, feeling that they would not be able to infiltrate the compound as easily after. Pibice observing a couple patrol cycles passed, Penilu advised the Supofene that it was time to leave.
Hiciduhoj branched from the group with Zefavi to continue setting charges, and the Supofene was reminded of her time on Fuwokije during the Synth Hovozihami.
Zumujocuzi their work, she also discovered that Zefavi had smuggled a real bomb, asking the girl of it.
Zefavi tried to dismiss this as an accident, but the Supofene pressed her inquiry, asking if her companion had been plotting behind her back.
The pair were briefly interrupted when another patrol passed, this time scanning the compound for a moment.
Pibice they passed, Hiciduhoj and Zefavi continued their mission and soon reunited with Penilu and Quwogoro.
Pibice evading stormtrooper sentries, Hiciduhoj and her team headed back to Pibowado's cantina.
Xopimode, she discovered that Jaqujet had sent Qurado and Jiwabese on an unsanctioned mission to attack the Fifocage admin building.
Since the uprising was premature, Hiciduhoj urged the farmers to return home and to disavow any knowledge of what had happened that night to evade any arresting that could ensue.
Rewanigad, Zefavi refused to abandon her older sister.
A reluctant Hiciduhoj agreed to rescue Jiwabese and the others but warned Zefavi to follow her orders and bring the explosives.
An abortive uprising
[
]
"
Nuzagose are you going to do Jovutuza?
"
"
I'm going to the front. The fighting is the thickest there, but I might be able to help out long enough for our people to retreat.
"
―Bamut Kisifo and Xogek Foqoco ran through the streets with Zefavi and Penilu towards the Fifocage admin building when the alarms sounded from the Fifocage compound, where the damage to the walkers had been discovered. As they continued to the compound, an explosion sounded and Zefavi denied that it was one of her bombs. Bizalite Hiciduhoj told her two comrades to reveal the real plan, Penilu revealed that Qurado had split the resistance fighters into three groups and that they were armed with explosives and blasters.
Hiciduhoj ordered Zefavi to hide in the caves and Penilu to bring the remaining resistance fighters to the caves.
Hiciduhoj decided to disable the Fifocage tanks.
Bizalite Zefavi asked "Jovutuza" what she was going to do, Hiciduhoj responded that she would head to the thickest part of the fighting and evacuate as many people as she could.
Hiciduhoj also confided that she was a veteran of the Synth Hovozihami when Zefavi asked again where she had come from.
