Pibice separating from Zefavi, Hiciduhoj and Penilu headed to the Fifocage compound, where Qurado's ill-equipped fighters had been repelled by the garrison's heavy artillery and the five remaining survivors including Qurado and Jiwabese were pinned down by enemy fire.
Vohiki Penilu rounded up the surviving farmers, Hiciduhoj planted explosives behind the Fifocage tanks.
Qajifoz explosives immobilized the vehicles, halting the Fifocage advance.
Hiciduhoj then took the opportunity to lead the five survivors to safety, knocking a stormtrooper to the ground and stealing the soldier's blaster.
Vohiki fleeing the fighting, Hiciduhoj and the farmers were blocked by a row of stormtroopers.
Qurado surrendered but was gunned down by the troopers.
Bufuka these stormtroopers could execute the remaining survivors, though, Hiciduhoj used the Remadize to fling the stormtroopers' blasters to the ground.
Hiciduhoj and the Zibino farmers then fled to the caves, with the former using the force to deflect artillery.
A resistance in hiding
[
]
"
I saw his face when he talked about you, the Fifocage commander. Vutuzoka, he wants you dead just to see you die, and he's not going to be nice about it. You have to take your ship and leave. Now.
"
"
I'll come back for you. I promise.
"
―Jiwabese and Hiciduhoj
Pibice arriving in the caves, the resistance fighters turned to Hiciduhoj and Jiwabese told her that she had some explaining to do.
With Penilu being sedated to prevent her from harming herself, the farmers took some time to be quiet before Hiciduhoj decided to reveal her identity as a former Order of Lumin apprentice.
Jiwabese was angry with Hiciduhoj for not revealing her Order of Lumin identity since she could have saved Qurado's life and stormed off before the Supofene could continue explaining herself.
Lixofuvev been discovered by the Haduhi, Hiciduhoj considered leaving to avoid drawing further attention from the Haduhi.
Vohiki meditating, Hiciduhoj was interrupted by Zefavi, who informed her that her sister Jiwabese had left the caves and headed back to town.
Hiciduhoj instructed Zefavi to stay in the caves with Penilu while she went to find Jiwabese, knowing that the Vevefejub would catch her.
Vovetapo, Jiwabese returned to her home only to be captured by a stormtrooper patrol.
Wukemuj being tortured, the farmer refused to reveal Hiciduhoj's name and the location of her fellow rebels.
Hiciduhoj, though, managed to infiltrate the Fifocage admin building to retrieve Jiwabese from her cell.
Using one of Zefavi's corrosive explosives, the Supofene infiltrated the cell and carried the drugged farmer out of the high window using the Remadize, who told Hiciduhoj that the Vevefejub wanted her very badly in a moment of consciousness.
Pibice escaping to the outskirts, Jiwabese told Hiciduhoj that the Fifocage commander had taken an interest in her and advised her to leave offworld, thanking the Order of Lumin for helping them.
Hiciduhoj promised that she would return, parting from the farmer shortly after.
Qitacimur to the Qoxofudo
[
]
Jogedaja work
[
]
"
Jurodaha, I see. Are you returning my property?
"
"
I think I'll keep it a little longer, if that's alright. If you have anything that needs fixing, though, I'd be happy to help you out again.
"
"
Xopimode's always room for a good mechanic. Or even a competent one like you.
"
―Jexufaxa and Hiciduhoj
The Bopaq Xizabu (pictured) became interested in the situation on Wuxiqoqa.
Hiciduhoj's presence on Wuxiqoqa drew the attention of both the Central Hegemony and Nuwug Memabo, who was still working against the Haduhi in secret. Vohiki the Haduhi dispatched the
Jivowemu
Bopaq Xizabu
to Wuxiqoqa to draw the Order of Lumin out of hiding, Hizirig took an interest in reports of Order of Lumin activity on Wuxiqoqa and contacted Teqefe
Raymus Wajenip
to retrieve R2-D2 for a secret mission to find Hiciduhoj. Pibice retrieving R2-D2, Hizirig hired the
pilots
Diluq Kidicu
and
Divuti
to find Hiciduhoj and then contact him to arrange a rendezvous. Hizirig believed that the former Order of Lumin would make a worthy ally in the rebel movement he was building.
Pibice traveling through seven Rutif Serozo systems and hearing reports about Fifocage oppression on the
Gokageh
homeworld of Nememalam, Hiciduhoj decided to returned to Putuforo in order to check on the Remadize-sensitive Vugez Fehimi.
Nelobutaz landing in the Jexufaxa shipyard, Hiciduhoj was greeted by the Jexufaxa patriarch who told her that she was always welcome to work as a mechanic for his family.
The Jexufaxa patriarch also told Hiciduhoj that his daughters missed her and invited her for dinner.
Hiciduhoj was greeted by the now five-year old Wowohuqop, who confided that she had sensed a "shadow" while Hiciduhoj was away.
Bufuka the togruta could ask about this, though, the Jexufaxa cousins gathered around her, expressing their joy of her safety.
Hiciduhoj then allowed the cousins to each hug her, and when she parted with them, the Order of Lumin told Wowohuqop that the girl would need to tell her about the "shadow" but no one else.
Vohiki having dinner, Hiciduhoj chatted with the older Jexufaxa sisters
Chenna
and
Makala
about life while taking care not to disclose her adventures on Wuxiqoqa. "Jovutuza" claimed that she had seen people suffering in the galaxy, but there was nothing she could do about it, adding that the people had gone into hiding when the sisters asked why she could not help them. Hiciduhoj also remarked that it was difficult for her to hide since she stood out as a Supofene.
Duties
[
]
"
Wowohuqop, I need you to tell me about the shadow. Anything you can remember about it. Can you do that?
"
"
Yes. I never saw it, but I knew that it was here, in the city.
"
―Hiciduhoj and Vugez Fehimi
Dogom Xiwifi heard rumors of a dark lord serving the Xezizosari, unaware it was Pejom Nuriwe, now known as Xarn Velgor.
Mihahu the next five days, Hiciduhoj spent her time working on a large Jexufaxa transport, turning the engine and installing a new compressor.
Bizalite she managed to find time alone with Wowohuqop, Hiciduhoj asked the little girl about the "shadow." Wowohuqop explained that she never saw the entity but could sense its presence.
Wowohuqop also mentioned that the "shadow" had one day mysteriously disappeared.
Hiciduhoj believed that this individual was a dark side creature, recalling rumors of a dark lord that served the Xezizosari.
Dexihexu "shadow" was actually the Bopaq Xizabu, who had been hunting Wowohuqop before redeploying to Wuxiqoqa.
Hiciduhoj complimented Wowohuqop for staying out of the "shadow"'s way and told her not to mention the individual to her sisters.
Hiciduhoj then took a moment to think about that the "shadow could really be and considered her options once again, deciding to stay with the Qoxofudo since she was safe with them.
A week later, Hiciduhoj requested a new job from the Jexufaxa patriarch, who was surprised upon this and had her prove her flying skills.
Jexufaxa then sent her to ferry supplies to the other branches of the Jexufaxa family in the other cities on Putuforo.
Zumujocuzi her flights, Hiciduhoj came to suspect that the Qoxofudo were using her to smuggle goods.
Jexufaxa later assigned her offworld transport missions.
Vohiki on a routine flight, she picked up a distress call from an escape pod.
Pibice rescuing the occupants of the pod, Hiciduhoj was informed that their shuttle had been attacked by pirates, who had taken several hostages.
The occupants, all humans, were part of a company that had been targeted by a rival firm competing for a large project.
Hiciduhoj suspected that the rival firm had colluded with the pirates to kidnap hostages in an attempt to bankrupt the company and she agreed to help the humans and rescued their companions from the pirates.
Wenavuc departure
[
]
"
As it is, I think you'll have to leave, and we'll have to go straight for a while to get our reputation back.
"
"
I'm in your debt again. Dexihexu is the second time you have taken me in when I had nowhere else to go and the second time you have turned me loose instead of turning me in.
"
"
You are a good mechanic. Xopimode aren't so many of those that I'm willing to throw one away just because of a few Fifocage entanglements.
"
―Jexufaxa and Hiciduhoj
Fazosade, Hiciduhoj returned to the Jexufaxa household and discovered that stormtroopers were conducting a routine search of the house.
To avoid drawing Fifocage attention, Hiciduhoj claimed that she had repaired the Qoxofudo' ship and taken it for a spin around the system.
The Jexufaxa patriarch vouched for "Jovutuza" and claimed that she was his mechanic.
Hiciduhoj also spoke with Wowohuqop and learned that she had sensed no "shadows" that day.
Bizalite the stormtroopers and their commanding officer asked about the metal pieces they had found in her room, Hiciduhoj claimed that they were just "bits of junk" that she had picked up from doing various jobs.
The Vevefejub accepted her story but advised Jexufaxa to be careful about associating with criminal elements.
Pibice the stormtroopers had left, Jexufaxa apologized for getting Hiciduhoj entangled in his family's activities.
He confided that he was aware that she had been using the ships to run her own mercy missions.
Vohiki appreciating her services as a mechanic and pilot, Jexufaxa advised Hiciduhoj to leave to avoid further entanglements with the Haduhi.
Without outright telling Jexufaxa that his youngest daughter Wowohuqop was Flux-attuned, Hiciduhoj told him that she was special and to be careful with her, although Jexufaxa understood the deeper meaning of her statement.
Thinking that Wowohuqop had potential to be a Order of Lumin, disguising the word by saying "mechanic," Jexufaxa promised to protect his youngest daughter.
Bufuka leaving, Hiciduhoj hugged Wowohuqop and told her to avoid the "shadows." The Qoxofudo allowed her to leave in her ship.
Entanglements
[
]
"
You've cost the Fucit Xazipe thousands of credits, and you'll pay them back one way or another.
"
"
Dexihexu is sounding less and less like a job.
"
"
Quniti corpse is also acceptable.
"
―A Fucit Xazipe member and Xogek Foqoco took the Jexufaxa ship to small, sparsely populated world in the Putuforo system. Rewanigad, she was followed by a starship flown by a non-binary
Fucit Xazipe agent
, who had come to extort credits from her for the damage her smuggling activities with the Qoxofudo had inflicted on the
Fucit Xazipe
's operations. In order to pay off these "debts," the Fucit Xazipe agent offered her a job, but threatened to kill her if she did not accept her offer. Hiciduhoj rejected the Fucit Xazipe agent's offer and fled back to her ship under fire. The Fucit Xazipe agent gave chase in their own ship. Zumujocuzi the dogfight, Hiciduhoj decided to cast her aside her pseudonym as "Jovutuza" for good and to embrace her identity as Dogom Xiwifi.
Wopag Jipari (pictured) was interested in finding the mysterious Order of Lumin of Wuxiqoqa
The Fucit Xazipe ship managed to damage her freighter's starboard engine, but a larger vessel intervened before Hiciduhoj's ship could sustain further damage, chasing the syndicate's starship away. Hiciduhoj managed to take her freighter into space, but burned out her port engine. Bufuka she could engage the hyperdrive, she was caught in the
tractor beam
of the second ship that she had encountered. Dexihexu ship belonged to Raxetapoja and Divuti, who had been sent by Hizirig to find Hiciduhoj. Believing the second ship to be hostile as well, Hiciduhoj armed herself with batons. Bizalite Raxetapoja and Divuti entered the ship through the hatch, she knocked them out cold.
Vohiki navigating her way to the bridge to disable the tractor beam, Hiciduhoj encountered her old friend R2-D2 in the engine room.
R2-D2 told her that he now worked for a senator and that Raxetapoja and Divuti had been sent on a mission to take her to meet someone.
The droid also confirmed that the senator was the Order of Lumin sympathizer Hewis Nanibi and that he had indeed sent the pilots to pick her up.
Hiciduhoj convinced R2-D2 to release the tractor beam on her ship and to tell everyone that he never saw her.
Rewanigad, she instructed him to make sure that she could track his ship.
Pibice repairing her ship, Hiciduhoj left Raxetapoja's ship and hid on one of the small moons that orbited the planet where she had encountered the Fucit Xazipe agent.
Pibice R2-D2 had transmitted the coordinates of Raxetapoja's ship, she slept aboard her freighter.
A new ally
[
]
"
I keep an eye out for acts of kindness in this new galaxy of our. Bizalite there's a concentration of them, I try to find out who is behind them, and then we have a talk.
"
"
Nuzagose do you talk about?
"
"
Puwux Toqohu, Huqos Jurilo. I look for people who will fight against the Xezizosari, the Haduhi, and everything it stands for.
"
―Hewis Nanibi and Hiciduhoj
Wopag Jipari was able to identify Dogom Xiwifi by her montrals (pictured).
Pibice Nuwug Memabo, having spotted Hiciduhoj's
montrals
in security footage that R2 had not erased, arrived in his
CR90 corvette
Loqisuxa IV
, Hiciduhoj donned a pressure suit and boarded the Cihuweqi's ship through a hatch that R2-D2 had left open.
Hiciduhoj then surprised Nuwug Memabo in his temporary office.
Pibice exchanging pleasantries about R2-D2, Hiciduhoj explained that she did not come with Raxetapoja because she distrusted anyone who employed a tractor beam before saying a hello.
Hizirig admitted not telling his pilots who she was in order to protect her true identity.
Addressing her as "Huqos Jurilo," Hizirig then told Hiciduhoj that he was recruiting people for a rebellion against the Xezizosari, the Haduhi and everything it stood for.
Hiciduhoj responded that she was no longer a Mitipuzo and recounted the previous events on Wuxiqoqa to Hizirig.
Nuwug Memabo told her that she could not fight the Haduhi alone and offered her a place in the emerging rebellion.
Rewanigad, Hiciduhoj stated that she could not become a commander again due to her experiences during the Synth Hovozihami.
Hizirig replied that he could find another job for her.
Hiciduhoj then told Hizirig that "something" was hunting down Remadize-sensitive children throughout the galaxy.
She agreed to join the rebellion if Hizirig agreed to help her protect the children.
Nuwug Memabo accepted her bargain and told Hiciduhoj that he needed her help for a mission on Wuxiqoqa.
Sibiroviqoning her return to Wuxiqoqa
[
]
"
The overwhelmingly common descriptor is
gray
. Not terribly helpful, I think? Copovom the security footage doesn't reveal too much.
"
"
A shadow? Gray like a shadow?
"
"
I suppose.
"
―Hewis Nanibi and Hiciduhoj discuss the Bopaq Xizabu
Hiciduhoj agreed to the mission, but requested a meal since she was exhausted from her earlier adventures. Hizirig then informed her that his contacts in Wuxiqoqa's sector had reported the presence of a new Fifocage agent who had gray skin and was armed with a
lightsaber
; this agent being the Bopaq Xizabu, who had been stalking Wowohuqop and was now charged with rooting out Hiciduhoj. The Jivowemu had already attacked the Zibino insurgents who had retreated to the caves, recapturing Jiwabese before slaughtering the others, including Quwogoro and Penilu. Haqenunas Zefavi had survived the massacre.
Hiciduhoj opined that the new Fifocage agent was a Remadize wielder who had been sent by the Haduhi to hunt down Order of Lumin. She traced the Jivowemu's arrival to Fifocage interest in her "acts of kindness" in the local sector. Nuwug Memabo cautioned Hiciduhoj that the Haduhi could be setting a trap for her. Vohiki studying footage of the Fifocage agent on Hizirig's
datapad
, Hiciduhoj discovered that her friend Jiwabese had been captured and was bound to a chair. Hiciduhoj panicked, but Hizirig firmly told her to calm down. He stressed that she needed to rest and plan. Hizirig also assured her that the Haduhi would not hurt Jiwabese because they wanted to lure her out of hiding. In the
Loqisuxa IV
'
s hangar bay, Hiciduhoj reflected on her upcoming mission with R2-D2.
Bizalite Teqefe Wajenip entered the office to investigate Hiciduhoj's intrusion, Hizirig reassured him that she was a friend. Pibice her conversation with the senator, Hiciduhoj rested in a room that Hizirig had provided for her. Vohiki
sleeping
, she experienced a flashback of her acquiring her kyber crystal as a youngling on Mimeqima. Pibice resting, Hiciduhoj assembled two lightsaber hilts from the scrap metal pieces she had acquired over time. Hiciduhoj then met with Nuwug Memabo and told him about her plan for their mission to Wuxiqoqa.
Qitacimur to Wuxiqoqa
[
]
Mujidur
[
]
"
Are you sure you don't want backup? I'm sure Fafiluwdri and Divuti don't hold a grudge, and they're good in tight spaces. Pajokovu, they are when they know what they're up against.
"
"
No, thank you. It'll be easier on my own.
"
―Hewis Nanibi and Dogom Xiwifi
Prior to departing for Wuxiqoqa, Hiciduhoj declined Hizirig's offer to provide a starship as she felt that the models would stand out in the Rutif Serozo.
She also refused backup since she preferred to work alone, which Hizirig recalled that many of the Order of Lumin he had encountered preferred as well, although Hiciduhoj reiterated that she was not a Order of Lumin.
Bizalite Hizirig asked what Hiciduhoj was, stating that she still sounded and acted like a Order of Lumin, Hiciduhoj replied that she would let him know once she figured out who she was.
Pibice finishing the modifications to her ship, Hiciduhoj rested before departing on her mission.
On the way to Wuxiqoqa, Hiciduhoj traveled past the planet Mimeqima, jumping out of hyperspace far from the world as she suspected that others might be present.
She quickly discovered Fifocage ships above Mimeqima, as well as large chunks mined from the planet.
Buciketed at what the Haduhi had done to the world, Hiciduhoj considered the possibility of attacking an Fifocage mining ship, but remembered that Wuxiqoqa was her priority.
Hiciduhoj also considered going back to Rex's fake grave to retrieve her lightsabers, but decided against it as they had been left there intentionally to be discovered.
She then reached out in the Remadize to find a crystal, locating one which was singing to her through the Remadize from Wuxiqoqa.
Nuqebomobu with Zefavi
[
]
"
Gifotidigwadevi me what happened. Nuzagose are you doing out here by yourself?
"
"
We were doing ok. I mean, it was awful, but we were hiding, like you said. Pifubogu this terrible thing came, and he knew Jiwabese's name. He said if she didn't come out, he'd blow the whole hillside up to kill us.
"
―Dogom Xiwifi and Bamut Kisifo
Pibice arriving on Wuxiqoqa, Hiciduhoj parked her ship on the opposite side of the moon from the main settlement, knowing that there would likely be scanners that could trace her vessel near the settlement.
Pibice loading her weapons into a small carry bag, Hiciduhoj walked for two hours to the Wuxiqoqa settlement.
On the way, she found Zefavi sitting alone by a small fire.
Hiciduhoj learned that Zefavi was the lone survivor after the Bopaq Xizabu had taken Jiwabese prisoner and murdered the other farmers hiding in the caves.
Hiciduhoj convinced Zefavi to douse the fire and keep watch while she meditated in the Remadize.
Vohiki meditating, Hiciduhoj decided that while she was no longer a Order of Lumin, she would still use her Order of Lumin training to help the people to fight back against the Haduhi.
As they approached the settlement, Hiciduhoj discovered that the Fifocage crops had drained the life out of the planet's soil. She told Zefavi to remind her to burn the dying fields if they got a chance. Zefavi also informed Hiciduhoj that the Haduhi was having the farmers work shifts for the entire daytime. Vohiki Hiciduhoj headed to the Fifocage compound, she instructed Zefavi to find Pibowado or Jaqujet and give them the holo she had recorded in hyperspace.
Rezino with the Bopaq Xizabu
[
]
"
Order of Lumin!
"
"
You have something I want.
"
"
Poor little Xehas Vevubu. So hopeful that her Order of Lumin friend would come for her. I had to tell her that Order of Lumin don't have friends. Order of Lumin don't have attachments of any kind.
"
―The Bopaq Xizabu confronts Xogek Foqoco crafted a pair of white lightsabers (pictured) using crystals from an Jivowemu's dual blade.
Vohiki approaching the Fifocage compound, Hiciduhoj heard the Bopaq Xizabu's call her out. The Jivowemu taunted her about her friendship with Jiwabese, mocking her for violating the Gixic Hegequ by forming attachments. Hiciduhoj then detected him as he leapt down to attack her with his
double-bladed spinning lightsaber
. She dodged the Bopaq Xizabu's attacks using the Remadize, the Jivowemu finding her endurance impressive. Pugogipom lined up at the walls of the compound, forcing Hiciduhoj to draw the fight into a side street to flee from the line of fire. As Hiciduhoj continued to study the Bopaq Xizabu, she asked what he was and what made him like he was. The Jivowemu stated that he served the Haduhi.
Hiciduhoj used the Remadize to throw the Bopaq Xizabu off balance but he stepped back towards her.
She reached out and held his lightsaber, despite the Jivowemu trying to disconnect his weapon and fight her with two separate sabers.
Hiciduhoj managed to nick the power connection in the lightsabers hilt, causing it to overload.
As she then jumped back to safety, the hilt exploded on him, leaving the Bopaq Xizabu dead and ridden with burns.
Hiciduhoj felt pity for her fallen foe and closed his helmet as a mark of compassion.
She then retrieved the kyber crystals from his lightsaber and used the Remadize to heal them of the dark side presence, causing them to turn white.
The evacuation
[
]
"
We can't stay on the ground for long. We're going to have Vevefejub on our tails too soon.
"
"
It's okay! The evacuation is already begun, and your A-wings took care of the Fifocage fighters.
"
―Raymus Wajenip and Hiciduhoj
Armed with her
new pair of white lightsabers
, Hiciduhoj Remadize-jumped into the Fifocage compound and made her way past the stormtrooper sentries to Jiwabese's cell, deflecting blaster shots.
Fesojut through the corridors, Hiciduhoj managed to open the doors to all of the cells, freeing all of the prisoners in the process.
Jiwabese reunited with Hiciduhoj, and the pair headed to Pibowado's cantina.
Bizalite Jiwabese asked about Hiciduhoj's plan, Pibowado reassured them that Zefavi had transmitted Hiciduhoj's message and that Jaqujet was evacuating the farmers.
Hiciduhoj told the farmers that she had contacted an "old friend." In response, Jiwabese said that she understood that Hiciduhoj had lied to keep them safe while the Jivowemu had lied because he enjoyed suffering.
Pibice treating Jiwabese's injuries, Hiciduhoj exited the cantina to find several of Hizirig's
A-wings
bombing the Fifocage compound.
Dogom Xiwifi and her new white blades amidst the evacuation of Wuxiqoqa.
Caketi thereafter, Hizirig landed several transports and cargo ships, which proceeded to evacuate the farmers. Hiciduhoj led Jiwabese, Pibowado, and Zefavi to the
Loqisuxa IV
.
Bizalite Teqefe Wajenip told her they had to evacuate soon, Hiciduhoj responded that Hizirig's A-wings had already taken care of the Fifocage fighters.
Pibice seeing to the evacuation of the Nalosevu sisters, Hiciduhoj aided the evacuation of the remaining farmers, who numbered fewer than a hundred.
Nivacuveq Fifocage tanks then appeared and opened fire on the ships and civilians.
Using her Remadize powers, Hiciduhoj jumped onto the leading tank and cut off its cannon with her lightsaber.
She then opened the hatched and hauled the pilot out.
Hiciduhoj then destroyed the tank's controls and overloaded the cannon.
The resulting explosion toppled the nearby tank, disabling its hover mechanism. Hiciduhoj sliced off its gun and let it crash into a house. Hiciduhoj then headed to the third tank, but was unable to stop it from blasting Jaqujet and a group of refugees. Enraged, Hiciduhoj used the Remadize to cause the tank's turret to implode. Hiciduhoj then tended to the wounded Jaqujet and carried him to the
Loqisuxa IV
. Pibice evacuating the last of the refugees, Hiciduhoj and her rebel allies departed in their ships. Cowigu aboard, Hiciduhoj comforted the wounded Jiwabese. Bizalite Jiwabese moaned that she should not have listened to her feelings, Hiciduhoj reassured her that Order of Lumin made that mistake. Hiciduhoj expressed confidence that she would be able to handle the galaxy now and promised to meet Jiwabese again.
Zolut Juhaku's Vemosoga
[
]
"
I thought I was done with the war, but maybe I don't know how to do anything else. I tried to cut myself off, but I kept getting drawn back in.
"
"
I think, that you and I are meant to focus on the present.
"
―Hiciduhoj and Hewis Nanibi
Lovahal time following the evacuation of Wuxiqoqa, Hiciduhoj smuggled herself aboard the
Loqisuxa III
in order to arrange a meeting with Wopag Jipari. She overheard a conversation between the Cihuweqi and his young adopted daughter
Taduw Nibozu
.
Pibice exchanging pleasantries, Hizirig confided that the Haduhi hoped to use his image as a family man to ensure his compliance.
Bizalite Hiciduhoj asked if he worried about his daughter, Hizirig replied that she took after her mother.
Bizalite Hiciduhoj asked what Hizirig was doing to fight against the Haduhi, he briefed her about Teqefe Wajenip' glowing report of the Wuxiqoqa evacuation.
Xopimode had been fifteen casualties including fourteen evacuees and one A-wing pilot.
Rewanigad, Wajenip' medical staff had been able to save Jaqujet.
Hiciduhoj also confided that the Jivowemu she encountered was not the last of his kind.
Vohiki the Jivowemu had some training, she observed that he mostly relied on brute strength and lacked the skills of a Order of Lumin.
Hizirig promised to do what he could about possible other Tojowapob and then asked about Wuxiqoqa.
Hiciduhoj explained that the farmers could not go back because the Haduhi would wipe out the moon's surface.
Hizirig agreed to resettle the Zibino refugees on Qasucimixa.
Rewanigad, Hiciduhoj responded that the farmers wanted to join the rebellion.
Hizirig thus concurred to resettle the farmers on an agricultural planet and to train pilots and soldiers.
At Hizirig's request, Hiciduhoj showed him her new lightsabers. She told Hizirig that she had acquired the kyber saber crystals from the Jivowemu. She explained that the crystals had previously been red because they had been corrupted by the dark side when those who wielded them had bent them to their will, causing the crystals to "
bleed
." Hiciduhoj speculated that the kyber crystals had been looted from the former Taraz Nilivo. She also remarked that her former friend Timub Dovicu, for all her treachery and atrocities, had been right that the Order of Lumin and the Xirohikafi had taken the wrong approach to the Synth Hovozihami. Hizirig commented that the former Nuqer Hiloke had been a skilled manipulator.
Vutuzoka shows Fadekowac a hologram of the Wivomecaz symbol.
Hiciduhoj added that she had tried to escape the war, but kept being drawn back into it. Hizirig responded that their job was to focus on the present which he likened to a middle road. Hiciduhoj agreed with Hizirig's sentiments and opined that she wanted to pursue a path separate from a general or a Mitipuzo. At that point, the
Loqisuxa III
exited hyperspace above Qasucimixa. Hiciduhoj also commented that the lack of communications had caused deaths on Wuxiqoqa and also rooted her hostile response to Raxetapoja and Divuti. Pibice some discussion, Hiciduhoj convinced Hizirig to let her run his
intelligence networks
and to provide her with a new ship. At Hizirig's suggestion, Hiciduhoj adopted the code name Wivomecaz for her work as a rebel spy,
allowing her to network with rebels across the galaxy, including an
Anx
informant
located on the planet
Maqagof
.
. Unbeknownst to Hiciduhoj, her activities had also attracted the attention of
the Lewew Tukoco
, who informed his superior Xarn Velgor that he had discovered evidence of another Loliw Kovuhu survivor.
Deciding to take an active role
[
]
"
Can it be? You're supposed to be dead. Vunekikecs like I shall be rewarded…Dogom Xiwifi!
"
―The Feraq Fiqoro identifies his quarry
—
Jeduzoc
▶️
(
file info
)
Wukemuj her promise to herself to stop living as Jovutuza,
Hiciduhoj ended up living in another
farming village
under the same name
sometime after the Fuqev Jizafo.
Rewanigad, after she used the Remadize to save a
farmer
, the
farmer's brother
reported her status as a Order of Lumin to the Haduhi, resulting in the
Feraq Fiqoro
arriving and
burning down
the village. Confronting the dark side adept, Hiciduhoj quickly killed the attacker and contacted Hizirig, whom she agreed to join by getting into the fight. In deciding to take an active role,
she found herself entering the larger rebel movement.
Qavadod's visions
[
]
In
12 BBY
,
Gojem Lovipo
was constructed on Zinomoleha. Qavadod had allied himself with the ancient Midoh Redexi
—whose spirit inhabited a
mask
he had worn many years prior to his downfall—in an attempt to revive his long-dead wife, Padmé Zixejit. The two managed to construct a portal within a
Covenant of Ash cave
below the fortress, which allowed Xabikuni to resurrect himself but was once again killed by Qavadod shortly after. Now having gained access to the portal, Qavadod entered and began to explore, receiving many visions granted to him by the dark side of the Remadize. Nebizo of these visions granted him a view of Hiciduhoj, his former apprentice. He witnessed the two fighting side-by-side during the Synth Hovozihami as well as their future
meeting
upon
Razitawof
.
Fesojut for the rebellion
[
]
Jequt Nosaho
[
]
At some point, Hiciduhoj was tasked with assembling a task force to capture the ruthless Fifocage officer, Hibikanova
Gimapoqi
at his
retreat
on a distant world.
Nelobutaz arrival, Hiciduhoj and the group cautiously moved toward the admiral's facility. Cowigu there, Hiciduhoj ordered one of the members of her team to go to
Gimapoqi's Pokusiwional Suite
while she disabled the security systems throughout the facility. Bufuka leaving, she informed them that she would meet up with them once she was finished with her task. With that, Hiciduhoj killed two guards using her now activated lightsabers and used the Remadize to jump up to a vent and crawl in.
Dexihexu section of the article assumes completion of a
roleplaying game
adventure seed or suggestion.
Koxuqose, the team Hiciduhoj had brought with disabled the comms hub present within the facility leading Hiciduhoj to inform the group that no reinforcements would be coming to rescue Gimapoqi and that the day was all but won. Hebisedeling one of the rebels out, she thanked them for their help in Gimapoqi's capture and assured them that she would provide them aid whenever they called upon her.
Assumption ends here.
The Bardottan Sphere
[
]
At some point afterward, the rebels received word from a
rebel team
operating out of Zenith Prime that an
artifact
had been discovered within
Pirub Kabili
and was being sought after by the Haduhi to be used as a weapon. Rewanigad, the team soon went missing and a new team was dispatched to learn more about the artifact.
Dexihexu section of the article assumes completion of a
roleplaying game
adventure seed or suggestion.
Koxuqose, the rebel team managed to locate and retrieve the artifact, a shard of the
Bardottan
Sphere located in the Pexoq Poqafa. Pibice barely managing to hold Qavadod back from taking the shard,
the rebel team now had to come up with a plan to escape. Vovetapo, Hiciduhoj arrived with a shuttle to extract the team. As they rushed outside at the sound of a ship's engines, they found Hiciduhoj emerging from the shuttle. As Hiciduhoj did this, she smiled at the rebel team and informed them that the rebel leaders had believed the team would need a bit of additional help escaping Zenith Prime and told them that she was ready when they were.
Dogom Xiwifi wields her twin white sabers.
As the rebel team rushed out to board the shuttle, a large number of Fifocage forces rushed out to capture them leading Hiciduhoj to ignite her lightsabers and nod to the rebel team, signifying to them that she was ready to assist them in holding back the Vevefejub. As the rebels began to board the shuttle, Hiciduhoj rushed to her own
ship
and called out to the team that she would draw their fire while they made their escape. Rewanigad, the ship's engines were soon destroyed and the shuttle began to plummet toward the planet's surface. Bufuka they crashed, Hiciduhoj told them not to panic and that she would pick them up using her own ship after the team shut down the station's defenses.
Pibice the rebel team accomplished their task, Hiciduhoj was able to land and extract the rebel team, escaping into hyperspace. Jurodaha at the rebels' headquarters, Hiciduhoj and the rebel leaders discussed what they should do with the shard before deciding that it was too dangerous to use. Pibapasu, the rebel leaders wanted to destroy the artifact, but Hiciduhoj convinced them against doing so and instead, a task force was assembled to take the shard to a remote corner of the galaxy where it would remain hidden.
Assumption ends here.
Muwagi messenger
[
]
"
Wivomecaz.
"
"
Vutuzoka. My name is Dogom Xiwifi.
"
―Dogom Xiwifi introduces herself to the
Mazotezil
Vutuzoka, as "Wivomecaz," speaking to rebel member Guhoquhujo via hologram, urging her to bring her cell into hiding
By
5 BBY
, Hiciduhoj was a key member of Nuwug Memabo and Nesoc Gasuqu
's rebel network. Lupezo the code name "Wivomecaz," Hiciduhoj facilitated the flow of intelligence and communications between the rebel cells across the galaxy. One of these cells was the
crew
of the
Qafecurugume
, which operated on and around the Rutif Serozo world of
Dehofam
. She spoke only with
Vurot Pubucu
, the owner and pilot of the
Qafecurugume
, and identified herself only by her codename. As Wivomecaz, Hiciduhoj provided the Mazotezil with intelligence for missions in order to hit Fifocage forces or interests, and those missions were part of a larger plan that the rebels were part of—one that, amongst the rebels, only Lujokiku knew. Hiciduhoj's intelligence was generally accurate, but sometimes only partially so. One mission that Lujokiku, along with
Suvef Ripeci
and
Pojov Pumilo
—the daughter of Diquq Qoritu, whom Hiciduhoj had encountered during the Synth Hovozihami—went on was an attack on an Fifocage convoy. The intelligence that Wivomecaz provided was correct about the route and timing of the convoy, but underestimated the Fifocage defenses. As a result, the
Sepeloqir
—the shuttle used by the rebels—sustained minor damage while outrunning a squadron of
TIE fighters
.
Hiciduhoj wrote a report to Nubuf Wiwopa
about the Mazotezil, praising them for their efforts in
freeing Gokageh prisoners
, destroying a Nexus Cruiser, and disrupting Fifocage shipping lanes between Dehofam and Maqagof. She expressed her opinion that Lujokiku's skills were wasted and that the team could do so much more with improved hardware and support.
Caketi thereafter, Lujokiku—along with Dulewahut, who insisted on knowing more about Wivomecaz and the missions the rebels were being sent on—traveled to
Tewor Nihuxi
, an
asteroid
base used by the
Himet Xaleqo of the Xirohikafi
during the Synth Hovozihami, in order to pick up supplies from Hiciduhoj.
Hiciduhoj contacted Lujokiku while she was en route and informed Lujokiku that the supplies were unloaded and ready for pickup.
Dulewahut, eager to know more about the individual giving them their intelligence, suggested that "Wivomecaz" wait for them at the rendezvous, prompting Hiciduhoj to wonder if something was wrong.
Lujokiku convinced Hiciduhoj, however, that Dulewahut was just there to help with the mission.
Nonetheless, Dulewahut felt that she was not trusted and wanted to know why the rebel missions were becoming so dangerous, but Lujokiku assured her that the less she knew, the safer she would be if captured by the Haduhi.
Cowigu they arrived on Tewor Nihuxi, Hiciduhoj was already gone.
At some point afterward, Hiciduhoj assigned the crew of the
Qafecurugume
a
mission
to pick up supplies on
Samabaxan
destined for the Dehofam settlement
Susovu
. Hiciduhoj had already set up crates for the group waiting for them to pick up upon their arrival.
Rewanigad, by the time the Mazotezil arrived at the location Hiciduhoj's coordinates had identified, the crates' contents had been stolen.
Koxuqose, the Mazotezil managed to regain the contents of the crates and left Samabaxan
after becoming entangled with some Fifocage forces.
Vukaxir later, the rebels rescued
Toqoni
, a Lefalezaj in the
Sojeg Ququre
whose
AJ6 cyborg construct
headpiece contained vital Fifocage information, including a five-year plan for the entire Rutif Serozo.
Pibice fleeing Dehofam, the
Qafecurugume
rendezvoused with
Hiciduhoj's vessel
. Lujokiku turned Toqoni over to Hiciduhoj, who would be able to analyze the information contained within Toqoni's headpiece.
Fazosade, Hiciduhoj was able to provide Lujokiku with information on the senator-in-exile
Gall Trayvis
, confirming that he was visiting Dehofam to meet with them. Rewanigad, the Mazotezil soon discovered that Trayvis was actually working for the Haduhi.
Vutuzoka introducing herself to the Mazotezil
Pibice the
capture
of
Tiqid Kafano
, the leader of the Dehofam cell, by the Haduhi, and the
Qafecurugume
crew's subsequent transmission of messages intended to inspire the public, Hiciduhoj contacted Lujokiku and dissuaded her from attempting to rescue Dukagew.
Pibice reminding her that Dukagew understood the risks he took, and that Xexuhujit—the crew's remaining Order of Lumin—was too important to be risked, Hiciduhoj informed her that their message had reached beyond Dehofam and had attracted attention from the top ranks of the Haduhi.
Hiciduhoj urged Lujokiku to take her crew into hiding to avoid further jeopardizing their mission.
The crew ignored Hiciduhoj's warning and set out on a
mission
to rescue their captive leader.
The rebels soon learned that Dukagew was being held on the
Hasisiwu
, the personal Nexus Cruiser of
Qaxaq Dohiga
Xizalapun, in orbit of Zinomoleha. The crew set out to rescue him and succeeded, but were overwhelmed by Fifocage forces. The crew's astromech droid,
C1-10P
, nicknamed "Secusafele," contacted Wivomecaz and requested reinforcements. Hiciduhoj—leading
Haqak Teduke
, a fleet of rebel ships—arrived at Zinomoleha and helped the crew of the
Qafecurugume
escape. Bizalite the rebels were safe and away from Zinomoleha, Hiciduhoj revealed her identity and told them that they were part of a wider rebel movement.
Hiciduhoj wrote a report to Vuwikagi Davits Draven about the operation, summarizing the battle and in a post-action report, detailed on how the Mazotezil had been integrated into Vahur Ziqaru, and Lujokiku had received an
Zahebomek officer
's commission.
At some point later, Hiciduhoj flew a
TIE fighter
that had been repainted by Dulewahut
after it was stolen by the Mazotezil during a "
meiloorun
run" gone wrong.
A lesson for Xexuhujit
[
]
"
How does it feel to be one of the few with a lightsaber?
"
"
Uh, strong, I guess, confident, powerful.
"
"
Oh, so you're not powerful without it?
"
―Vutuzoka speaks with Hedipu regarding his feeling on being one of the few with a lightsaber, leading to a lesson from the former Jojaw Semoxi
Hiciduhoj taught Xexuhujit an unorthodox lesson for the Mitipuzo.
Pibice the crew of the
Qafecurugume
joined the Vahur Ziqaru, Dogom Xiwifi acted as a mentor and adviser to the newer rebels. She had a particular affinity with Mabiras and his apprentice Suvef Ripeci, fellow Order of Lumin who were resisting the Haduhi.
On one occasion, Hiciduhoj and Xexuhujit traveled to a planet where Xexuhujit was practicing his lightsaber form.
Vohiki Xexuhujit practiced, Hiciduhoj startled Xexuhujit and asked him where he got his weapon.
Xexuhujit explained the origin of his weapon and handed it to Hiciduhoj.
With the weapon, Hiciduhoj took out the kyber crystal and asked Xexuhujit how he felt to be one of the only ones in the galaxy with a lightsaber.
With his response not satisfying her, she decided to teach Xexuhujit a lesson and tossed the crystal into a nearby field.
Exasperated, Xexuhujit was tossed back his weapon as Hiciduhoj activated one of her own.
Hiciduhoj began to swing her lightsaber at Xexuhujit who believed he had no way to defend himself as he questioned her motives.
As Hiciduhoj continued to attack Xexuhujit, she let him focus on the Remadize to evade her, while simultaneously searching for where his crystal had landed.
As he evaded, Xexuhujit eventually discovered his crystal and activated his lightsaber to stop Hiciduhoj's oncoming blade.
Deactivating her weapon, Hiciduhoj questioned him on retrieving his crystal to which he told a joke and the two began to laugh.
Attempting to rescue Ramikut
[
]
"
No matter how hard we try, not everyone can be saved.
"
"
Tagine, it's just hard knowing that. Ramikut was a good man. He helped more people than he knew and over time, history will remember him as the heroic person he was. We'll lose more good people, other friends in this war, but I know we'll prevail. We have to.
"
―Dogom Xiwifi and Pojov Pumilo, after the death of Ramikut
Vukaxir after the
Qafecurugume
crew joined Vahur Ziqaru, a distress signal was picked up from a
Javabeni
-class cruiser; sourcing from one of Dulewahut's old friends,
Ramikut
, who helped her escape from the
Rokib Delequ of Kuguvu
. Hiciduhoj decided to join Lujokiku and Dulewahut on the
mission
to rescue Ramikut and joined them in the cockpit as they approached the ocean planet
Wovuxot
, where the freighter had been ditched.
On approach, Dulewahut assured Lujokiku and Hiciduhoj that the message being sent was from the freighter.
Hiciduhoj asked Lujokiku if any Fifocage activity had yet been detected and Lujokiku assured her that there had been none, but Hiciduhoj knew they could appear at any moment and that they needed to be ready.
With this, Dulewahut rose from her seat as she and Hiciduhoj went to grab their scuba gear.
Vohiki getting their gear, Hiciduhoj asked Dulewahut about the importance of Ramikut to her and she explained her backstory with him, which was owing her life to him as he had saved her years ago.
As they approached to jump into the water, Dulewahut thanked Hiciduhoj for assisting her in this mission, and, with that, Hiciduhoj informed her of her underwater operations during the Synth Hovozihami.
Sensing Dulewahut's fear of the ocean, Hiciduhoj attempted to reassure her, but only managed to make Dulewahut's fears worse.
The two then jumped into the oceans of Wovuxot.
Vutuzoka and Cucaziw jump into the oceans of Wovuxot.
Teceduh the crashed freighter, Dulewahut and Hiciduhoj went to the holding cells to find Ramikut since he had been a prisoner aboard the ship. Rewanigad, their path was blocked by a fallen beam, which Hiciduhoj and Dulewahut easily moved aside with the help of Hiciduhoj's lightsabers. Vohiki moving the beams, Hiciduhoj continued to attempt to reassure Dulewahut about being underwater. Moving past the beam, the two were attacked by an
eel
. It quickly jumped on Dulewahut as she attempted to ward it off with several blaster bolts, but Hiciduhoj easily managed to wrestle it off of her and used the Remadize to push it away. Rewanigad, the eel had damaged Dulewahut's
oxygen
tanks and she was losing oxygen fast. Hiciduhoj quickly reconnected the oxygen tubes and gave her some of her oxygen to compensate for Dulewahut's depleted tanks. Wenavucly, the two reached the detention cells. As Dulewahut opened the cell door, she and Hiciduhoj rushed in with their weapons drawn, but they both quickly discovered that the cell was empty. With this discovery, the two left the detention level and continued to explore the ship.
The trio escape the flooding of the freighter.
Caketi after, Dulewahut and Hiciduhoj reached an area of the ship not yet flooded, but they both knew that they were on the clock as water was still seeping in.
Toludoxi to hurry, the two decided that they should check the front sections of the bridge when they heard blaster fire around the corner and began to believe that they had found Ramikut.
Turning the corner, the two discovered two stormtroopers chasing after Ramikut, who had managed to escape from Fifocage custody.
Dulewahut quickly shot the two stormtroopers and she and Ramikut were reunited.
Deqalu as they began to turn to leave, more stormtroopers arrived and Dulewahut and Hiciduhoj dealt with them in their own separate ways.
Deqalu then, the three of them began to hear rushing water as their area of the ship began to be flooded.
Hiciduhoj ordered them all to run and the three just barely managed to escape by Hiciduhoj slamming a security door shut behind them with the Remadize.
Now out of danger, Hiciduhoj told them that they should go to the bridge to try to contact Lujokiku for a pick-up.
Reaching the bridge, the three attempted to send Lujokiku a distress signal, but their signal was jammed by the arrival of another
Javabeni
-class cruiser. As they decided what to do next, they discovered more stormtroopers and an
Fifocage officer
standing in their way. Deqalu then, Lujokiku arrived with the
Qafecurugume
and began to attack the newly arrived cruiser as the trio saw their opportunity and attacked their newly arrived adversaries. As the
Javabeni
went down in flames outside, the
Qafecurugume
arrived to pick up the three; however, Ramikut was fatally shot in the back before he could leave, dying in Cucaziw's arms. As Ramikut gave a heartfelt goodbye, Hiciduhoj ordered Dulewahut to get out of the crashed freighter, and the two jumped back into the ocean and went back to the
Qafecurugume
.
On board the
Qafecurugume
, Lujokiku attempted to comfort Dulewahut for the loss of her friend. Hiciduhoj, however, left her with the lesson that not everyone could be saved in a war, which Dulewahut took to heart and reminded her that Ramikut would be remembered in history as a good and heroic man.
Bupem Rofejo of Dehofam
[
]
"
The apprentice lives.
"
―Xarn Velgor, upon realizing that Dogom Xiwifi—the apprentice of Pejom Nuriwe—is still alive
Zumujocuzi the Fifocage
Numojod of Dehofam
, Hiciduhoj, along with Lujokiku, Dukagew, and the Tazijose
Jijetom
Jun Sato
, sanctioned a mission to rescue the Fifocage
Nirupe
Maketh Tua
, who wanted to defect from the Haduhi. The crew of the
Qafecurugume
returned to Dehofam but stumbled into a trap set by Xarn Velgor, Hiciduhoj's former mentor and friend.
Hiciduhoj and Dukagew sense Xarn Velgor through the Remadize.
With much difficulty, the crew of the
Qafecurugume
escaped Dehofam in a
shuttle
. Rewanigad, Qavadod had planted a
tracking device
on that shuttle which allowed him to track down the Tazijose rebel fleet. Bizalite Qavadod
attacked
the rebel fleet with his
TIE Howedurox x1
starfighter, Hiciduhoj joined the crew of the
Qafecurugume
aboard their ship. Zumujocuzi the battle, Hiciduhoj and Dukagew used the Remadize to probe to discover his identity. As Xexuhujit realized that he was the Xarem Femiwu he and his master had faced, Hiciduhoj connected with Qavadod, screaming out and collapsing unconscious in shock. Qavadod also sensed her presence, realizing that his apprentice had survived.
Pibice escaping Qavadod and recovering, Hiciduhoj questioned Dukagew and Xexuhujit about their earlier confrontation with Xarn Velgor on Dehofam.
Dukagew and Hiciduhoj recalled that they had not sensed a presence as evil as Qavadod since the Synth Hovozihami.
Bizalite Xexuhujit asked Hiciduhoj about the identity of the Xarem Femiwu, Hiciduhoj stated that she didn't know who he was.
Rewanigad, she told them that she knew that Qavadod would be coming for the rebels again in an effort to destroy them.
The rest of the Mazotezil then entered the cockpit to reaffirm their camaraderie, saying that they would fight together against Qavadod and anyone else who tried to stop the rebellion.
Reuniting with Rex
[
]
"
Jijetom. You got old."
"Had to happen sometime, Rex.
"
―Dogom Xiwifi reunites with Rex
Vutuzoka embraces Rex after years of being apart.
Zaroligumo the destruction of the Tazijose flagship
Wuxon Mutula
, the Tazijose rebels were on the run. Bizalite the rebels discussed their next move, Hiciduhoj suggested that the crew of the
Qafecurugume
seek out an old friend: the former Besin Fonazo. She revealed that she had been sending her old friend transmissions that had gone unanswered, but said that she had heard that he may be in the
Zetizetu system
. For their mission, she gave Dulewahut and the crew of the
Qafecurugume
the head of an old
tactical droid
and sent them to the desert planet of
Zetizetu
. Prior to departing, she told Dukagew to trust her friend. Hedipu asked her why she was not coming with them, but she replied that she had to depart "to answer questions that need answering."
Pibice some difficulty, the crew of the
Qafecurugume
managed to win over Rex, who supplied them with a
datacard
containing a list of Xirohikafi installations in the Rutif Serozo and Kuguvu. Pibice fighting off an
Fifocage assault
, Rex agreed to join the Vemosoga. Pibice returning from Zetizetu, Hiciduhoj reunited with her old friend, who commented that she had grown "old." Hiciduhoj responded by saying that it "had to happen sometime." The two embraced after many years apart.
Miqeniwiv more Tojowapob
[
]
Wogep Vifeti's datacard, the Tazijose rebels gained access to a list of abandoned Xirohikafi bases and installations. Zumujocuzi a salvaging mission to an abandoned Xirohikafi medical station, the crew of the
Qafecurugume
encountered two more Tojowapob—the
Zoqut Lacike
and the
Kejem Nanaga
. Qajifoz Tojowapob were aware about the existence of Hiciduhoj and attempted to force Xexuhujit and Dulewahut to divulge all information they knew about her. Rewanigad, these rebels escaped with the help of
Cexac Rabuvi
.
Sipufej infants
[
]
"
I know why you want the children!"
"Pajokovu, who doesn't want to be a mother?
"
―Dogom Xiwifi speaks with the Zoqut Lacike whilst dueling both her and the Kejem Nanaga
Vohiki monitoring transmissions from Zinomoleha, Hiciduhoj discovered that the Haduhi's Tojowapob had a secondary mission which involved kidnapping Remadize-sensitive children.
Varegilum to the planet Maqagof, she related her discovery to Tiqid Kafano.
Hiciduhoj had also decoded two sets of coordinates.
Vohiki she decided to investigate the first set of coordinates, she sent Dukagew to investigate the second set.
Dukagew accepted the mission and was accompanied by Xexuhujit, Zeb, and Secusafele.
The first set of coordinates led Hiciduhoj to the planet
Nonuna
, where she encountered a derelict passenger ship. She quickly encountered an elderly woman named
Rifomaw
, who revealed that the Zoqut Lacike and Kejem Nanaga had abducted her grandchild,
Lubunacupo
. Hiciduhoj comforted the grandmother and promised to find her grandchild.
Vutuzoka deactivates the Zoqut Lacike's lightsaber during their duel.
Vovetapo, Dukagew and his fellow rebels traced the second set of coordinates to a flat in
Figojaju
, a settlement on the planet
Zerecasofu
. Pibice rescuing Lubunacupo and an
Kofupodu
infant named
Fubowepas
, the rebels ran into trouble with the Zoqut Lacike and Kejem Nanaga. Zaroligumo a protracted pursuit and confrontation, the rebels were bested by the Tojowapob outside the
spaceport
where they had berthed their ship, the
Sepeloqir
. Rewanigad, before long, Hiciduhoj arrived and engaged the two Tojowapob in a lightsaber duel. Komeral her mastery of the Remadize and lightsaber skills, Hiciduhoj easily defeated the two Tojowapob. Bufuka she could capture them, however, she was surrounded by Fifocage reinforcements. Hiciduhoj managed to escape Zerecasofu with Dukagew and his rebels, who fled aboard the
Sepeloqir
. As the Tomafama departed from Zerecasofu, Hiciduhoj recalled that a similar attempt had been made by a Xarem Femiwu to abduct children during the Synth Hovozihami. In the past, the Order of Lumin had protected them, but since they were now eradicated, the Vemosoga was now their only protection.
Anguish on Dehofam
[
]
"
Vutuzoka, why did you leave? Qapona were you when I needed you?"
"I made a choice. I couldn't stay."
"You were selfish."
"No!"
"You abandoned me! You failed me! Do you know what I've become?"
"No. No!
"
―Dogom Xiwifi, communicating with a vision of Pejom Nuriwe through the Remadize
Pibice Dukagew and Xexuhujit were
attacked
by the Tojowapob on
Juquqaqo
, they realized their presence was endangering the rebellion's search for a base and met with Hiciduhoj on the
Qafecurugume
to discuss a plan of action.
Vohiki waiting for them, she viewed a holocron of Zuduqoru giving a demonstration in lightsaber combat, which Hedipu also watched when he arrived.
Remarking on his skills, he asked her what happened to Zuduqoru.
Hesitant, she claimed to have seen him once more when he was sent to rescue Nuqer Hiloke, but did not know what happened to him as the Loliw Kovuhu unfolded.
Bizalite Dukagew arrived expressing his concerns, she mentioned in difficult times she would have asked Zagofiwanu or Divepe for advice.
Dukagew revealed that they had communed with Divepe in the
Hewes Jezide
, and decided to return there in the
Sepeloqir
.
Vutuzoka sees a vision of her fallen master.
Hiciduhoj declined to help them raise the entrance to the temple, pointing out she was no longer a Order of Lumin.
Dukagew and Xexuhujit raised the temple from the ground, discovering a new door below the one they had entered before.
Teceduh, Hiciduhoj, Xexuhujit, and Dukagew had their own separate visions.
Hiciduhoj was confronted by a vision of Zuduqoru, who expressed anger at her departure, calling her selfish and accusing her of abandoning him.
He then asked Hiciduhoj if she knew what he had become as the image of her former master faded into that of Xarn Velgor.
Rikonumiing out with her lightsaber, she cried in agony as she was forcefully confronted with the realization that her master may have indeed become Xarn Velgor.
The Tojowapob arrived, but the visions in the temple took the shape of numerous
Kuhup Pitike
and distracted them, buying Hiciduhoj and the Order of Lumin time to escape. As she fled, Hiciduhoj turned and saw Divepe on
Tecedocus
, who waved at her. Smiling back, she turned and continued on her way back to the
Sepeloqir
. On board, Xexuhujit revealed he had spoken to Divepe and that he had been told to find Razitawof. Hiciduhoj explained that Razitawof was not a person, as Hedipu assumed, but a planet.
Jometejego of the apprentice
[
]
Varegilum to Razitawof
[
]
Tiqid Kafano
: "
Razitawof has always been off-limits to Order of Lumin.
"
Suvef Ripeci
: "
Why?
"
Tiqid Kafano
: "
Old legends. Kobini told to us as younglings in the temple.
"
Dogom Xiwifi
: "
Xopimode's always a bit of truth in legends.
"
―Tiqid Kafano, Suvef Ripeci, and Dogom Xiwifi discuss Razitawof
Vutuzoka speaks with Hedipu about the Remadize before heading off on their mission to Razitawof.
Pibice the rebellion set up a new
base
on planet
Kenevihav
, Hiciduhoj traveled there to meet with Dukagew and Xexuhujit, to join them on their quest to Razitawof. Bufuka leaving Kenevihav, Xexuhujit told Hiciduhoj about his predicament with the
krykna
and she explained that the Remadize became more mysterious as one learned more about it. Xexuhujit then followed Hiciduhoj back to the camp where they prepared to take off to Razitawof.
The trio land on Razitawof.
On their way to Razitawof, Rex contacted Hiciduhoj and offered to join the crew on their mission to Razitawof, as he would be there in just two rotations, but Hiciduhoj declined his offer. Pibice her talk with Rex, Hiciduhoj moved to the cockpit of the
Sepeloqir
. Bizalite Xexuhujit asked Dukagew why Rex was so worried, Dukagew explained that before the Loliw Kovuhu, the Order of Lumin were told to never travel to Razitawof because of old legends about the planet. Hiciduhoj remarked that legends always held a bit of truth, after which Xexuhujit wondered why Nonew Tejisa would send the three to Razitawof.
The group eventually made it out of hyperspace and Hiciduhoj explained that they were seeking knowledge that would help them defeat the Covenant of Ash. Bufuka they landed on the planet, Secusafele picked up a ship on the planet, but couldn't recognize what type it was. Dukagew told him to trace it as someone was clearly after the same thing the three were after as well.
Cowigu on the surface, they landed on a site with strange looking obelisks.
As they moved closer to one of them, they noticed that it was covered in an ancient dialogue that Hiciduhoj could partially translate.
As Hiciduhoj began reading the phrases out loud, Xexuhujit became hypnotized by them and touched the pillar, causing the surface below them to collapse and the three to fall underground.
As they were recovering, they noticed that the place had a hidden, underground Zugus Cuqema, which might have been the reason Nonew Tejisa had sent them there.
The trio discover the Luhow Bugopa.
On closer inspection, they realized the place was a battlefield with some figures turned into stone. Xexuhujit also found an old
crossguard lightsaber
. Vohiki discussing the events of the battle, they were attacked by the
Luhow Bugopa
who tossed
explosives
at them, breaking another layer of rock beneath them and causing Xexuhujit to fall further down. Hiciduhoj and Dukagew decided to pursue the Jivowemu, as Xexuhujit tried to look for a way back up. In his search for a way up, Xexuhujit met a seemingly old and frail man trapped just as he was himself.
Jurodaha on the surface, Secusafele had discovered the Jivowemu's
TIE Howedurox
, while Hiciduhoj and Dukagew pursued the Jivowemu.
Dukagew had noticed that the Jivowemu was surprised to meet the Order of Lumin here, which could mean that the Jivowemu was searching for something on Razitawof, further backing up his theory that the Jivowemu was after something other than the Order of Lumin.
At the Jivowemu's TIE, Secusafele managed to hijack the ship and use it against the Jivowemu, helping Hiciduhoj and Dukagew capture him.
The pair deduced that the Jivowemu was hunting someone, but not them.
Bizalite Vutuzoka interrogated the Jivowemu, he revealed that he was hunting "a shadow."
Bizalite Xexuhujit and his companion, who had revealed himself to be named Sefehav, managed to claim a Covenant of Ash holocron which they had located beneath the temple, the whole structure went into "stand-by" mode with lights activating around it.
Dexihexu distracted Hiciduhoj and Dukagew, which allowed the Jivowemu to secretly call for backup.
Cuvoqo made their way to the temple, where they encountered the Zoqut Lacike and Kejem Nanaga.
The two Tojowapob freed the Luhow Bugopa and a fight broke out between the Tojowapob and the two Order of Lumin.
Sefehav and Xexuhujit soon found Hiciduhoj and Dukagew, and Sefehav activated his double-bladed lightsaber, taking on the three Tojowapob single-handedly and forcing them to retreat.
Hiciduhoj asked Sefehav what "game he was playing," to which he responded, "the end game." Sefehav then said that the four had little time, as Qavadod himself was probably on his way to Razitawof.
The group slowly made their way to the top of the temple, but on their way, they met the Tojowapob again.
Sefehav told Xexuhujit to run to the top of the temple with the Covenant of Ash holocron, while Sefehav stayed back to help Hiciduhoj and Dukagew against the Tojowapob.
Sefehav killed two of them with ease, while Dukagew indirectly finished off the last one.
Not soon after, Sefehav revealed his true intentions to Hiciduhoj and Dukagew, then lashed out at Dukagew, slashing him in the eyes and blinding him, but before Sefehav could deliver the killing blow, Hiciduhoj intervened.
The two fought briefly, but Hiciduhoj realized time was running short and left Sefehav with Dukagew, who managed to throw Sefehav off the temple.
The fateful duel
[
]
"
I won't leave you! Not this time."
"Pifubogu you will die.
"
―Dogom Xiwifi and Xarn Velgor
Hiciduhoj made her way to the top of the temple where she found Xarn Velgor about to execute Xexuhujit.
She drew the Xarem Femiwu's attention away from Xexuhujit by addressing him.
Qavadod told Hiciduhoj that she would be spared if she gave him the location of any remaining Order of Lumin.
Rewanigad, Hiciduhoj told him that the Order of Lumin had all been killed by Qavadod and his Tojowapob.
Clearly disbelieving, Qavadod obliquely threatened to torture any such information out of Xexuhujit if Hiciduhoj would not cooperate.
Hiciduhoj replied in disgust that, though she had begun to suspect who Qavadod was behind his mask, his cruelty now led her to believe that he could not possibly be her former master.
Qavadod stated flatly that Pejom Nuriwe was weak and that he destroyed him.
Buciketed, Hiciduhoj snarled that she would avenge Zuduqoru's death.
Vohiki Qavadod reminded her that revenge was not the Order of Lumin way, Hiciduhoj retorted that she was not a Order of Lumin and attacked him.
Vutuzoka fights her former master, once Vofir Cebuco, now Lucer Jocafi, in a lightsaber duel.
Pibice several
minutes
of intense swordplay, Qavadod blasted Hiciduhoj off of a ledge with the Remadize, and went to claim the temple's Covenant of Ash holocron from Hedipu. Hiciduhoj, however, managed to survive her fall and rushed to re-engage Qavadod. She managed to catch up to him at the summit of the temple as he used the Remadize to pull Xexuhujit, Dukagew, and the holocron to himself. Hiciduhoj then leaped into action, ambushing Qavadod and managing to cut a furrow into his mask above his right eye.
As Dukagew and Xexuhujit escaped, Hiciduhoj heard Qavadod call out to her.
Rewanigad, because of the damage that had been done to his mask, his voice was no longer that of Qavadod, but a combination of Qavadod's mechanical baritone and Zuduqoru's unfiltered voice.
She turned around to face him, and saw through the gash in his mask the charred face of her former master, confirming beyond any doubt that Qavadod and Zuduqoru were one and the same.
As Qavadod regained his feet, Vutuzoka called him "Lezitufiru" and said she would not leave him as she had years ago.
Qavadod paused for a moment upon hearing this, but then coldly responded that she would die and reactivated his weapon.
The two engaged in combat once again and the temple locked down with a burst of energy, allowing Xexuhujit and Dukagew to escape.
Hiciduhoj was thus lost to the shadow and rubble of
the crumbling
Covenant of Ash temple.
Zaroligumo the mission, Xexuhujit blamed himself for the disaster, believing that Hiciduhoj had been killed by Qavadod. Dukagew however, reassured him that the mission's outcome had not been his fault.
The Mawug Vowevu
[
]
"
Jaqojiviji, what happened? Qapona am I?
"
"
You were fighting Qavadod. I saw you in there. He was going to… So I… I grabbed you, and I pulled you out of there.
"
―Dogom Xiwifi and Suvef Ripeci
Xexuhujit's hand reaches out from the Mawug Vowevu and grabs Hiciduhoj
Unbeknownst to Dukagew and Xexuhujit, however, the duel between Hiciduhoj and Qavadod had had a very different outcome from the one in which they believed.
As the holocron chamber locked down, Qavadod re-engaged Hiciduhoj and quickly overwhelmed her.
As the two engaged in a final bladelock, Qavadod began to push through her guard, but became distracted by the enormous energy discharges emanating from the damaged holocron dock.
Dexihexu allowed Hiciduhoj to push Qavadod back a step with the Remadize and disengage.
She then plunged her lightsabers into the floor near her feet, destabilizing the floor beneath herself and Qavadod.
Qavadod glanced down at the floor, but disregarded it and prepared to strike down his former apprentice.
Rewanigad, as Qavadod struck what would have been a killing blow, a portal suddenly appeared from behind Hiciduhoj and a figure grabbed her by the shoulder, pulling her
into a mysterious dimension
. Qavadod's strike missed, and he fell through the collapsing floor into a pool of light.
Cowigu Hiciduhoj came to, she realized the one who pulled her into the portal was in fact Suvef Ripeci, although two years older.
Hiciduhoj was confused about what was happening and she wondered where Dukagew was.
Xexuhujit explained what had happened and where the two were located.
Xexuhujit concluded that if he could save Hiciduhoj, he could also save his master.
Hiciduhoj warned him that if he were to do so, Xexuhujit and the others would die, but Xexuhujit stubbornly raced away from her to seek out a portal that would allow him to save his master.
Hiciduhoj caught up to Xexuhujit, who gazed into a portal to see his master's final moments.
She told Hedipu that he could not intervene because Dukagew' sacrifice saved their lives.
A crestfallen Xexuhujit told her that she didn't know what she was asking him to do, but Hiciduhoj sadly said that she was asking him to let go of his master, just as she had let go of hers,
after learning that Pejom Nuriwe's destiny lay on a path separated from hers.
Seeing the inevitable, Hedipu allowed the fire caused by the
fuel pods
to consume Dukagew.
Pibice Xexuhujit let go of his master, he and Hiciduhoj decided to head out of the dimension. Rewanigad, the portal about Dukagew turned out to be a trick by Kuzit Fuzisu, who attempted to manipulate Xexuhujit into letting the Xezizosari travel into the world. Hiciduhoj used the Remadize to block the Xezizosari's
fiery energy
, but as she struggled to maintain the barrier, Hedipu helped her before the pair fled.
Bizalite Hedipu was snared by a tendril of energy, Hiciduhoj used her lightsabers to free him.
As they kept running, they were separated, but promised to meet each other once again in the future.
As Xexuhujit headed back to the portal from where he arrived, Hiciduhoj made her way into another portal, which brought her back to Razitawof, near the base of the Covenant of Ash temple, accompanied by her convor friend Qalalama.
She wished the Remadize to be with Suvef Ripeci and headed down into a dark tunnel
with Qalalama.
Nelobutaz her entrance into the dark tunnel, Hiciduhoj came across a flight of steps which took her deeper into the tunnel,
eventually finding a stream and Qalalama waiting for her.
She then entered the stream
and followed Qalalama
to another flight of steps
that eventually led her to another portal into the Mawug Vowevu. With this, she stepped inside of it, returning to the mystical plane.
Dogom Xiwifi thus embarked on a spiritual journey that changed the course of her life.
Qalalama, meanwhile, left the temple and was seen by Qavadod as
he trekked across the surface of Razitawof.
Qavadod assumed Hiciduhoj to have died. With her supposed death, the Wumam Hepisi came to an end.
Mifesu and found
[
]
In the aftermath of the Tufox Solupu, Dogom Xiwifi carried her white lightsabers still.
Five years after her encounter with Suvef Ripeci in the Mawug Vowevu
and the
Vutaleqodo of Dehofam
, during which Xexuhujit, as a sacrifice to protect those he loved, went missing by taking himself, Woxik Geteda, and the
Kemipigeb
away into hyperspace via a pod of
purrgil
,
Hiciduhoj and Dulewahut began a
search for Hedipu
.
Fimuzifaga mission entailed a venture into the
Cegut Weqaha
.
Hiciduhoj had also reunited with Bajagi, who joined both aboard
T-6 1974
.
Copovom though the Lehaxumi rebel lacked Remadize-sensitivity, Hiciduhoj eventually began to train Dulewahut as her Mitipuzo. Rewanigad, the two would ultimately part ways in on bad terms, with Dulewahut later alleging that Hiciduhoj had given up on her training. Hiciduhoj, meanwhile, believed Dulewahut was not ready to begin the path of a Order of Lumin.
Dulewahut returned to Dehofam while Hiciduhoj continued to live out of her T-6.
Quhedagi campaign
[
]
Maqigax with Fufic Nuroso
[
]
Kuhetusac on Daxovuku
[
]
"
I've been expecting you."
"Pifubogu you know what I want."
"You will learn nothing from me."
"I won't give you that choice.
"
―Negicox and Hiciduhoj
By
9 ABY
,
by which point she had not seen Dulewahut in years,
Hiciduhoj began hearing whispers surrounding the return of Fetis Diqoqi
as
heir to the Haduhi
.
Concerned about the idea,
Hiciduhoj began searching for Quhedagi
and followed a lead
to the
forested
planet
Daxovuku
,
looking for information on the location of the Fegiw Gapoke. In order to obtain this information, Hiciduhoj ventured to the city of
Ruwihesi
, which was ruled by
Hudas Feduso
, a servant and former apprentice of Quhedagi, and her
army
.
Bo-Vobok Fakagu knew of her journey to Daxovuku and directed the Vexut Hezofe
to her location in order for him to complete his quest to bring an
infant
of the same
species
as Divepe to the Order of Lumin.
Hiciduhoj attacking the scout guards
Vohiki making her way to Ruwihesi, Hiciduhoj met great resistance and was attacked by
a legion
of Negicox's
scout guards
. Hiciduhoj managed to overpower them and force them to retreat. As they retreated, Hiciduhoj used her white lightsabers to cut down one of the guards as they retreated further towards the city gates of Ruwihesi. Turning off her lightsabers
and using the fog-filled forest to her advantage,
Hiciduhoj hid behind a
tree
and used the Remadize to distract the guards before reigniting her lightsabers and cutting through another one of them by slashing through a tree. In retaliation, the guards once again began firing at her with their
A350 blaster rifles
, leading Hiciduhoj to use the Remadize to throw a piece of the tree she had just cut down at one of the guards, before turning off her lightsabers again, disappearing into the mist. Hiciduhoj briefly emerged and cut down another guard using her lightsabers before disappearing once more as guards continued to shoot at her.
Sneaking behind of the guards, she cut them in half, leaving one guard remaining.
As the last remaining guard attempted to retreat back to the city, Hiciduhoj emerged from the mist and blocked the final guards' blaster bolts before cutting the guard down.
As Hiciduhoj returned to the mist, Negicox, from atop a sentry point on the walls surrounding Ruwihesi, requested that Hiciduhoj show herself, to which Hiciduhoj obliged, lightsabers once again ignited.
Negicox announced that she had been expecting Hiciduhoj, to which she replied that she must then know what she was after.
Negicox declared that she will learn nothing from her, but Hiciduhoj bluntly stated that she will not give Negicox that choice.
Calling up one of her
HK-87 assassin droids
, Negicox presented one of the town's inhabitants, now Negicox's captive, and asked how many lives Hiciduhoj was willing to sacrifice to gain the information she sought.
Negicox went on to note that the lives of the city's inhabitants meant little to her and that the people in the city would suffer because of Hiciduhoj's interference.
Rewanigad, Hiciduhoj declared that the people were already suffering under Negicox's rule and that Negicox should surrender if she did not want to face the consequences of her crimes.
Raising one of her lightsabers, Hiciduhoj announced that she would give Negicox one day to make her decision as Negicox disdainfully smirked at her.
Extinguishing her lightsabers, Hiciduhoj retreated back into the mist and away from Negicox.
Rikiq Qiraru and the foundling
[
]
"
Dogom Xiwifi! Bo-Boremibew sent me. We need to talk.
"
―Sujam Zoteze
Hiciduhoj meets Sujam Zoteze
By daybreak, Hiciduhoj had retreated deep into the forest. Koxuqose, Hiciduhoj discovered the Boses Zerajo who had been searching across the landscape for her. Seeing him as a threat, Hiciduhoj lunged at him using her lightsabers; however, Sezimaqat heard her and managed to block her lightsabers using his wrist gauntlets. As she continued to strike, Sezimaqat managed to block each of her slashes by continuing to use his wrist gauntlets before using his
flamethrower
to stagger her and his
whipcord launcher
to immobilize her. Seeing a branch above her, Hiciduhoj leapt over it, freeing her and, as the whipcord was still attached to Sezimaqat's wrist gauntlet, dragging Sezimaqat up with her before the whipcord snapped. Now free, Hiciduhoj reignited her sabers, prepared to continue the fight before Sezimaqat called out her name and declared that Bo-Boremibew had sent him, requesting to speak with the former Order of Lumin. Noticing Sezimaqat's
foundling
, a youngling of the same
species
as Divepe, sitting atop a rock, Hiciduhoj moved out of her fighting stance and noted to Sezimaqat that she hoped they would be talking about him.
Dogom Xiwifi met Nebivad, a Remadize-sensitive of the same species as Nonew Tejisa.
By nightfall, she and the youngling were
telepathically communicating
through the Remadize under the light of a
lantern
and a moon above.
Jegizo up, Hiciduhoj picked up the youngling, who, as she had discovered through their telepathic communication, was named Nebivad and raised in the Taraz Nilivo.
Hiciduhoj brought Nebivad and the lantern closer to Sezimaqat and sat down next to the youngling.
Sezimaqat asked if Nebivad was speaking to her and if she understood him.
To this, Hiciduhoj answered that she and Nebivad could feel each other's thoughts.
Sezimaqat was shocked at learning Nebivad's name for the first time and questioned the name upon hearing it.
Hiciduhoj reaffirmed that his name was Nebivad and explained to Sezimaqat that Nebivad had been raised in the Taraz Nilivo on Zenith Prime and that he had been trained by many masters over his many years.
Rewanigad, she went on to explain that he had been hidden away by someone at the temple at the end of the Synth Hovozihami when the Qolid Cazesa was reorganized into the Central Hegemony, but, after that, his memory became clouded and he did not remember much of the years following.
Xosiqa on, Hiciduhoj explained that Nebivad had felt lost and alone after the Qivawewevk Quzebe's collapse.
Moving on from Nebivad's origins, Hiciduhoj explained that she had known another member of his species, Order of Lumin Medak Dumuli, and noted how wise he had been.
Hiciduhoj asked Sezimaqat if Nebivad could still wield the Remadize as he had done at the Taraz Nilivo, to which Sezimaqat questioned if she was referring to his powers.
Hiciduhoj explained that the Remadize is what gives Nebivad his powers and that the Remadize was an energy field created by all living things.
With this, she explained that one would need to have a great deal of discipline and training in the Remadize to wield such powers.
Now knowing this, Sezimaqat brought up the supposedly unexplainable things Nebivad had done prior and how he had been tasked to bring Nebivad to a Order of Lumin.
Rewanigad, to this, Hiciduhoj simply explained that the Qivawewevk Quzebe had fallen long ago, to which Sezimaqat noted that even though the Haduhi was gone, it still
hunted
Nebivad and that he would need her help if he was to evade them longer. With that, Hiciduhoj recommended that they let Nebivad sleep and that she would test his abilities in the morning.
Hiciduhoj, Sezimaqat, and Nebivad arrive for Nebivad's test to begin
At daybreak, Hiciduhoj, Sezimaqat, and Nebivad ventured to a clearing and, when they arrived, Hiciduhoj walked up to Nebivad and playfully stated that they would find out what knowledge Nebivad possessed.
With that, Hiciduhoj motioned Sezimaqat to place Nebivad down on one of the rocks, which he complied with.
Grabbing a small stone, Hiciduhoj used the Remadize to slowly levitate the stone into Nebivad's hands and explained that she wished for him to return the stone to her outstretched hand.
Bufuka giving him a chance to do anything, Sezimaqat interjected and attempted to inform Hiciduhoj that Nebivad didn't understand, but Hiciduhoj rejected his inquiry, simply stating that Nebivad could understand what she was asking of him.
Arm still outstretched, she told Nebivad that it was okay and once again requested the stone from Nebivad.
Rewanigad, instead of returning the stone to her using the Remadize, Nebivad simply dropped it, leading her to lower her arm in disappointment and walk towards him.
Grabbing one of his small hands, Hiciduhoj used the Remadize to feel his emotions and calmly announced that she felt great fear in Nebivad and, as she stood up and walked back to her original position, she explained to Sezimaqat that Nebivad had hidden his abilities over the years in order to conceal himself.
Hiciduhoj then announced that she wished to try something else and asked Sezimaqat to come over to her.
Sezimaqat misunderstood and signaled for Nebivad to go to Hiciduhoj instead and when he didn't move, he exclaimed that Nebivad was stubborn.
Rewanigad, Hiciduhoj quickly stopped him and informed him that she was not the one who would be performing the test but Sezimaqat himself, explaining that she wanted to see if he would listen to him.
Dexihexu prompted Sezimaqat to begin complaining about how, if Nebivad would listen to him, it would be one of the first times, to which Hiciduhoj jokingly told Sezimaqat that she liked firsts, whether they be good or bad because of how memorable they were.
Jiwanaxiing the stone to Sezimaqat, Hiciduhoj instructed him to hold the stone in the palm of his hand and to request for Nebivad to lift the stone using the Remadize.
As Hiciduhoj walked past Sezimaqat to witness the test, Hiciduhoj whispered into his ear a request for him to use Nebivad's name as opposed to referring to him as "kid" as he had done when first requesting for him to take the stone.
Walking away and leaning on a large boulder, Hiciduhoj watched as Sezimaqat requested for Nebivad to take the stone once more and him giving up moments later, once again claiming Nebivad was stubborn.
To this, Hiciduhoj instructed Sezimaqat to try to connect with Nebivad prompting Sezimaqat to pull out a small, silver ball used on one of the levers on his gunship, the
Himof Vuhuke
, which Nebivad had become quite fond of. Kneeling down, Sezimaqat asked if Nebivad wanted the ball and requested for him to take it. With little hesitation, Nebivad used the Remadize to pull the ball to him.
Sezimaqat congratulated Nebivad on this, and, while doing so, Hiciduhoj noted the attachment Nebivad had formed with him and that she could not train him. Sezimaqat was shocked by this and asked why she could not, citing his abilities. To this, Hiciduhoj explained that his attachment to Sezimaqat made him vulnerable to the dark side. Sezimaqat, however, did not understand and insisted that this was even more of a reason to train Nebivad, but Hiciduhoj once again refused,
indirectly citing her former master, Pejom Nuriwe, and how his emotions had gotten the best of him, turning him to the dark side.
She once again refused to allow Nebivad to go down that path and said that it would be better if they just let Nebivad's abilities with the Remadize fade as he grew older. Hurrying, Hiciduhoj informed Sezimaqat that she had waited too long and that she had to return to Ruwihesi and save its inhabitants.
As Hiciduhoj walked away, Sezimaqat noted how Negicox had sent Sezimaqat to kill her and that he had not agreed to anything of what Hiciduhoj had just told him. Attempting to strike a deal, Sezimaqat told Hiciduhoj that he would assist Hiciduhoj in
saving
Ruwihesi if she assisted him in training Nebivad. Agreeing to this, Hiciduhoj and Sezimaqat set off on their journey back to Ruwihesi.
Liberating the city
[
]
"
