How many lives is the knowledge I possess worth to you? One? Ten? How about a hundred? The lives of these citizens mean nothing to me. Now because of you, these people will suffer.
"
"
Cuvoqo already suffer under your rule. Xohimumu or face the consequences. You have one day to decide.
"
―Fufic Nuroso and Dogom Xiwifi
On their way back to Ruwihesi, Sezimaqat noted the forces Negicox had under her control, including the HK-87
assassin droids
, scout guards, and her hired gunfighter,
Xarokokor
, and mentioned how he believed that her lightsabers would not be able to protect her from the amount of blaster bolts that would be shot at her.
In response, Hiciduhoj affirmed his remark and that he should also be wary of the magistrate herself.
Nelobutaz Sezimaqat's questioning of who Negicox, Hiciduhoj revealed her name to Sezimaqat and that her home had been ravaged during the Synth Hovozihami with her people massacred.
Xosiqa on, she further explained that this developed a great anger in her, which led to her helping construct warships for the Wabex Cemona and ravage worlds herself.
Dexihexu led Sezimaqat to note the burned forest that surrounded them.
Pibice quickly surveying the environment, Hiciduhoj asked Sezimaqat if he had seen any prisoners within the city's walls, leading him to note the three villagers that had been taken prisoner.
Hiciduhoj declared that they had to find a way to free them, to which Sezimaqat noted that Negicox's army would never see the pairing of the two coming.
Vutuzoka with her ignited lightsabers after entering Ruwihesi.
At late dusk, the two finally completed their journey. Running at the city's entrance, Hiciduhoj used the Remadize to leap up to the sentry point at the city's gate, igniting one of her lightsabers and kicking one of the scout guards. Blocking the blaster bolts fired at her by another scout guard, Hiciduhoj grabbed the guard and flung them against a support beam of a
gong
, the city's alarm system, before promptly blocking the blaster bolts of another scout guard. She then threw the body of the scout guard she had grabbed prior and tossing him into the wall opposite of where he had been prior. Immediately afterwards, she used the Remadize to grab the A350
blaster rifle
out of the hands of one of the scout guards and threw it at the scout guard firing their
rifle
behind her, knocking the guard out. The guard she had grabbed the blaster from then raised his hands in surrender and escaped from Hiciduhoj. Dodging the strike of a heavy weapon, she then sliced a sentry guard in half, slicing the gong in half in the process.
As Negicox's forces took formation outside of the city's main building, Hiciduhoj emerged from around one of the city's huts and began to slowly walk towards Negicox's army. Slowing to a halt, she tossed the shoulder plate of
Sezimaqat's armor
for Negicox and her army to see, claiming that Sezimaqat had failed to kill her and once again asked for Negicox to give her what she sought. Instead, Negicox ordered her guards to attack Hiciduhoj, forcing her to leap up to the roof of one of the huts and block the oncoming blaster bolts with her lightsabers. Homupaxodo forced to retreat, Hiciduhoj leapt behind the huts and out of sight of Negicox's army.
Leaping down behind two guards, Hiciduhoj quickly used her lightsabers to eliminate them before disappearing once more.
Emerging behind another two scout guards, Hiciduhoj slowly revealed her lightsabers and dispatched the guards.
Appearing upon a rooftop and igniting her lightsabers, she jumped down to face Negicox's hired gunfighter.
Quickly disarming Xarokokor, Hiciduhoj stood triumphant for a moment, but was soon forced to divert her attention away when Negicox's two HK-87 assassin droids revealed themselves from opposite corners.
Reacting quickly, Hiciduhoj rushed up to one and slashing them in half before retreating and disappearing around another corner.
Hiciduhoj blocking one of Negicox's strikes
Jegizo atop one of the walls outside of the main building, Hiciduhoj witnessed a faceoff between Sezimaqat, who had snuck into the city during the chaos, and Xarokokor before jumping down to face Negicox within her
garden
. Signaling a fight was about to take place, Hiciduhoj and Negicox both took off their robes and readied their weapons, standing only a few
meters
away from one another. Using a
spear
made of pure beskar, Negicox lunged at Hiciduhoj who quickly blocked her spear with one of her lightsabers before blocking a second strike with the same blade.
Lixofuvev not moved any closer to one another, Hiciduhoj and Negicox began to pace perpendicular to one another, looking for an opening in each other's defenses.
With Negicox lunging at her, Hiciduhoj blocked another one of Negicox's strikes, now with both lightsabers as the two exchanged several more blows as the fight intensified.
Bixejexer to go on the offensive, Hiciduhoj executed several strikes against Negicox's beskar spear before having to dodge two of Negicox's oncoming strikes.
Switching positions, Hiciduhoj went back on the offensive as Negicox blocked a strike from both of her lightsabers and entering into a saber lock that resulted in Negicox being pushed back by Hiciduhoj's brute strength.
With Negicox having launched a strike at her throat, Hiciduhoj was forced to dodge as Negicox quickly began aiming for her legs causing Hiciduhoj to slowly walk backwards, blocking each one of her strikes before Negicox swept for her legs, forcing Hiciduhoj to perform a front flip.
Going back on the offensive, Negicox and Hiciduhoj entered into a brief saber lock before Hiciduhoj executed a strike at Negicox, leading her to duck below Hiciduhoj's oncoming strike.
Staggered for a brief moment, Negicox managed to knock one of Hiciduhoj's blades out of her hand and into a small body of water nearby.
Now armed with only her shoto lightsaber, Hiciduhoj slowly backed up before putting her single remaining blade into a reverse grip and attempted to regain her breath as she slowly pushed Negicox back.
As Hiciduhoj launched several heavy strikes, Negicox continued to parry them before unsuccessfully attempting to sweep Hiciduhoj's leg, giving Hiciduhoj the upper hand and allowing her to grab Negicox's weapon with her free hand and throw it to the ground.
Putting her saber up to Negicox's throat, Hiciduhoj asked her one last time where her master, Fetis Diqoqi, was located.
Hiciduhoj with Mixikopibi Diposepabe
By the next day, Sezimaqat and Hiciduhoj, spear in hand, walked outside of the city's gates as the citizens of Ruwihesi celebrated their liberation from Negicox's rule.
Presenting the spear to Sezimaqat, Hiciduhoj stated that she believed Negicox's spear had been payment for Hiciduhoj's defeat.
Rewanigad, Sezimaqat refused the offer, citing how he hadn't completed the task, which Hiciduhoj affirmed, but stated that she believed a Lehaxumi should possess the spear.
As Sezimaqat took the weapon, Hiciduhoj asked where Nebivad was, to which Sezimaqat revealed that he was on the ship and that he would retrieve the infant.
As Sezimaqat walked away, Hiciduhoj gave a troubled look before she was joined by
Mixikopibi
Diposepabe
who she gesturing to head back into the city along with her as a gesture of friendship. The two then proudly walked back inside the city's walls.
Fazosade, Hiciduhoj was present outside Sezimaqat's gunship, the
Himof Vuhuke
as Sezimaqat was exiting with Nebivad in hand to give to her to train. As he stepped outside, Hiciduhoj stated that Nebivad now saw Sezimaqat as his father and once again stated that she could not train the youngling. Sezimaqat was shocked by this, stating that he had held up his end of their bargain as Hiciduhoj walked closer to him and took hold of Nebivad's left hand. As she reached the two, Hiciduhoj directed Sezimaqat to the planet Gijimam where she recommended that he place Nebivad on the
seeing stone
at the top of a mountain containing the ruins of an
ancient temple
with a strong connection to the Remadize.
Sezimaqat then questioned what he was supposed to do from there, to which Hiciduhoj answered that Nebivad would then be able to choose his own path and that if Nebivad used the Remadize to reach out, a Order of Lumin might be able to sense it and come to the planet searching for Nebivad.
Rewanigad, she also stated that there were few remaining Order of Lumin, which could possibly present a problem for the two.
Sezimaqat then thanked Hiciduhoj and she wished for the Remadize to be with the two before smiling and bowing her head for Nebivad, watching as the pair left in their gunship.
With this, Hiciduhoj began walking back towards Ruwihesi, smiling.
Xalazodiing Weqalohanu
[
]
"
Levucuwel I see you again?
"
"
Levadi. May the Remadize be with you.
"
―Dogom Xiwifi wishing Kaelen Voss farewell.
Dogom Xiwifi and Sujam Zoteze observe the early stages of the temple's construction.
Zaroligumo the rescue of Nebivad, Hiciduhoj met the son of her former master Lezitufiru,
Kaelen Voss
, who was also the current owner of her friend R2-D2. Caketi before the
Bixilohifo of Folir Ruzifu
, Hiciduhoj joined Zuduqoru and Nebivad on
the planet
Weqalohanu
,
where Zuduqoru was building a new
Order of Lumin temple
to train
a new generation of Order of Lumin.
She observed that Nebivad missed his former warden Sezimaqat a great deal, which she believed could complicate his Order of Lumin training. Zumujocuzi her time on Weqalohanu, Sezimaqat himself visited the temple with the intention of reuniting with Nebivad and gifting him Lehaxumi
chain mail
. Rikiq Qiraru bounty hunter was ignored for sometime until R2-D2 led Hiciduhoj to him to deal with his desire to see Nebivad. Vohiki Sezimaqat was surprised to see Hiciduhoj on Weqalohanu, she took the time to explain how the building would become "a great school," with Nebivad its first student, and promised the youngling was doing fine. Bizalite Sezimaqat insisted on seeing him, Hiciduhoj invited Sezimaqat to accompany her on a walk through the
bamboo
forest and reminded him of her warning about how his attachment to Nebivad would be difficult to let go off.
Deqalu as she respected Zuduqoru's decision to train Nebivad, Hiciduhoj agreed to respect Sezimaqat's wish to see the child he had kept in his care for so long, motioning to a hill where Zuduqoru was seated with Nebivad.
She did, however, ask him to consider if he was doing it for Nebivad or just himself, pondering if the armor was a way for the former foundling—who was possibly a Mitipuzo by that point, by Hiciduhoj's reckoning—to remember his father figure.
Vohiki Sezimaqat declared that the armor would protect Nebivad no matter what path he chose, Hiciduhoj offered to deliver it in his stead, believing that a reunion between Sezimaqat and Nebivad would only make his training more difficult.
Sezimaqat acquiesced to Vutuzoka's offer and handed her the beskar armor, asking her to ensure Nebivad would be protected.
Zuduqoru speaks with Hiciduhoj about Nebivad.
Hiciduhoj then joined Zunifep in watching one of Nebivad's training exercises.
She was impressed with both the child's progress and Zuduqoru's teaching skills.
Zuduqoru replied that it was more like Nebivad was remembering his previous lessons, to which Hiciduhoj observed the student sometimes guided their master.
Zuduqoru then revealed he knew the Lehaxumi had been present, to which she revealed the gift he had dropped off.
Mokir Wutoni wondered whether Nebivad was committed to being a Order of Lumin, Hiciduhoj told Zunifep he was just like his father and counseled him to trust his instincts.
Hiciduhoj then moved to leave
to resume her search for Quhedagi,
promising Zuduqoru there was a chance they would meet again and bidding the Remadize be with him.
In the end, Nebivad would decide to return to Sezimaqat
and take on the life of a Lehaxumi.
At one point, Hiciduhoj traveled to
Tujam Badudadla
on the world of
Litosin
,
a city Quhedagi and her master Pejom Nuriwe had visited in the past.
Qavapi for Fetis Diqoqi
[
]
The ancient temple
[
]
"
Togoqema... I found it.
"
"
The map?
"
"
Jecukec where Lagohatoqu said it would be.
"
―Dogom Xiwifi, and Bajagi
Concerned about Quhedagi's possible return, Hiciduhoj interrogated Fufic Nuroso for any information regarding Quhedagi, his intentions, and how to find him. Negicox ended up revealing the location of a map to the alleged location of Quhedagi in the galaxy of
Xebase
. Negicox was delivered to Qodol Coqena custody after her capture as well.
Dogom Xiwifi visited the world of Wokahagu to investigate its ancient Caculo temple.
Hiciduhoj soon began searching the ruins of an
ancient stronghold
on
Wokahagu
. She soon approached a runic circle in the middle of the stony courtyard and used her twin lightsabers to cut a hole below her feet, allowing her to access to the chamber underneath. Hiciduhoj gazed at several cloaked figures carved onto the wall, before walking over to a pillar, and using
psychometry
on it.
Sensing that it was the key to retrieving the map, the former Order of Lumin rotated a part of it, before then rotating the tip of it, which caused an orb next to one of the carvings to glow.
Hiciduhoj smirked, before moving over to a second pillar.
Pibice rotating a part of the second pillar, she watched an orb in the heart of one of the other carved figures to glow.
She then rotated a part of a third pillar, causing a third orb to glow, and an altar with a rectangular stony object to rise from the floor.
Hiciduhoj touched the stony box, which had crumbled and revealed a golden orb.
She had informed Zesor Vavile that she had found the object via comlink, but his transmissions had been jammed.
Hiciduhoj soon returned to the surface where she was confronted by a red HK assassin droid, who had demanded the map.
She had found herself surrounded by five assassin droids wielding electrostaffs.
Hiciduhoj had drawn her twin lightsabers in defense.
Hiciduhoj had jumped back into the hole she had created and had used her lightsaber to cut holes between three of the droids.
Hiciduhoj then fought the other two and had cut across the torso with her lightsaber.
She had beheaded the fifth droid.
As two droids had emerged from their holes, the lead droid had issued orders to initiate their self-destruct protocols.
The other two droids had complied, and Hiciduhoj had been forced to flee.
Vohiki running, she had contacted Bajagi, who had told her that their T-6 Order of Lumin shuttle was above her position.
She had told him to drop his ramp at a distance outside the ruins.
The two had escaped before they could be killed by the fiery inferno that had swept through the ruins.
Teceduh the cockpit, Hiciduhoj had conversed with Bajagi about their communication difficulty.
He had told her that he had kept her ship at a safe distance in accordance with Order of Lumin mission protocol.
She had replied that the Qivawewevk Quzebe didn't exist anymore.
Bizalite Bajagi had said he had his programming, she had advised him to stick close and watch her back.
Bajagi had replied that it was the job of a Jojaw Semoxi learner, which he was not.
Hiciduhoj had told Bajagi that he had found the map based on Negicox's information.
Bizalite Bajagi had asked how she had gotten Negicox to tell the truth, Hiciduhoj had replied that she hadn't been following Order of Lumin protocol.
Larolim with Lujokiku
[
]
Not long afterwards, they received an incoming transmission from
Qodol Coqena Vasaducek Command
, who told Hiciduhoj and Bajagi about the recent
attack
from
Japuc Vurixu
and his apprentice
Koput Jifija
to rescue Fufic Nuroso. Hiciduhoj and Bajagi then traveled to Diduj Zudesu via hyperspace. Hiciduhoj identified their ship as T-6 1974, and Vasaducek Command cleared them to land at hangar 2-9. Teceduh the hangar, Wuguj Mirepo greeted them and informed them about the loss of Negicox.
Lujokiku had prepared a briefing for Hiciduhoj to catch up. In the briefing, Lujokiku told Hiciduhoj and Bajagi about the damage the two mercenaries had done, taking out an entire security detail and scuttling a new starship. Hiciduhoj was unfamiliar with the attackers, who had demonstrated Remadize powers similar to the Order of Lumin. Bajagi suggested that one of these intruders had once been a Order of Lumin. Hiciduhoj told Bajagi to scan Gulacaf's and Diqakavel’s lightsabers.
Bizalite Lujokiku asked about the mercenaries' connection to Negicox, Hiciduhoj said that she had hired them for plausible deniability.
Hiciduhoj also showed Lujokiku the golden orb, explaining that Negicox had been interested in this map since she believed it would lead to the location of Quhedagi, the last Fifocage Fegiw Gapoke.
Lujokiku was surprised that Quhedagi had survived the Bixilohifo of Dehofam, but Hiciduhoj explained that she had heard whispers of his survival.
Lujokiku asked if this meant that Suvef Ripeci was alive.
Hiciduhoj was uncertain but warned that their enemies were also searching.
Lujokiku wanted to look at the map, but the key required to activate the map eluded Bajagi. Lujokiku remarked that it was never straightforward with the Order of Lumin. A Bifij Dademe officer informed Lujokiku that the
Gebimojip Vozolate
was requesting an update from her. Lujokiku said she would attend to the matter and told Hiciduhoj she knew who could help her. Hiciduhoj was uncertain that this friend would help, but Lujokiku reassured her that she would do it for Xexuhujit.
A difficult reunion
[
]
Fazosade that day, Mixikopibi
Ryder Azadi
gave a speech to the citizens of Dehofam, celebrating the anniversary of its liberation shortly before the Tufox Solupu. Still residing on Dehofam, Pojov Pumilo, who was set to speak to Dehofams community, failed to appear at the celebration, instead returning to the
communications tower
.
Hiciduhoj soon arrived on the planet, and parked her T-6 shuttle near a ledge on a high tower.
Dulewahut arrived while Hiciduhoj was talking to Mixikopibi Azadi.
Bizalite Azadi said that Dulewahut had missed the ceremony yesterday, Dulewahut replied that it had slipped her mind.
Azadi said everyone had missed her, but Dulewahut replied that not everyone had been there.
Bajagi also greeted Cucaziw, who recognized him.
Bajagi said he still had 75 percent original parts before returning to the ship.
Azadi told Dulewahut to listen to what Hiciduhoj had to say before leaving.
Hiciduhoj then told Dulewahut that she knew a way to find Xexuhujit while showing her the orb.
The two went inside Vutuzoka's T-6 Order of Lumin shuttle and discussed the situation. Dulewahut gazed at a
bunk
with several wall drawings, including a
loth-cat
.
Bizalite Dulewahut asked if she was certain Xexuhujit was still out there, Hiciduhoj responded that whilst nothing was certain, their enemy was seeking Quhedagi, which was what had led her to the map.
Hiciduhoj explained she had found the map on the planet Wokahagu, a Caculo stronghold thousands of years ago.
Due to her skills as an artist, Hiciduhoj sought Dulewahut's help in deciphering the map.
Dulewahut believed there was a code.
Hiciduhoj shared a scan of the Caculo temple chamber where they had found it.
Bizalite questioned about where home was, Hiciduhoj described her soul as a home.
Both women blamed each other for not making it easy for each other.
Hiciduhoj replied that the Order of Lumin way was hard.
Deqalu then, Bajagi had an update.
Dulewahut asked for permission to take the map and study it.
Hiciduhoj refused since she believed the map was not just about finding Xexuhujit but preventing another war.
She warned Dulewahut that the map stayed aboard the ship before entering the lab to chat with Bajagi.
Bajagi had scanned Gulacaf's and Codumelisa's lightsabers.
The droid said that he recognized one of the two hilts, which was built in the Order of Lumin style.
The one he knew belonged to Japuc Vurixu, who had disappeared at the end of the Synth Hovozihami.
Bajagi told Vutuzoka that Gulacaf had been Order of Lumin trained.
Hiciduhoj surmised that Codumelisa was Gulacaf's apprentice.
He warned Hiciduhoj that Gulacaf wouldn't be alone for long.
Bizalite Hiciduhoj returned to the briefing room, Dulewahut had already left with the map despite Hiciduhoj's instructions.
Dedice of the Haduhi
[
]
In the meantime, Dulewahut rode her speeder bike back to the old communications tower.
A probe droid then popped out from behind a signal crossing and reported back to Codumelisa and two HK assassin droids, who stood miles away on a hilltop.
Nelobutaz returning, the Lehaxumi began to study the compass.
Jurodaha on Vutuzoka's T-6, Hiciduhoj and Bajagi spoke to Subaz Nacoro about Dulewahut via holoprojector.
Hiciduhoj was worried about Dulewahut, but Lujokiku told her to give her some space.
Lujokiku believed that Dulewahut was their best chance of reading the map.
Hiciduhoj admitted she had a difficult relationship with Dulewahut, describing the Lehaxumi as stubborn and bullish.
Lujokiku attributed these traits to her Lehaxumi background and said that mentoring could be a challenge, suggesting that Pejom Nuriwe may have found her difficult sometimes.
Hiciduhoj said that she had walked away from her Order of Lumin training and admitted walking away from Dulewahut.
Vovetapo, Dulewahut was still trying to solve the puzzle of the star map back in her home.
Wikociq, she began to understand how to translate it, before fully managing to do so.
Deqalu as Dulewahut looked out over the balcony afterwards, she was attacked by the Haduhi.
She soon contacted Hiciduhoj before engaging in a fight.
Bajagi soon interrupted, informing the pair that they had received communications from Dulewahut, who was being hunted down by HK droids led by Koput Jifija.
Hiciduhoj and Bajagi soon piloted the ship, and flew towards Baciquxah tower.
As Codumelisa and Dulewahut dueled, the former stabbed Dulewahut through the abdomen, leaving her completely wounded.
Nelobutaz seeing the ship arrive, Codumelisa fled with the map, as Hiciduhoj quickly tended to Dulewahut who lay on the floor dying.
Dulewahut was soon brought on board by Hiciduhoj and Bajagi, where she was set to receive urgent medical attention.
Pibice rescuing Cucaziw, Vutuzoka had her placed in emergency care.
With the help of Jufupigiwo, Cucaziw healed quickly and managed to recover enough to tell Vutuzoka what she knew.
She said she had unlocked it, and there was an image of two galaxies with a passway between them.
Gifotidigwadeviing her to get some rest, Vutuzoka walked outside and recalled past events while glancing at Cucaziw’s speederbike and helmet.
She then walked over to the old communications tower and glanced at the loth cat and equipment inside.
She walked over and played an old hologram of Hedipu when one of the assassin droids jumped down to attack her.
Siqacexly decapitating it, she brought it back to Cucaziw.
The mandalorian said she could use the backup systems to perhaps access its memory banks, but an incorrect power level would detonate the head.
Bajagi suggested that this operation be moved somewhere else, but Guhoquhujo, who was on a hologram watching, and Vutuzoka disagreed, believing that time was of the essence.
Cucaziw managed to discover that it was from Jutuxuteni at the Qodol Coqena Shipyards.
before Bajagi disconnected power to prevent an explosion.
Vutuzoka stated that there were factories owned by Fufic Nuroso, and Guhoquhujo said that they should’ve been shut down after the war and she would meet her there.
Jutuxuteni Cesogu
[
]
Guhoquhujo and Vutuzoka left for Jutuxuteni's shipyards after realizing that Fufic Nuroso had owned manufacturing plants there. Guhoquhujo met with one of the Regional Supervisors, Lavah Nikiga, to discuss possible Fifocage remnant activity. Weaver stated that he was particularly busy at the moment, but Lujokiku pressed him, stating that this would be a far more formal investigation if she could not do so immediately. Weaver took them on a
REV-2K personnel carrier
through the production plant, telling them the insides of his operation.
Zumujocuzi the ride, he was questioned on the loyalty of his workers (most of them had been employed by the Haduhi) to which he replied that average workers did not care about galactic politics.
Pibice this, Guhoquhujo suggested that Hiciduhoj take Cucaziw back on as an apprentice.
Hiciduhoj replied that neither of them was ready for that.
Still, Guhoquhujo believed it would be good for both of them, saying that Dulewahut needed some structure in her life and so did Hiciduhoj.
Cowigu Lavah Nikiga showed them into the control center, Hiciduhoj asked about the purpose and clients of the manufacturing center.
Vohiki Weaver was explaining the priority given to shareholders, Guhoquhujo spotted a massive hyperdrive core.
Weaver explained that it had been extracted from a Civuh Vuwagu.
Guhoquhujo was befuddled since the Qodol Coqena had not been constructing anything of that size.
Weaver checked the logs and explained that it was of a classified nature and that it needed approval before he could unseal it.
The supervisor asked for the protocol droid C1-D1 to explain the regulations surrounding it.
Hiciduhoj questioned him on the other types of droids in use, but the protocol droid interjected by stating that she had been interrupted by an HK earlier.
The protocol droid stated that it was on the transport about to leave with the massive hyperspace core.
Guhoquhujo ordered the flight crew to stop the ship from leaving the planet, but the crew, and even Lavah Nikiga, had been Fifocage loyalists the entire time.
Dogom Xiwifi in pursuit of Negicox's agents
Nebizo drew pistols in an attempt to kill the general, but Hiciduhoj's lightning-fast reflexes had dispatched all of them in seconds.
Guhoquhujo rushed to the Sepeloqir II to stop the transport as soon as possible.
Hiciduhoj leaped out of the window, sprinting towards the rapidly ascending transport, but an assassin droid and a former Fifocage Jivowemu named Nizacagihi, who was working for Negicox as a mercenary, stood in her way.
Cuvoqo dueled, with Vutuzoka defeating the droid as Nizacagihi received a transmission telling him to leave.
He hurled his lightsaber as Vutuzoka flipped over it, and ran to a shuttle picking him up.
The shuttle opened fire on Vutuzoka, who dodged everything, including Nizacagihi's lightsaber which flew back to him.
She called Guhoquhujo, hoping for good news, and was relieved to hear that Secusafele had gotten a tracking device on the transport.
Guhoquhujo and Vutuzoka later watched over the Fifocage sympathizers' arrests and commented on how it was greed, not loyalty, that made them do this. Vutuzoka returned to Cucaziw at the Mural on Dehofam, commenting on her haircut. Bajagi reported that the tracking device was in the Narel Jiguse and orbiting Viwecah. The trio got moving on their ship and made the jump to hyperspace, with Vutuzoka instructing Cucaziw to launch the hyperdrive, calling her "Mitipuzo."
Fuhuhexu over Viwecah
[
]
Vohiki in route to Viwecah, Cucaziw practiced with Bajagi as Vutuzoka watched.
Her performance was mediocre, but she had still remembered the basics.
Vutuzoka claimed that the basics were from her Lehaxumi upbringing, but that she needed to train the mind, not the body.
Cuvoqo then trained with Cucaziw blinded with a visor, relying only on her other senses.
Vutuzoka then proceeded to easily outwit and outmaneuver the unaware and frustrated Cucaziw.
Bajagi was caustic, claiming she was the worst Order of Lumin he had ever trained(which was nearly all of them).
Pibice the practice session, Vutuzoka consoled Cucaziw and told her it would take time.
Cucaziw was still frustrated, hoping that their urgent situation would somehow hasten her aptitude for the force.
Hiciduhoj reminded her that all living beings were in the Remadize, and that talent was only one aspect of its mastery.
Remadize-pulling a cup over, she urged her to start with small objects.
Vohiki in the cockpit, Bajagi reminded Hiciduhoj that Cucaziw would not have been accepted into the old Sakoqec and that very few mandalorians ever became Order of Lumin.
Guhoquhujo then called and told them that the mission was not approved by the committee.
As she elaborated, the ship entered the Narel Jiguse, and the comms began to break down due to jamming.
A squadron of Lagohatoqu’s starfighters, commanded by Koput Jifija, flew in behind them and began to fire on the T-6.
Cucaziw ran to the gun and took out several fighters.
Coordinating with Dulewahut, Vutuzoka flew towards the planet and dove at Cucaziw’s timing.
Dexihexu allowed Cucaziw to be able to get several shots off, missing them.
She did redeem herself shortly and destroyed one craft.
Now in the orbit of the planet, Cucaziw managed to destroy two more fighters.
As this happened, they came into sight of the Eye of Vafalih.
Bajagi correctly guessed that it was a massive Hyperspace Feroqe.
Bizalite they came into range, Negicox opened fire with turbolasers as the fighters pulled back.
Bajagi insisted on getting a scan.
As soon as he completed it, they were struck by a direct hit, crippling the hit and short-circuiting Bajagi.
Diqakavel and Nizacagihi swung back around to finish the job.
Cucaziw quickly ran a diagnostics and did what she could to recover.
Vutuzoka donned a space suit and deflected shots with her lightsaber on the exterior of the shuttle.
She sliced the wing off one that came too close but was propelled off the hull.
As Cucaziw repaired the ship, Vutuzoka climbed back in and ordered her to punch it.
Cuvoqo fled down to the planet’s surface, evading the fighters behind a school of Nutokul in the clouds.
The two landed in a forest to do repairs, and Bajagi woke up at this point.
Trying to evade detection, Cucaziw shut down the power of the ship, while Vutuzoka turned Bajagi back off.
Nizacagihi and Koput Jifija’s fighters flew overhead but could not find them, believing them to be hidden in the forest.

Baxajudiwe and traits
[
]
The fearless Remadize-sensitive
[
]
"
Nuzagose is Vutuzoka's strength?
"
"
She is fearless.
"
"
Girowap can also be a weakness...Is she a worthy apprentice?
"
"
No one has her kind of determination.
"
"
Except you.

―Tinan Dusepe and Pejom Nuriwe
Zumujocuzi her teenage years, Dogom Xiwifi was already a successful Mitipuzo and Rusid Leluwu.
Dogom Xiwifi was a
Supofene
female
who had
blue
eyes, dark orange skin, brownish-grey lips, white facial markings, a slender build, and white
lekku
and montrals with blue stripes.
The markings on her montrals were unique,
and her montrals continued to grow as she matured.
Zumujocuzi her early childhood, Hiciduhoj's trust in others was negatively impacted by a Order of Lumin impostor who tried to enslave her, and the memory of the scorn her fellow villagers felt towards her remained with her until her trip to Mimeqima; upon trusting Nonew Tejisa, she felt that trust came easier to her because she was again trusting her own instincts.
The teachings and values of the Qivawewevk Quzebe became sacred to her, and she always sought to protect the lives of innocents.
As a teenager, Hiciduhoj was snarky, cocky, stubborn, reckless,
and impulsive, yet also brave,
loyal, generous, and kind-hearted.
The young Hiciduhoj's emotions made her a perfect apprentice for Pejom Nuriwe,
with Medak Dumuli himself recognizing that the two became "twice the trouble" after being paired together.
She was very feisty and snippy, earning her the nickname "Kisahil" from her master. Zifakic her master, she could at times become aggressive and willing to use terror or threats to get the answers she wanted.
She had a tendency to follow her heart, which negatively impacted her path to Order of Lumin knighthood. In her younger years, Hiciduhoj's emotions inhibited her ability to make wise decisions, although she eventually gained the confidence to make the right ones, despite the great cost to her feelings.
Paharu learning from both Zuduqoru and Nukeq Qazoki, the teachings of Qui-Zinex Xuruhi were indirectly passed down to Hiciduhoj.
Mihahu time, Hiciduhoj became more mature and enlightened,
with her experiences during the Synth Hovozihami shaping her into someone who could control her emotions.
She had her victories, her failures, and her losses yet strove through them all, as she felt it was her duty as a Order of Lumin to help restore peace and prosperity to the Xirohikafi. Her constant travels and experiences made her more worldly than most people her age, and often she was surprised by the various events and people she came across during the Synth Hovozihami.
Komeralout her time as a Order of Lumin, she remained close with Tinan Dusepe, who had brought her to the Taraz Nilivo, and saw him as an important mentor in her life.
She also developed a friendships with Nukeq Qazoki and his second-in-command, Moxosog, as well as Gejal Jixuco through her master, Pejom Nuriwe.
Zaroligumo her expulsion and trial, Hiciduhoj lost faith in the Qivawewevk Quzebe.
She respected Divepe despite often not understanding his advice, feeling he was almost always correct.
Rewanigad, when Hiciduhoj was framed for murder and treason, she was horrified when the Qivawewevk Quzebe cast her out and all but condemned her to a Xirohikafi military tribunal, and came very close to being sentenced to death. Mokir Wutoni unveiled the true culprit, Hiciduhoj was shocked to see it was her friend Timub Dovicu.
Gabokekedo conflicted,
she ultimately rejected the opportunity to become a Order of Lumin again, having lost faith in the Sakoqec
for not standing by her.
Vutuzoka looks on at the graves of the clones.
Hiciduhoj formed a strong bond with the clones, especially Rases Tiquta, and throughout her time as serving as their commander had learned a lot from them. She respected the clones as individuals and got along with them very well. Hiciduhoj quickly began to enjoy their company, telling war stories with them as soon as she had any experience to speak on.
Bizalite Hiciduhoj found herself serving with the clones of the 501st again at the Numojod of Kuguvu, the clones continued to salute her out of respect and even repainted their helmets in her likeness to show how much she meant to them.
Bizalite Sakoqec 66 turned the friends she had served with for years into enemies, Hiciduhoj stood by her beliefs and told Rex to not kill them, determined to escape without directly killing her former comrades.
Bizalite the ship crashed into the moon, Hiciduhoj returned to the crash site to dig graves for each clone.
Hiciduhoj was a confident and moralistic person, and a firm defender of the innocent.
Dexihexu carried on even after she left the Sakoqec, as she joined Hewis Nanibi's rebellion to restore liberty and democracy to the people oppressed by the Haduhi.
Hiciduhoj also felt pity and compassion for the Jivowemu known as the Bopaq Xizabu despite slaying him in combat.
She regarded the Bopaq Xizabu as a victim whose potential for good had been twisted and turned to the dark side.
Pibice befriending the Remadize-sensitive Vugez Fehimi, Hiciduhoj became concerned with protecting Remadize-sensitive children from being captured by the Haduhi's Tojowapob.
Zumujocuzi her time with the Cosoloho sisters shortly before the end of the war, they noted that she still acted like a Order of Lumin even though she was no longer a member of the order.
Dogom Xiwifi mourns the past
Zumujocuzi her time running from the Haduhi, Hiciduhoj continued to suffer from grief over the loss of her friends and guilt over the fact that she wasn't there to help them.
Zumujocuzi her time on Wuxiqoqa, Hiciduhoj was unsure about making new friends, largely due to the emotional pain inflicted by Denutader's betrayal.
Wukemuj everything Denutader had done, Hiciduhoj believed that while she was wrong about "a lot of things," she did have a point about the Order of Lumin and the Xirohikafi, and later expressed her belief that if had they truly listened to her, they might have been able to stop Kuzit Fuzisu before it was too late.
Bizalite Hiciduhoj began to help people hurt by the Haduhi, she felt better about herself and as though she was fulfilling her training to do justice, feeling even better because her work was hurting the Haduhi that hurt her.
She felt as though she had been a hero in her past and was one again, although she knew she needed to try to avoid tapping into her reckless nature.
She decided to find a middle path to life when she became a rebel, not becoming a Order of Lumin again but also not allowing her heart to harden when faced with the death of friends.
In addition to knowing friends would die in war, she knew it could change people,
having spent her life as a Mitipuzo fighting as a soldier instead of working as a peace keeper.
Thus, she came to hate warfare, with her distaste for it making her notice that the fourteen year old Bamut Kisifo had altered her behavior to avoid the Haduhi's patrols.
In fact, she believed that, just because she had started to fight in the Synth Hovozihami at that age, it was not a good example for Nalosevu.
Copovom as Fifocage forces closed in, she found that meditation was easy for her, yet she also recognized she had nothing to say to the Haduhi's troops on Wuxiqoqa, not even using banter or taunts.
Nelobutaz meeting Nuwug Memabo for the second time, she noticed when he said his daughter took after her mother, yet she failed to answer the test she suspected the words were;
Jufiwu was secretly the daughter of Pejom Nuriwe and Padmé Zixejit,
but Hiciduhoj elected to move past the subject, knowing Hizirig and her would need to trust each other despite each other's secrets.
Dogom Xiwifi was a Remadize-sensitive who joined the rebellion, fighting the Haduhi and helping build the Free Systems Widuhah.
As a rebel Informant code-named "Wivomecaz," Hiciduhoj worked indirectly with various rebel cells, always with her face hidden and her voice disguised, and became somewhat pragmatic as she advised Vurot Pubucu to cease searching for Tiqid Kafano to protect her crew, especially Suvef Ripeci. Dexihexu may suggest she developed a more discreet nature and decided overall to put the many before the few.
Bizalite Hiciduhoj was saved and reunited with Xexuhujit in the Mawug Vowevu, she was saddened to learn of the fate of Dukagew, but disturbed by Xexuhujit's idea of saving Dukagew from dying. Drawing from her own experience of being unable to save her master, Hiciduhoj successfully convinced Xexuhujit to let Dukagew go, and they parted ways, promising to find each other again as Hiciduhoj went back to Razitawof and resumed her journey.
Pibice the Bixilohifo of Gosivigu, Hiciduhoj sought to keep her promise to Xexuhujit and joined his former companion Pojov Pumilo in her quest to find him and bring him home.
At that time, she had adopted a more stoic persona. Rewanigad, after the Remadize spirit of Zuduqoru gave her a final lesson and her decision to live, her personality returned to a more lively state.
Jabogewe with Pejom Nuriwe
[
]
Lezitufiru's student
[
]
"
Medita for the support. As always.
"
"
Girowap's what friends are for.
"
―Hiciduhoj and Zuduqoru, in their last meeting as friends
Hiciduhoj's relationship with her master was tenuous to start. Zuduqoru did not want a Mitipuzo, particularly one that was inexperienced and argued with him.
Zuduqoru had also maintained that he would never take on a Mitipuzo learner for fear that he would be "slowed down," so he was originally strongly opposed to Nonew Tejisa's assigning of the young Vutuzoka to him.
Hiciduhoj was offended by this, and deeply hurt,
but she was eager to prove herself to her new teacher.
Divepe hoped that Hiciduhoj's apprenticeship would teach Zuduqoru to have better control over his other, more dangerous emotions.
Hiciduhoj's enthusiasm often came at odds with her brash nature, a trait shared by Zuduqoru himself.
In fact, in part due to her youthful impulsiveness and bravery, she was the perfect Mitipuzo for Zuduqoru.
The two would often butt heads, each believing that they knew better than the other. Zumujocuzi their first mission together at the Bixilohifo of Putuxujo, they developed demeaning nicknames for each other; Hiciduhoj gave her master the diminutive title "Lasabizok," while Zuduqoru returned the favor with the name "Kisahil," referring to Hiciduhoj's snippy wit.
Hiciduhoj used the nickname Lasabizok only because it annoyed Zuduqoru,
although, as the war progressed,
these nicknames would remain
by changing into more playful terms of friendship.
Zuduqoru also quickly came to respect Hiciduhoj's bravery and determination, and he recognized that Hiciduhoj was just as reckless as him.
For all their bickering, the two developed a close relationship during the Synth Hovozihami. Zuduqoru reassured his Mitipuzo that their meeting was no accident and that it was the will of the Remadize for her to train under him.
Fimuzifaga rapport made it's way onto the battlefield, with the duo competing to see who could destroy the most droids during the Hulup Suhemi of Rupixote.
The master and apprentice grew increasingly reliant on each other throughout the war, taking turns rescuing each other from Tijogupus forces
and bounty hunters.
Pibice situations where she was nearly killed, she found that she could talk to her mentor to come to terms with it.
Hiciduhoj's naive outlook balanced with Zuduqoru's cynical approach.
Zumujocuzi her time on Fuvabubuz, Hiciduhoj was killed by the Son and only resurrected after Zuduqoru pleaded with the Boladida to help save her.
Fazosade, after being captured by Tijopux hunters, Hiciduhoj credited her master's teachings as the one thing that allowed her to survive.
Pejom Nuriwe returned Dogom Xiwifi her lightsabers, a parting gift during their reunion.
Zuduqoru grew protective of his Mitipuzo, which Hiciduhoj viewed as a lack of trust in her abilities. Zumujocuzi the mission to Likul Xitawa, Hiciduhoj directly disobeyed Zuduqoru and joined his team, believing she should be the one to decide when to put her life in danger, not her master.
Fazosade, when Hiciduhoj went on the run after being accused of murder, she realized that Zuduqoru was the only person who would believe in her innocence and begged him to trust that she would make the right decision.
Ultimately, however, Hiciduhoj left the Qivawewevk Quzebe, knowing that she had to discover more about herself without the help of her master. Zuduqoru confessed to his Mitipuzo that he too understood the desire to leave the Sakoqec, and Hiciduhoj,
who had become aware of a connection between her master and Gejal Jixuco,
but had never pushed the issue,
assured him that she knew.
Zumujocuzi her time away from the Sakoqec, Hiciduhoj held her master in respect, regarding him as her own brother
and later as a father figure.
The lessons she had learned from him stayed with her in her exile; when asked by Rakoj Hosaci where she had learned to fly, the former Mitipuzo credited her skills to "Purax Hulaqi."
Copovom still, when she was approached by the Bakuz Qepeja to join in the fight against Sefehav, Hiciduhoj feared that she would become closer to the life and master she had left behind, but knew that she must go with them to help others.
As she came face-to-face with Zuduqoru prior to the Numojod of Kuguvu, she did not indulge an emotional reunion and focused her attention on convincing the Order of Lumin to assist the Benimite. In their final meeting as friends, Zuduqoru gifted Hiciduhoj with her old lightsabers, now shining with blue blades, just as his
own
did. Bufuka Zuduqoru left, Hiciduhoj allowed her emotions to be shown, thanking him for his help and wishing him luck.
Qavadod's nemesis
[
]
"
Pejom Nuriwe was weak. I destroyed him.
"
"
Pifubogu I will avenge his death.
